    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncItem@8053619(RecRef_P@8053602 : RecordRef;xRecRef_P@8053601 : RecordRef;Action_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recVariant_L@8053603 : Record 5401;
      FieldRef_L@8053604 : FieldRef;
      recItem_L@8053605 : Record 27;
      CodeItem_L@8053606 : Code[20];
      xCodeItem_L@8053607 : Code[20];
      recGuid_L@8053608 : Record 8053601;
      recVariantSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053090;
      SyncItem_L@8053001 : Boolean;
      SyncVariant_L@8053002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise l'article pass' en paramStre avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - xRecRef_P : Ancien enregistrement
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Le code des piSces dans le CRM est du type CodeItem ou CodeItem-Variant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
          TextModify: BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recItem_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            SyncItemVariant(recItem_L);
          END;

          TextRename: BEGIN
            //Recherche si la donn'e . 't' renomm'e
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            CodeItem_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            xCodeItem_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            recItem_L.GET(CodeItem_L);
            IF CodeItem_L <> xCodeItem_L THEN
            BEGIN
              //renommage donc traitement
              IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, xCodeItem_L) THEN BEGIN
                recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, CodeItem_L);
              END;
              SyncItemToCrm(recItem_L, recItem_L."No.", recVariant_L, FALSE);

              //on ne recherche pas les GUID des variants 'tant en relation avec l'article renomm' puisqu'ils vont ^tre renomm'
            END;
          END;

          TextDelete: BEGIN
           //gestion de la suppression de l'article
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN
              DeleteEntityFromNav('product', recGuid_L.GUID, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncItemVariant@8053005(recItem_P@8053000 : Record 27);
    VAR
      recVariant_L@8053001 : Record 5401;
      SyncItem_L@8053002 : Boolean;
      SyncVariant_L@8053003 : Boolean;
      recVariantSetup_L@8053004 : Record 8053090;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053005 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053006 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053007 : Record 8053601;
      Count_L@8053008 : Integer;
      recTmp_L@8053009 : TEMPORARY Record 8053610;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise tous les articles filtr's avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recItem_P : Article
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //V'rification de la pr'sence de variant
      IF recItem_P."Generic Product" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        IF SearchVariantSetup(recVariantSetup_L, recItem_P) THEN BEGIN
          CASE recVariantSetup_L."Sales Entry Check" OF
            recVariantSetup_L."Sales Entry Check"::Mandatory: BEGIN
              SyncItem_L := FALSE;
              SyncVariant_L := TRUE;
            END;
            recVariantSetup_L."Sales Entry Check"::"Forbidden ": BEGIN
              SyncItem_L := TRUE;
              SyncVariant_L := FALSE;
            END;
            recVariantSetup_L."Sales Entry Check"::" ": BEGIN
              SyncItem_L := TRUE;
              SyncVariant_L := TRUE;
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          SyncItem_L := TRUE;
          SyncVariant_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        SyncItem_L := TRUE;
        SyncVariant_L := FALSE;
      END;

      // --> 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
      EntityName_L := 'product';
      // <-- 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01

      IF SyncItem_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        //Article sans variant
        SyncItemToCrm(recItem_P, recItem_P."No.", recVariant_L, FALSE);
      // --> 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // --> 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //EntityName_L := 'product';
        //AFTER
        // <-- 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053627, recItem_P."No.");
        // --> 20150307-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
        //AFTER
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20150307-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
        // --> 20150307-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
        END ELSE BEGIN
          SyncItemToCrm(recItem_P, recItem_P."No.", recVariant_L, FALSE);
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053627, recItem_P."No.");
          DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
        END;
        // <-- 20150307-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
      // <-- 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
        // --> 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3003-FHS01
        //Liaison entre l'article Nav et le produit CRM si celle-ci n'existe pas encore
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', '=', recItem_P."No.");
        Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            recTmp_L.INIT;
            recTmp_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'));
            recTmp_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;

        recTmp_L.RESET;
        IF recTmp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, recTmp_L."CRM Guid", recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
          UNTIL recTmp_L.NEXT = 0;
        // <-- 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3003-FHS01
      END;

      IF SyncVariant_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
        recTmp_L.RESET;
        recTmp_L.DELETEALL;
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', '');
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', 'LIKE', recItem_P."No." + GetItemSeparator() + '%');
        Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            recTmp_L.RESET;
            recTmp_L.INIT;
            recTmp_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'));
            recTmp_L.Comment := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'productnumber');
            recTmp_L.Comment := COPYSTR(recTmp_L.Comment, STRPOS(recTmp_L.Comment, GetItemSeparator()) + 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recTmp_L.Comment));
            IF NOT(recVariant_L.GET(recItem_P."No.", recTmp_L.Comment)) THEN
              recTmp_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;

        recTmp_L.RESET;
        IF recTmp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, recTmp_L."CRM Guid", recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
          UNTIL recTmp_L.NEXT = 0;
        // <-- 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
        recVariant_L.RESET;
        recVariant_L.SETRANGE("Item No.", recItem_P."No.");
        IF recVariant_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            //Article avec Variant
            SyncItemToCrm(recItem_P, recItem_P."No." + GetItemSeparator() + recVariant_L.Code, recVariant_L, TRUE);
          UNTIL recVariant_L.NEXT = 0;
      // --> 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // --> 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //EntityName_L := 'product';
        //AFTER
        // <-- 20150629-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1750-FHS01
        recVariant_L.RESET;
        recVariant_L.SETRANGE("Item No.", recItem_P."No.");
        IF recVariant_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053627, recItem_P."No." + GetItemSeparator() + recVariant_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
                DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
            // --> 20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
            END ELSE BEGIN
              SyncItemToCrm(recItem_P, recItem_P."No." + GetItemSeparator() + recVariant_L.Code, recVariant_L, TRUE);
              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053627, recItem_P."No." + GetItemSeparator() + recVariant_L.Code);
              DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
            END;
            // <-- 20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
          UNTIL recVariant_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncVariant@8053621(RecRef_P@8053602 : RecordRef;xRecRef_P@8053601 : RecordRef;Action_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recVariant_L@8053605 : Record 5401;
      FieldRef_L@8053604 : FieldRef;
      recItem_L@8053603 : Record 27;
      CodeItem_L@8053609 : Code[20];
      xCodeItem_L@8053608 : Code[20];
      CodeVariant_L@8053611 : Code[20];
      xCodeVariant_L@8053610 : Code[20];
      recGuid_L@8053606 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise l'article li' au variant pass' en paramStre avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - xRecRef_P : Ancien enregistrement
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Le code des piSces dans le CRM est du type CodeItem ou CodeItem-Variant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
          TextModify: BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recItem_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            //V'rification de la pr'sence de variant
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2);
            recVariant_L.GET(recItem_L."No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            //Article avec Variant
            SyncItemToCrm(recItem_L, recItem_L."No." + GetItemSeparator() + recVariant_L.Code, recVariant_L, TRUE);
          END;

          TextRename: BEGIN
            //Recherche si la donn'e . 't' renomm'e
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            CodeItem_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);

            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            xCodeItem_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            recItem_L.GET(CodeItem_L);

            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2);
            CodeVariant_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);

            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2);
            xCodeVariant_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            IF (CodeItem_L <> xCodeItem_L) OR (CodeItem_L <> xCodeItem_L) THEN
            BEGIN
              //renommage donc traitement
              recVariant_L.GET(recItem_L."No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
              IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, xCodeItem_L + GetItemSeparator() + xCodeVariant_L) THEN BEGIN
                recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, CodeItem_L + GetItemSeparator() + CodeVariant_L);
              END;
              SyncItemToCrm(recItem_L, CodeItem_L + GetItemSeparator() + CodeVariant_L, recVariant_L, TRUE);
            END;
          END;

          TextDelete: BEGIN
           //gestion de la suppression du variant de l'article
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            CodeItem_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);

            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2);
            CodeVariant_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);

            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, CodeItem_L + GetItemSeparator() + CodeVariant_L) THEN
              DeleteEntityFromNav('product', recGuid_L.GUID, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
          END;
        END;
      END;

      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAddress@8053653(recRRAddress_P@8053602 : RecordRef;recRRCustomer_P@8053601 : RecordRef;UseAddress_P@8053600 : Boolean;GuidAddress_P@8053606 : Text[50];TypeAddress_P@8053608 : Integer;CompanyName_P@8053609 : Text[30];Action_P@8053612 : Code[10]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      UserGuid_L@8053607 : Text[54];
      FieldRef_L@8053604 : FieldRef;
      CustomerCode_L@8053610 : Code[20];
      AddressCode_L@8053611 : Code[10];
      PostCode_L@8053613 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise une adresse avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recRRAddress_P : Adresse de livraison
      //    - recCustomer_P : Client
      //    - UseAddress_P : TRUE = utiliser adresse; FALSE = utiliser client
      //    - GuidAddress_P : Le GUID de l'adresse dans le cas d'une mise . jour
      //    - TypeAddress_P : type d'adresse : 0=principale, 1= livraison, 2=facturation, 3=exp'dition
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't' (n'est utilisation qu'en cas de modification)
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID de l'adresse mise . jour. Vide si problSme de synchro
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //il faut avoir initialis' la connexion CRM avant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //initialisation des paramStres et pr'paration de la communication
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_additionaladdress';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(8061180); //Dynamics CRM Id
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', CleanGuidToCRM(FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE)));

      IF UseAddress_P = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(3); //Name
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(5); //Address
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(6); //Address 2
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(92); //County
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));

        //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(91); //Post Code
        IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L) <> '' THEN BEGIN
          PostCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053613, PostCode_L, CompanyName_P);
          IF TxtEntityGuid_L <> '' THEN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
        END;

        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(7); //City
        IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L) <> '' THEN BEGIN
          TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053612, COPYSTR(PostCode_L + GetSeparator() +
            FORMAT(FieldRef_L), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recGuid_G."Code Nav")), CompanyName_P);
          IF TxtEntityGuid_L <> '' THEN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
        END;

        FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(35); //Country/Region Code
        IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L) <> '' THEN BEGIN
          TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053611, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), CompanyName_P);
          IF TxtEntityGuid_L <> '' THEN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(2); //Name
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(5); //Address
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(6); //Address 2
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(92); //County
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3', FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));

        //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(91); //Post Code
        IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE) <> '' THEN BEGIN
          PostCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE);
          TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053613, PostCode_L, CompanyName_P);
          IF TxtEntityGuid_L <> '' THEN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
        END;

        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(7); //City
        IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE) <> '' THEN BEGIN
          TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053612, COPYSTR(PostCode_L + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE),
            1, MAXSTRLEN(recGuid_G."Code Nav")), CompanyName_P);
          IF TxtEntityGuid_L <> '' THEN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
        END;

        FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(35); //Country
        IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE) <> '' THEN BEGIN
          TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053611, FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE), CompanyName_P);
          IF TxtEntityGuid_L <> '' THEN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
        END;
      END;

      CASE TypeAddress_P OF
        0:  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490003'); //adresse principale
        1:  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490000'); //livraison
        2:  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490001'); //facturation
        3:  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490002'); //exp'dition
      END;

      //mise . jour ou cr'ation de l'adresse
      IF GuidAddress_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_P)) THEN
          GuidAddress_P := '';
      END;

      IF GuidAddress_P = '' THEN BEGIN
        IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF TypeAddress_P = 0 THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := recRRCustomer_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
            UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053635, FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE), Result_L, CompanyName_P);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
            CustomerCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            FieldRef_L := recRRAddress_P.FIELD(2); //Code
            AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053636, CustomerCode_L + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
          END;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_P) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := GuidAddress_P;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingPlace@8053666(recUnloadingPlace_P@8053602 : RecordRef;AddressGuid_P@8053600 : Text[50];CompanyName_P@8053603 : Text[30];Action_P@8053610 : Code[10]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      UserGuid_L@8053607 : Text[54];
      TxtGuid_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      FieldRef_L@8053604 : FieldRef;
      CustoNo_L@8053606 : Code[20];
      AddressNo_L@8053608 : Code[10];
      Code_L@8053609 : Code[10];
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053611 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053612 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@8053613 : Record 8053167;
      GuidMethod_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      GuidSize_L@8053615 : Text[50];
      GuidTime_L@8053614 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise un lieu de d'chargement avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recUnloadingPlace_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //    - AddressGuid_P : GUID de l'adresse de d'chargement
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't' (n'est utilisation qu'en cas de modification)
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID du lieu de d'chargement mis . jour. Vide si problSme de synchro
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //il faut avoir initialis' la connexion CRM avant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_locationunloading';

      //synchroniser les entit's li'es :
      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(8); //Code mode d'chargement
      IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L) <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recUnloadingMethod_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        GuidMethod_L := SyncUnloadingMethod(recUnloadingMethod_L, CompanyName_P, Action_P, '', recUnloadingMethod_L);
      END;

      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(7); //Code gabarit v'hicule
      IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L) <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recUnloadingVehSize_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        GuidSize_L := SyncUnloadingVehSize(recUnloadingVehSize_L, CompanyName_P, Action_P, '', recUnloadingVehSize_L);
      END;

      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(6); //Code tranche Horaire
      IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L) <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recUnloadingTime_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
        GuidTime_L := SyncUnloadingTime(recUnloadingTime_L, CompanyName_P, Action_P, '', recUnloadingTime_L);
      END;

      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(3); //Code
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(4); //Description
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdress_id', CleanGuidToCRM(AddressGuid_P));

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmode_id', CleanGuidToCRM(GuidMethod_L));
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_maxsize_id', CleanGuidToCRM(GuidSize_L));
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_schedule_id', CleanGuidToCRM(GuidTime_L));

      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(1); //Client
      CustoNo_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(2); //Adresse
      AddressNo_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
      FieldRef_L := recUnloadingPlace_P.FIELD(3); //Code livraison
      Code_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
      IF AddressNo_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        TxtGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053637, CustoNo_L + GetSeparator() + AddressNo_L + GetSeparator() + Code_L, CompanyName_P);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        TxtGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053637, CustoNo_L, CompanyName_P);
      END;

      //mise . jour ou cr'ation de l'adresse
      IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
          TxtGuid_L := '';
      END;

      IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
        IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN
            UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053637, CustoNo_L + GetSeparator() + AddressNo_L + GetSeparator() + Code_L,
              Result_L, CompanyName_P);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
          Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingMethod@8053684(recUnloadingMethod_P@8053603 : Record 8053168;CompanyName_P@8053601 : Text[30];Action_P@8053600 : Code[10];GuidToDelete_P@8053602 : Text[50];xrecUnloadingMethod_P@8053604 : Record 8053168) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053605 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise un mode de d'chargement avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recUnloadingMethod_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't' (n'est utilisation qu'en cas de modification)
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //    - GuidToDelete_P : Guid de l'enregistrement . supprimer
      //    - xrecUnloadingMethod_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID du mode de d'chargement mis . jour. Vide si problSme de synchro
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //il faut avoir initialis' la connexion CRM avant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
        TextModify: BEGIN
          TxtGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053641, recUnloadingMethod_P.Code, CompanyName_P);

          //mise . jour ou cr'ation
          IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
              TxtGuid_L := '';
            END ELSE BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', ''); //Unloading method
              SetField(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, ''); //permet de v'rifier s'il est toujours actif
              IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                IF (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') <> recUnloadingMethod_P.Code) OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recUnloadingMethod_P.Description) OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype') <> '175490002') OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode) <> '1')
                THEN
                  TxtGuid_L := '';
              END ELSE
                TxtGuid_L := '';
            END;
          END;

          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingMethod_P.Code);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingMethod_P.Description);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490002'); //Unloading method

          IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN
              UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053641, recUnloadingMethod_P.Code, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
              Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
          END;
        END;
        TextDelete: BEGIN
          //suppression de l'enregistrement dans la liste des GUID
          GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053641, GuidToDelete_P, TRUE);
          IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L, 2); //d'sactivation de l'enregistrement dans le CRM
        END;
        TextRename: BEGIN
          //Recherche si la donn'e . 't' renomm'e
          IF recUnloadingMethod_P.Code <> xrecUnloadingMethod_P.Code THEN BEGIN
            //renommage donc traitement
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", xrecUnloadingMethod_P.Code) THEN BEGIN
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", recUnloadingMethod_P.Code);
              TxtGuid_L := recGuid_L.GUID;
              IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
                TxtGuid_L := '';

              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingMethod_P.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingMethod_P.Description);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490002'); //Unloading method

              IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN
                  UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053641, recUnloadingMethod_P.Code, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
                  Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingVehSize@8053686(recUnloadingVehSize_P@8053603 : Record 8053166;CompanyName_P@8053601 : Text[30];Action_P@8053600 : Code[10];GuidToDelete_P@8053602 : Text[50];xrecUnloadingVehSize_P@8053606 : Record 8053166) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053607 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise une taille maximum de v'hicule avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recUnloadingVehSize_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't' (n'est utilisation qu'en cas de modification)
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //    - GuidToDelete_P : Guid de l'enregistrement . supprimer
      //    - xrecUnloadingVehSize_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID de taille maximum de v'hicule mis . jour. Vide si problSme de synchro
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //il faut avoir initialis' la connexion CRM avant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
        TextModify: BEGIN
          TxtGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053642, recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code, CompanyName_P);

          //mise . jour ou cr'ation
          IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
              TxtGuid_L := '';
            END ELSE BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', ''); //vehicle size
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber', ''); //Taille du v'hicule
              SetField(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, ''); //permet de v'rifier s'il est toujours actif
              IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                IF (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') <> recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code) OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recUnloadingVehSize_P.Description) OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype') <> '175490000') OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber') <> FormatIntToCrm(recUnloadingVehSize_P."Size Index")) OR
                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode) <> '1')
                THEN
                  TxtGuid_L := '';
              END ELSE
                TxtGuid_L := '';
            END;
          END;

          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingVehSize_P.Description);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490000'); //vehicle size
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber', FormatIntToCrm(recUnloadingVehSize_P."Size Index")); //Taille du v'hicule

          IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN
              UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053642, recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
              Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
          END;
        END;
        TextDelete: BEGIN
          //suppression de l'enregistrement dans la liste des GUID
          GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053642, GuidToDelete_P, TRUE);
          IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L, 2); //d'sactivation de l'enregistrement dans le CRM
        END;
        TextRename: BEGIN
          //Recherche si la donn'e . 't' renomm'e
          IF recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code <> xrecUnloadingVehSize_P.Code THEN BEGIN
            //renommage donc traitement
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", xrecUnloadingVehSize_P.Code) THEN BEGIN
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code);
              TxtGuid_L := recGuid_L.GUID;
              IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
                TxtGuid_L := '';

              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingVehSize_P.Description);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490002'); //Unloading method
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber', FormatIntToCrm(recUnloadingVehSize_P."Size Index")); //Taille du v'hicule

              IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN
                  UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053642, recUnloadingVehSize_P.Code, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
                  Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingTime@8053687(recUnloadingTime_P@8053603 : Record 8053167;CompanyName_P@8053601 : Text[30];Action_P@8053600 : Code[10];GuidToDelete_P@8053602 : Text[50];xrecUnloadingTime_P@8053606 : Record 8053167) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053607 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise un horaire de d'chargement avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recUnloadingTime_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't' (n'est utilisation qu'en cas de modification)
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //    - GuidToDelete_P : Guid de l'enregistrement . supprimer
      //    - xrecUnloadingTime_P : Lieu de d'chargement . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID de l'horaire de d'chargement mis . jour. Vide si problSme de synchro
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //il faut avoir initialis' la connexion CRM avant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
        TextModify: BEGIN
          TxtGuid_L := GetGuidWithCompany(Text8053643, recUnloadingTime_P.Code, CompanyName_P);

          //mise . jour ou cr'ation
          IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
              TxtGuid_L := '';
            END ELSE BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

                   (GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode) <> '1')
                THEN
                  TxtGuid_L := '';
              END ELSE
                TxtGuid_L := '';
            END;
          END;

          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingTime_P.Code);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingTime_P.Description);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490001'); //Unloading Time

          IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN

              UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053643, recUnloadingTime_P.Code, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
              Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
          END;
        END;
        TextDelete: BEGIN
          //suppression de l'enregistrement dans la liste des GUID
          GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053643, GuidToDelete_P, TRUE);
          IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L, 2); //d'sactivation de l'enregistrement dans le CRM
        END;
        TextRename: BEGIN
          //Recherche si la donn'e . 't' renomm'e
          IF recUnloadingTime_P.Code <> xrecUnloadingTime_P.Code THEN BEGIN
            //renommage donc traitement
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", xrecUnloadingTime_P.Code) THEN BEGIN
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", recUnloadingTime_P.Code);
              TxtGuid_L := recGuid_L.GUID;
              IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
                TxtGuid_L := '';

              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingTime_P.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingTime_P.Description);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490002'); //Unloading method

              IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, Result_L) THEN
                  UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053641, recUnloadingTime_P.Code, Result_L, CompanyName_P);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
                  Result_L := TxtGuid_L;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncOrderHeader@8053638(RecRef_P@8053602 : RecordRef;Action_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      FieldRef_L@8053603 : FieldRef;
      recSalesHeaderTmp_L@8053604 : TEMPORARY Record 36;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      recCustomer_L@8053608 : Record 18;
      recLine_L@8053609 : Record 37;
      Date_L@8053611 : Date;
      UserIdGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053613 : Record 8053601;
      Adduc_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      OrderGuid_L@8053615 : Text[50];
      Total_L@8053616 : Decimal;
      Status_L@8053617 : Boolean;
      StatusCode_L@8053620 : Integer;
      recComment_L@8053621 : Record 44;
      Comment_L@8053622 : Text[250];
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053601 : Record 8053608;
      UserId_L@8053610 : Code[50];
      recContactRelation_L@8053619 : Record 5054;
      Delivery_L@8053623 : Boolean;
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
      TxtCampaCrmGuid_L@8053625 : Text[50];
      TxtLocationCrmGuid_L@8053626 : Text[50];
      OrderLineGuid_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      recDetail_L@8053618 : Record 8053170;
      OrderDetailGuid_L@8053624 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise l'ent^te de commande avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InterfaceIsActive() THEN BEGIN
        CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
          TextModify: BEGIN
            RecRef_P.SETTABLE(recSalesHeaderTmp_L);

            IF (recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type" = recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type"::Order) AND
               (recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> '')
            THEN BEGIN
              //R'cup'ration du Guid
              OrderGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053624, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.");

              //initialisation des variables
              EntityName_L := 'cmc_validorder';
              recCustomer_L.GET(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Sell-to Customer No.");

              //Mise . jour de la donn'e dans le record
              recContactRelation_L.RESET;
              recContactRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "No.", "MC Company Code");
              recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
              recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", recCustomer_L."No.");
              recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", GetCompanyMcCode());
              recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST;
              IF recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
                IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                  EXIT;
                END;
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053626, recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
              IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := recContactRelation_L."Contact No.";
              END;
              // --> 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27126-BSI01
              UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Salesperson Code");
              // <-- 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27126-BSI01
              IF UserId_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                UserIdGuid_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053631, UserId_L));
              END ELSE BEGIN
                UserIdGuid_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053631, USERID));
              END;

              TxtCampaCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053622, FORMAT(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."CMC Campaign No."));
              TxtLocationCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053623, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Location Code");

              //Gestion des commentaires : on agrSge ceux que l'on trouve
              Comment_L := '';
              recComment_L.RESET;
              recComment_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type");
              recComment_L.SETRANGE("No.", recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.");
              recComment_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", 0);
              recComment_L.SETRANGE(Type, recComment_L.Type::Logistic);
              IF recComment_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
                  IF STRLEN(Comment_L) + STRLEN(recComment_L.Comment) <= MAXSTRLEN(Comment_L) THEN
                    Comment_L := Comment_L + recComment_L.Comment
                  ELSE
                    Comment_L := Comment_L + COPYSTR(recComment_L.Comment, 1, MAXSTRLEN(Comment_L) - STRLEN(Comment_L));
                UNTIL (recComment_L.NEXT = 0) OR (MAXSTRLEN(Comment_L) = STRLEN(Comment_L));

              IF recCRMSetup_G."Total Type" = recCRMSetup_G."Total Type"::HT THEN BEGIN
                recSalesHeaderTmp_L.CALCFIELDS(Amount);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                recSalesHeaderTmp_L.CALCFIELDS("Amount Including VAT");
              END;

              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", Action_P, '');
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_account',
                CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), '');
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'owninguser',
                // --> 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //CleanGuidToCRM(UserIdGuid_L), '');
                //AFTER
                UserIdGuid_L, '');
                // <-- 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_ordernumber',
                recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", '');
              IF TxtCampaCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_campaign',
                  CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCampaCrmGuid_L), '');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_campaign',
                  '#NULL#', '');
              END;
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_deliverydate',
                FormatDateToCrm(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Requested Delivery Date"), '');

              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_pricelist',
                CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav)), '');

              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_orderdate',
                FormatDateToCrm(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Order Date"), '');

              IF recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_deliveryaddress',
                  CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053636, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Sell-to Customer No." + GetSeparator() +
                  recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Ship-to Code")), '');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_deliveryaddress',
                  CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053635, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Sell-to Customer No.")), '');
              END;

              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_store_id',
                CleanGuidToCRM(TxtLocationCrmGuid_L), '');

              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_comment', Comment_L, '');

              //commande . int'grer dans Nav - Actif
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', TextStatusCode, '1', '');

              //recherche des lignes de la commande
              Status_L := TRUE;
              Delivery_L := FALSE;

              //synchronisation des lignes de la commande
              recLine_L.RESET;
              recLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type");
              recLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.");
              recLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recLine_L.Type::Item);
              IF recLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
              REPEAT
                SyncOrderLine(recLine_L, TextModify, OrderGuid_L, Status_L, Delivery_L);
              UNTIL recLine_L.NEXT = 0;

              IF recCRMSetup_G."Total Type" = recCRMSetup_G."Total Type"::HT THEN BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_totalamount',
                  FormatDecToCrm(recSalesHeaderTmp_L.Amount), '');
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_totalamount_base',
                  FormatDecToCrm(recSalesHeaderTmp_L.Amount), '');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_totalamount',
                  FormatDecToCrm(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Amount Including VAT"), '');

                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', 'cmc_totalamount_base',
                  FormatDecToCrm(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Amount Including VAT"), '');
              END;

              IF Delivery_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                //Mise . jour du statut
                IF Status_L = TRUE THEN
                  //Prix accept'
                  AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '', TextStatusCode, '2', '')
                ELSE
                  //Prix refus'
                  AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, '',
                    TextStatusCode, '175490000', '');
              END;
            END;

            IF (recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type" = recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order") AND
               (recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> '')
            THEN BEGIN
              //R'cup'ration du Guid
              OrderGuid_L := GetGuid2(Text8053694, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.");
              IF GuidFindedWithoutError(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", OrderGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                //initialisation des variables
                EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgagreement';
                AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::TPGHeader, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", Action_P, '');
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnLineExp_G.Type::TPGHeader, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L,
                  'cmc_erpagreementnumber', recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", '');
                recLine_L.RESET;
                recLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesHeaderTmp_L."Document Type");
                recLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.");
                IF recLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                  REPEAT
                    EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgagreement';
                    OrderLineGuid_L := GetGuid2(Text8053695, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No." + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recLine_L."Line No."));
                    IF GuidFindedWithoutError(recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No." + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recLine_L."Line No."),
                       OrderLineGuid_L)
                    THEN BEGIN
                      AddJournalLineExport(recJrnLineExp_G.Type::TPGHeader, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No.", EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L,
                        'cmc_totaldeliveredquantity', FormatDecToCrm(recLine_L."Quantity Shipped"), '');
                      AddJournalLineExport(recJrnLineExp_G.Type::TPGHeader, recSalesHeaderTmp_L."No." + GetSeparator() +
                        FORMAT(recLine_L."Line No."), EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L, 'cmc_totalquantity',
                        FormatDecToCrm(recLine_L.Quantity), '');

                      EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgagreementline';
                      recDetail_L.RESET;
                      recDetail_L.SETRANGE("Sales/Purchase", recDetail_L."Sales/Purchase"::Sales);
                      recDetail_L.SETRANGE("Line Type", recDetail_L."Line Type"::Both);
                      recDetail_L.SETRANGE("Blanket Order No.", recLine_L."Document No.");
                      recDetail_L.SETRANGE("Blanket Order Line No.", recLine_L."Line No.");
                      IF recDetail_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                        REPEAT
                          OrderDetailGuid_L := GetGuid2(Text8053695, recDetail_L."Blanket Order No." + GetSeparator() +
                            FORMAT(recDetail_L."Blanket Order Line No.") + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recDetail_L."Starting Date",
                            8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'));
                          IF GuidFindedWithoutError(FORMAT(recDetail_L."Starting Date", 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'),
                             OrderDetailGuid_L)
                          THEN BEGIN
                            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnLineExp_G.Type::TPGLine, recDetail_L."Blanket Order No." + GetSeparator() +
                              FORMAT(recDetail_L."Blanket Order Line No.") + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recDetail_L."Starting Date",
                              8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'), EntityName_L, OrderDetailGuid_L, 'cmc_standardprice',
                              FormatDecToCrm(recDetail_L."Price/Cost"), '');
                            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnLineExp_G.Type::TPGLine, recDetail_L."Blanket Order No." + GetSeparator() +
                              FORMAT(recDetail_L."Blanket Order Line No.") + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recDetail_L."Starting Date",
                              8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'), EntityName_L, OrderDetailGuid_L, 'cmc_quantity',
                              FormatDecToCrm(recDetail_L.Quantity), '');
                          END;
                        UNTIL recDetail_L.NEXT = 0;
                      END;
                    END;
                  UNTIL recLine_L.NEXT = 0;
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;

          TextRename: BEGIN
          //On ne peut pas renommer une commande
          END;

          TextDelete: BEGIN
          //la suppression pour archivage ne supprime pas la commande valid'es dans le CRM
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncOrderLine@8053639(recLine_P@8053602 : Record 37;Action_P@8053600 : Code[10];TxtCrmGuid_P@8053601 : Text[50];VAR VarStatus_P@8053610 : Boolean;VAR VarDelivery_P@8053620 : Boolean);
    VAR
      FieldRef_L@8053608 : FieldRef;
      recSalesLine_L@8053607 : Record 37;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      UserIdGuid_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      recItemUnitMeasure_L@8053611 : Record 5404;
      SimpleLineGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      PriceOverridden_L@8053613 : Boolean;
      Price_L@8053614 : Decimal;
      recComment_L@8053616 : Record 44;
      Comment_L@8053617 : Text[250];
      OrderNo_L@8053615 : Code[20];
      LineNo_L@8053618 : Text[50];
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053619 : Record 8053608;
      UserId_L@8053603 : Code[50];
      recItem_L@8053000 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053609 : Record 5401;
      PrixStandard_L@8053621 : Decimal;
      RecRef_L@8053622 : RecordRef;
      recSalesHeader_L@8053001 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise la ligne de commande avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecLine_P : Ligne . synchroniser
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //    - TxtCrmGuid_P : GUID de l'ent^te de la commande
      //    - VarStatus_P : indique si le prix a 't' refus'
      //    - VarDelivery_P : indique si le prix a 't' refus'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cette fonction doit ^tre appel'e directement depuis la synchronisation de l'ent^te car elle ne gSre pas l'initialisation du
      // connecteur. Une seule ligne est synchronis'e . chaque fois mais la synchronisation de l'ent^te va faire en sorte de synchroniser
      // toutes les lignes.
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CASE UPPERCASE(Action_P) OF
        TextModify: BEGIN

          //on ne synchronise pas ce qui a 't' filtr'
          recItem_L.GET(recLine_P."No.");
          IF recLine_P."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            recVariant_L.GET(recLine_P."No.", recLine_P."Variant Code");
          END;

          EntityName_L := 'salesorderdetail';
          SimpleLineGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053630, COPYSTR(recLine_P."Document No." + GetSeparator() +
            FORMAT(recLine_P."Line No."), 1, 54));

          EntityName_L := 'cmc_validorderline';
          OrderNo_L := recLine_P."Document No.";
          LineNo_L := FORMAT(recLine_P."Line No.");

          // --> 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27126-BSI01
          UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recLine_P."Salesperson Code");
          // <-- 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27126-BSI01

          IF recLine_P."Quantity Shipped" > 0 THEN BEGIN
            VarDelivery_P := TRUE;
          END;

          IF recLine_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_product',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053627, recLine_P."No.")), '')
          ELSE
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_product',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053627, recLine_P."No." + GetItemSeparator() +
              recLine_P."Variant Code")), '');
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unit',
            CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053621, recLine_P."Unit of Measure Code")), '');
          IF recLine_P."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_campaign',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053622, recLine_P."CMC Campaign No.")), '');
          END;
          //Recherche du GUID du vendeur
          IF UserId_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            UserIdGuid_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053631, UserId_L));
          END ELSE BEGIN
            UserIdGuid_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053631, USERID));
          END;
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'owninguser',
            CleanGuidToCRM(UserIdGuid_L), '');

          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_erplinenumber', LineNo_L, '');

          IF recLine_P."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            PrixStandard_L := recVariant_L."Reference Sales Price";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            PrixStandard_L := recItem_L."Reference Sales Price";
          END;
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_price',
            FormatDecToCrm(PrixStandard_L), ''); //Prix unitaire net
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_price_base',
            FormatDecToCrm(PrixStandard_L), ''); //Prix unitaire
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_valuedprice',
            FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Net Unit Price"), ''); //Prix unitaire valoris'
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_valuedprice_base',
            FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Net Unit Price"), ''); //Prix unitaire valoris'
          IF recLine_P."Quantity Invoiced" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_validatedprice',
              FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Net Unit Price"), '');
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_validatedprice_base',
              FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Net Unit Price"), '');
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_validatedprice',
              FormatDecToCrm(0), '');
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_validatedprice_base',
              FormatDecToCrm(0), '');
          END;
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_orderedquantity',
            FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P.Quantity), ''); //quantit'
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_quantitydelivered',
            FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Quantity Shipped"), ''); //quantit' livr'e
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_deliverypaidqte',
            FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Quantity Invoiced"), ''); //quantit' factur'e
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_deliveryremainingqte',
            FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Outstanding Quantity"), ''); //quantit' restante
          // --> 20150609-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3051-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //IF recItemUnitMeasure_L.GET(recLine_P."No.", recLine_P."Unit of Measure") THEN BEGIN
          //AFTER
          IF recItemUnitMeasure_L.GET(recLine_P."No.", recLine_P."Unit of Measure Code") THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20150609-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3051-FHS01
            IF recItemUnitMeasure_L."Sales Indiv. Qty" = 0 THEN BEGIN
              IF recLine_P."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_quantityindivisable',
                  FormatDecToCrm(recVariant_L."Indiv. Base Qty"), ''); //quantit' indivisible
              END ELSE BEGIN
                AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_quantityindivisable',
                  FormatDecToCrm(recItem_L."Indiv. Base Qty"), ''); //quantit' indivisible
              END;
            END ELSE
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_quantityindivisable',
                FormatDecToCrm(recItemUnitMeasure_L."Sales Indiv. Qty"), ''); //quantit' indivisible
          END ELSE
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_quantityindivisable',
              FormatDecToCrm(recItem_L."Indiv. Base Qty"), ''); //quantit' indivisible

          IF recCRMSetup_G."Total Type" = recCRMSetup_G."Total Type"::HT THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_totalamount',
              FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P.Amount), ''); //Montant total
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_totalamount_base',
              FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P.Amount), ''); //Montant total
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_totalamount',
              FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Amount Including VAT"), ''); //Montant total TTC
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_totalamount_base',
              FormatDecToCrm(recLine_P."Amount Including VAT"), ''); //Montant total TTC
          END;

          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_deliverydate',
            FormatDateToCrm(recLine_P."Requested Delivery Date"), '');
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_maximumdate',
            FormatDateToCrm(recLine_P."Due Date"), '');
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_store_id',
            CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053623, recLine_P."Location Code")), '');

          //Adresse de livraison ou adresse du client
          IF recLine_P."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_addditionaladdress',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053636, recLine_P."Sell-to Customer No." + GetSeparator() + recLine_P."Ship-to Code")), '');
            //Lieu de d'chargement
            IF (recLine_P."Unloading Place Code" <> '')  AND (recLine_P."Ship-to Code" <> '') THEN
              AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_locationunloading',
                CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053637, recLine_P."Sell-to Customer No." + GetSeparator() +
                recLine_P."Ship-to Code" + GetSeparator() + recLine_P."Unloading Place Code")), '');
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_addditionaladdress',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053635, recLine_P."Sell-to Customer No.")), '');
            //Lieu de d'chargement
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_locationunloading',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053637, recLine_P."Sell-to Customer No.")), '');
          END;

          // Ajout des modalit's de d'chargement
          IF recLine_P."Time Slot Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodality1',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053643, recLine_P."Time Slot Code")), '');
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodality1',
              TextGuidVide, '');
          END;

          IF recLine_P."Vehicle Size Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodality2',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053642, recLine_P."Vehicle Size Code")), '');
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodality2',
              TextGuidVide, '');
          END;

          IF recLine_P."Unloading Method Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodality3',
              CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053641, recLine_P."Unloading Method Code")), '');
          END ELSE BEGIN
            AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodality3',
              TextGuidVide, '');
          END;

          //gestion des commentaires logistique
          recComment_L.RESET;
          recComment_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recLine_P."Document Type");
          recComment_L.SETRANGE("No.", recLine_P."Document No.");
          recComment_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", recLine_P."Line No.");
          recComment_L.SETRANGE(Type, recComment_L.Type::Logistic);
          IF recComment_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF STRLEN(Comment_L) + STRLEN(recComment_L.Comment) <= MAXSTRLEN(Comment_L) THEN
              Comment_L := Comment_L + recComment_L.Comment
            ELSE
              Comment_L := Comment_L + COPYSTR(recComment_L.Comment, 1, MAXSTRLEN(Comment_L) - STRLEN(Comment_L));
          UNTIL (recComment_L.NEXT = 0) OR (MAXSTRLEN(Comment_L) = STRLEN(Comment_L));

          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, 'cmc_comment', Comment_L, '');

          //commande . int'grer dans Nav - Actif
          AddJournalLineExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Order, OrderNo_L, EntityName_L, LineNo_L, TextStatusCode, '1', '');

          IF (recLine_P."Blanket Order No." <> '') AND (recLine_P."Blanket Order Line No." <> 0) THEN BEGIN
            IF recSalesHeader_L.GET(recLine_P."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recLine_P."Blanket Order No.") THEN BEGIN
              RecRef_L.GETTABLE(recSalesHeader_L);
              RecRef_L.SETPOSITION(recSalesHeader_L.GETPOSITION);
              SyncOrderHeader(RecRef_L, Action_P);
            END;
          END;
        END;

        TextRename: BEGIN
        //on ne peut pas renommer une ligne
        END;

        TextDelete: BEGIN
        //Les lignes synchronis'es sont supprim'es . chaque fois qu'il y a une mise . jour quelconque
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncCommonEntity@8053607(ClearConnexion_P@8053601 : Boolean) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      CanRun_L@8053602 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise toutes les entit's communes (une table comme les codes postaux, les villes, ...) avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - ClearConnexion_P : initialise ou non le connecteur
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      IF SetSyncCommonRunning(TRUE) THEN BEGIN

        GetSetup();
        CanRun_L := FALSE;

        IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
        BEGIN
          IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN
            CanRun_L := TRUE;
        END ELSE
            CanRun_L := TRUE;

        OpenWindow();

        IF CanRun_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := SyncEntity('Country');
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(2);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('PostalCode');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(3);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('City');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(4);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('GROUPMEASURE');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(5);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('Measure');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(6);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('Campaign');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(7);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('Location');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(8);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('Price');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(9);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('UnloadingModality');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(10);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('Species');
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(11);
            SyncAllFilteredItem(TRUE);
          END;
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            IF ClearConnexion_P = TRUE THEN
              COMMIT;
            NewStepWindow(12);
            Result_L := SyncEntity('FunnelFilter');
          END;

          CloseWindow();

          //on marque qu'il n'y a plus de synchronisation . faire
          recCRMSetup_G."Date Last Sync Entity" := TODAY;
          recCRMSetup_G."Time Last Sync Entity" := TIME;
          recCRMSetup_G.MODIFY(TRUE);
          ClearConnector();
        END;
        SetSyncCommonRunning(FALSE);
      END;

      ClearConnector();

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncEntity@1000000013(EntityType_P@1000000000 : Code[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recordGUID_L@1000000001 : GUID;
      recordGUIDUpdate_L@1000000007 : GUID;
      recPostalCodeCity_L@1000000003 : Record 225;
      recCountry_L@1000000004 : Record 9;
      recPaymentTerm_L@1000000005 : Record 3;
      recPaymentMethod_L@1000000006 : Record 289;
      recUnitOfMeasure_L@8053607 : Record 204;
      LastData_L@8053600 : Code[20];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      ToProcess_L@8053602 : Boolean;
      DataRead_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      PostCodeId_L@8053605 : Code[20];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Guiduomschedule_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      recCampaign_L@8053609 : Record 8052991;
      recLocation_L@8053610 : Record 14;
      crmRecordCount_L@8053611 : Integer;
      i_L@8053612 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053613 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053614 : Integer;
      ValBufferLast_L@8053615 : Integer;
      PostalCodeIdFinded_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recRespons_L@8053617 : Record 8053161;
      PV_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      PAL_L@8053620 : Text[50];
      PAR_L@8053621 : Text[50];
      PAV_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      PAP_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      recUserSetup_L@8053624 : Record 91;
      UserId_L@8053625 : Text[1024];
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053627 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053626 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@8053618 : Record 8053167;
      Code_L@8053628 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053629 : Boolean;
      PosSlash_L@8053632 : Integer;
      LastCode_L@8053616 : Text[250];
      recFunnel_L@8053633 : Record 8053358;
      recFunnel_Line_L@8053631 : Record 8053359;
      Responsable_L@1000000002 : Text[30];
      recSalesPersonn_L@1000000008 : Record 8053608;
      TxtUserGuid_L@1000000009 : Text[50];
      recMappingSalesp_L@1000000010 : Record 8053608;
      recShipmentMethod_L@8053000 : Record 10;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise l'entit' (une table comme les codes postaux, les villes, ...) avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityType_P : Type d'entit' (Code)
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //RSgle de gestion mis en oeuvre :
      //Si existe dans CRM et pas dans NAV => suppression dans le CRM
      //Si existe dans NAV et pas dans CRM => cr'ation dans le CRM
      //Si existe dans NAV et CRM => met . jour la valeur dans le CRM
      //ToProcess_L : 0=Ne rien faire, 1=ajouter, 2=modifier
      //Si on a supprimer une donn'e . la fois dans NAV et dans le CRM, alors l'enregistrement dans la table de synchro des GUID restera
      //
      //Il faut synchroniser les codes postaux avant de synchroniser les villes
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      CASE UPPERCASE(EntityType_P) OF
        //Synchronisation de pays
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'COUNTRY': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_country';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recCountry_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recCountry_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          IF recCountry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053611, recCountry_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codeiso', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codeiso', recCountry_L.Code);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCountry_L.Name);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_phoneformat', '175490000');
                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCountry(EntityName_L, recCountry_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
              IF NOT(SearchCountry(EntityName_L, recCountry_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END;
