            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codeiso', recCountry_L.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCountry_L.Name);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_phoneformat', '175490000');
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053611, recCountry_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recCountry_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codeiso', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              ToInactive_L := FALSE;
              Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codeiso');
              IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recCountry_L.Code) THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE
              ELSE
                IF NOT(recCountry_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;

        //Synchronisation des codes postaux
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'POSTALCODE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_postalcode';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          LastData_L := '';
          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recPostalCodeCity_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              IF (ValBuffer_L - ValBufferLast_L) >= 50 THEN BEGIN
                ValBufferLast_L := ValBuffer_L;
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBufferLast_L);
              END;
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //traitement de chaque code postal qu'une seule fois
            IF LastData_L <> recPostalCodeCity_L.Code THEN BEGIN
              LastData_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.Code;
              //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053613, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
              IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
                  //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                  IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                    //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                    IF recPostalCodeCity_L.Code <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') THEN BEGIN
                      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
                      UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                    END;
                    IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                      SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                  END;
                END ELSE
                  IF NOT(SearchPostalCode(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
                    ToProcess_L := TRUE;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchPostalCode(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053613, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recPostalCodeCity_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          LastCode_L := '';
          REPEAT
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
            IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '>', LastCode_L);
            END;
            SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
            Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
            i_L := 0;
            IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
              END;
              REPEAT
                IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                  i_L += 1;
                  ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                  ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
                END;

                ToInactive_L := FALSE;
                Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
                IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recPostalCodeCity_L.Code) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE BEGIN
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(Code, Code_L);
                  IF recPostalCodeCity_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;
                END;
                IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif
                LastCode_L := Code_L;
              UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
            END;
          UNTIL Count_L = 0;
        END;

        //Synchronisation des villes
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Les codes postaux doivent ^tre synchronis's AVANT de synchroniser les villes!!!
        'CITY': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_city';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          GetSetup();
          IF recPostalCodeCity_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;

              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              IF (ValBuffer_L - ValBufferLast_L) >= 50 THEN BEGIN
                ValBufferLast_L := ValBuffer_L;
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBufferLast_L);
              END;
            END;

            //Recherche des responsables : on r'cupSre les UserId
            //V'g'tal
            PV_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PV",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PV", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PV_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PV_L) THEN BEGIN
              PV_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PV");
              PV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PV_L);
            END;
            //Lapin
            PAL_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAL", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAL_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAL_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAL_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAL_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAL");
              PAL_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAL_L);
            END;
            //Ruminant
            PAR_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAR", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAR_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAR_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAR_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAR_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAR");
              PAR_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAR_L);
            END;
            //Volaille
            PAV_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAV", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAV_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAV_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAV_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAV");
              PAV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAV_L);
            END;
            //Porc
            PAP_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAP", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAP_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAP_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAP_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAP_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAP");
              PAP_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAP_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
            PostalCodeIdFinded_L := GetGuid(Text8053613, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_inseecode', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_inseecode', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_postalcode_id', PostalCodeIdFinded_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plantresponsibleid', PV_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rabbitresponsibleid', PAL_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ruminantresponsibleid', PAR_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_poultryresponsibleid', PAV_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pigresponsibleid', PAP_L);

                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCity(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L, PostalCodeIdFinded_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchCity(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L, PostalCodeIdFinded_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_inseecode', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_postalcode_id', PostalCodeIdFinded_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plantresponsibleid', PV_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rabbitresponsibleid', PAL_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ruminantresponsibleid', PAR_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_poultryresponsibleid', PAV_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pigresponsibleid', PAP_L);
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recPostalCodeCity_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          LastCode_L := '';
          REPEAT
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
            IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '>', LastCode_L);
            END;
            SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name');
            Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
            i_L := 0;
            IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
              END;
              REPEAT
                IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                  i_L += 1;
                  ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                  ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
                END;

                ToInactive_L := FALSE;
                Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name');
                IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recPostalCodeCity_L.City) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE BEGIN
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(City, Code_L);
                  IF recPostalCodeCity_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;
                END;
                IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif
                LastCode_L := Code_L;
              UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
            END;
          UNTIL Count_L = 0;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du groupe de mesure Nav si non synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'GROUPMEASURE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'uomschedule';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'name', '=', TextConstanteNav);
            IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid'));
                UpdateGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            END ELSE
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT(GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', TextConstanteNav);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'baseuomname', TextConstanteNav);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;

        //synchronisation des unit's de mesure
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'MEASURE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'uom';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recUnitOfMeasure_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnitOfMeasure_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recUnitOfMeasure_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            Guiduomschedule_L := GetGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav);
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053621, recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantity', '1');
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', CleanGuidToCRM(Guiduomschedule_L));
                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchMeasure(EntityName_L, recUnitOfMeasure_L, Guiduomschedule_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
              IF NOT(SearchMeasure(EntityName_L, recUnitOfMeasure_L, Guiduomschedule_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantity', '1');
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', CleanGuidToCRM(Guiduomschedule_L));
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053621, recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recUnitOfMeasure_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        //Synchronisation des campagnes
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'CAMPAIGN': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_productioncampaign';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recCampaign_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recCampaign_L.COUNT;

          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recCampaign_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053622, recCampaign_L."No.");
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_Code', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCampaign_L."No.");
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recCampaign_L."No.");
                  IF recCampaign_L."Starting Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Starting Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Ending Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Ending Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. Start. Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedbegindate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. Start. Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedbegindate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. End Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedenddate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. End Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedenddate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod Start. Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedbegindate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod Start. Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedbegindate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod End Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedenddate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod End Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedenddate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCampaign(EntityName_L, recCampaign_L)) THEN
                 ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchCampaign(EntityName_L, recCampaign_L)) THEN
               ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCampaign_L."No.");
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recCampaign_L."No.");
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Starting Date"));
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Ending Date"));
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053622, recCampaign_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recCampaign_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              ToInactive_L := FALSE;
              Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
              IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recCampaign_L."No.") THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE
              ELSE
                IF NOT(recCampaign_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;

        //Synchronisation des magasins
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'LOCATION': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_store';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recLocation_L.RESET;
          recLocation_L.SETRANGE("Use As In-Transit", FALSE);
          Count_L := recLocation_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recLocation_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053623, recLocation_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                  IF recLocation_L.Code <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') THEN BEGIN
                    ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recLocation_L.Code);
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recLocation_L.Name);
                    UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  END;
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchLocation(EntityName_L, recLocation_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchLocation(EntityName_L, recLocation_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recLocation_L.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recLocation_L.Name);
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053623, recLocation_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recLocation_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
             ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
             IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              ToInactive_L := FALSE;
              Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
              IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recLocation_L.Code) THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE
              ELSE
                IF NOT(recLocation_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE
                  //Si c'est un magasin de transit, on n'en a pas besoin dans le CRM
                  IF recLocation_L."Use As In-Transit" = TRUE THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;

        //Synchronisation du prix
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'PRICE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'pricelevel';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'name', '=', TextConstanteNav);
            IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid'));
                UpdateGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            END ELSE
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT(GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', TextConstanteNav);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;

        //Synchronisation des utilisateurs
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'USER': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'systemuser';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utile pour la mise . jour
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'systemuserid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_usercode', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'domainname', '');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN
            REPEAT
              Responsable_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_usercode'), 1, 10);
              UserId_L := UPPERCASE(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'domainname'));
              IF UserId_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                PosSlash_L := STRPOS(UserId_L, '\');
                IF PosSlash_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
                  UserId_L := COPYSTR(UserId_L, PosSlash_L + 1, STRLEN(UserId_L) - PosSlash_L);
                END;
                UserId_L := COPYSTR(UserId_L, 1, 54);
                UpdateGuid(Text8053631, UserId_L, FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'systemuserid')));
                IF NOT(recSalesPersonn_L.GET(UserId_L, Responsable_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  // --> 20150626-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3038-FHS01
                  recSalesPersonn_L.RESET;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.SETRANGE("User ID", UserId_L);
                  recSalesPersonn_L.DELETEALL;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.RESET;
                  // <-- 20150626-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3038-FHS01
                  recSalesPersonn_L.INIT;
                  recSalesPersonn_L."User ID" := UserId_L;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.Code := Responsable_L;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.INSERT(TRUE);
                END;

              END;
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;

        //Synchronisation des modalit's des lieux de d'chargement
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'UNLOADINGMODALITY': BEGIN
          //synchronisation de tous les modes de d'chargement pr'sents dans Nav
          i_L := 0;
          recUnloadingMethod_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnloadingMethod_L.COUNT;
          IF recUnloadingMethod_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              SyncUnloadingMethod(recUnloadingMethod_L, COMPANYNAME, TextModify, '', recUnloadingMethod_L);
            UNTIL recUnloadingMethod_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          //d'sactivation des modes de d'chargement non pr'sents dans Nav
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '=', '175490002');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid');
              IF NOT(recUnloadingMethod_L.GET(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))) THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
              ELSE
                IF GetCodeFromGuidWithCompany(Text8053641, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE, COMPANYNAME) = '' THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;

          //synchronisation de toutes les tailles de v'hicule pr'sentes dans Nav
          i_L := 0;
          recUnloadingVehSize_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnloadingVehSize_L.COUNT;
          IF recUnloadingVehSize_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              SyncUnloadingVehSize(recUnloadingVehSize_L, COMPANYNAME, TextModify, '', recUnloadingVehSize_L);
            UNTIL recUnloadingVehSize_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          //d'sactivation de tailles de v'hicules non pr'sentes dans Nav
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');

          //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '=', '175490000');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid');
              IF NOT(recUnloadingVehSize_L.GET(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))) THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
              ELSE
                IF GetCodeFromGuidWithCompany(Text8053642, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE, COMPANYNAME) = '' THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;

          //synchronisation de tous les horaires de d'chargement pr'sents dans Nav
          i_L := 0;
          recUnloadingTime_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnloadingTime_L.COUNT;
          IF recUnloadingTime_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              SyncUnloadingTime(recUnloadingTime_L, COMPANYNAME, TextModify, '', recUnloadingTime_L);
            UNTIL recUnloadingTime_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          //d'sactivation des horaires de d'chargement non pr'sents dans Nav
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');

          //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '=', '175490001');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid');
              IF NOT(recUnloadingTime_L.GET(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))) THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
              ELSE
                IF GetCodeFromGuidWithCompany(Text8053643, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE, COMPANYNAME) = '' THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;
        //Synchronisation des activit's, familles, sous-familles et espSces
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'SPECIES': BEGIN
          Result_L := SyncActivity();
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN
            Result_L := SyncFamily();
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN
            Result_L := SyncSubFamily();
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN
            Result_L := SyncSpecies();
        END;
        //Synchronisation des entonnoir articles
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'FUNNELFILTER': BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.RESET;
          IF recFunnel_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::EntonnoirArticle, recFunnel_L.Code, TextModify, '');
            UNTIL recFunnel_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      Result_L := TRUE;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncActivity@8053714() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053604 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053603 : Integer;
      ToProcess_L@8053606 : Boolean;
      Code_L@8053608 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      recActivity_L@8053601 : Record 8052985;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise les activit's
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Activit's
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_activity';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
      recActivity_L.RESET;
      Count_L := recActivity_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recActivity_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
      REPEAT
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          i_L += 1;
          ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
        END;

        ToProcess_L := FALSE;
        //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053648, recActivity_L.Code);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
          //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
          IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
            IF (recActivity_L.Code <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key')) OR
               (recActivity_L.Name <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))
            THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recActivity_L.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recActivity_L.Name);
              UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            END;
          END ELSE
            IF NOT(SearchActivity(EntityName_L, recActivity_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
        END ELSE
          IF NOT(SearchActivity(EntityName_L, recActivity_L)) THEN
            ToProcess_L := TRUE;

        //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
        IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recActivity_L.Code);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recActivity_L.Name);
          IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
            UpdateGuid(Text8053648, recActivity_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
          ELSE
            EXIT(Result_L);
        END;
      UNTIL recActivity_L.NEXT = 0;

      //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
      Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
      i_L := 0;
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
        END;
        REPEAT
          IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            i_L += 1;
            ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
          END;

          ToInactive_L := FALSE;
          Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key');
          IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recActivity_L.Code) THEN
            ToInactive_L := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT(recActivity_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
              ToInactive_L := TRUE;

          IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

        UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
      END;

      Result_L := TRUE;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncFamily@8053716() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053604 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053603 : Integer;
      ToProcess_L@8053602 : Boolean;
      Code_L@8053601 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053600 : Boolean;
      recFamily_L@8053608 : Record 8052986;
      txtGuidActivity_L@8053609 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise les familles
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Famille
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_family';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
      recFamily_L.RESET;
      Count_L := recFamily_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recFamily_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
      REPEAT
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          i_L += 1;
          ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
        END;

        txtGuidActivity_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053648, recFamily_L."Activity Code"));
        IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(txtGuidActivity_L)) THEN BEGIN
          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053649, recFamily_L.Code);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity', '');
            //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
            IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
              IF (recFamily_L.Code <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key')) OR
                 (recFamily_L.Name <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code')) OR
                 (txtGuidActivity_L <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity'))
              THEN BEGIN
                ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recFamily_L.Code);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recFamily_L.Name);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity', txtGuidActivity_L);
                UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              END;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchFamily(EntityName_L, recFamily_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE
            IF NOT(SearchFamily(EntityName_L, recFamily_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recFamily_L.Code);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recFamily_L.Name);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity', txtGuidActivity_L);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053649, recFamily_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL recFamily_L.NEXT = 0;

      //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
      Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
      i_L := 0;
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
        END;
        REPEAT
          IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            i_L += 1;
            ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
          END;

          ToInactive_L := FALSE;
          Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key');
          IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recFamily_L.Code) THEN
            ToInactive_L := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT(recFamily_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
              ToInactive_L := TRUE;

          IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

        UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
      END;

      Result_L := TRUE;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncSubFamily@8053718() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053604 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053603 : Integer;
      ToProcess_L@8053602 : Boolean;
      Code_L@8053601 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053600 : Boolean;
      recSubFamily_L@8053608 : Record 8052987;
      txtGuidFamily_L@8053609 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise les sous familles
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Sous-famille
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_subfamily';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
      recSubFamily_L.RESET;
      Count_L := recSubFamily_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recSubFamily_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
      REPEAT
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          i_L += 1;
          ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
        END;

        txtGuidFamily_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053649, recSubFamily_L."Family Code"));

        IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(txtGuidFamily_L)) THEN BEGIN
          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053650, recSubFamily_L.Code);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family', '');
            //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
            IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
              IF (recSubFamily_L.Code <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key')) OR
                 (recSubFamily_L.Name <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code')) OR
                 (txtGuidFamily_L <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family'))
              THEN BEGIN
                ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recSubFamily_L.Code);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recSubFamily_L.Name);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family', txtGuidFamily_L);
                UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              END;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchSubFamily(EntityName_L, recSubFamily_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE
            IF NOT(SearchSubFamily(EntityName_L, recSubFamily_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recSubFamily_L.Code);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recSubFamily_L.Name);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family', txtGuidFamily_L);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053650, recSubFamily_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL recSubFamily_L.NEXT = 0;

      //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
      Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
      i_L := 0;
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
        END;
        REPEAT
          IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            i_L += 1;
            ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
          END;

          ToInactive_L := FALSE;
          Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key');
          IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recSubFamily_L.Code) THEN
            ToInactive_L := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT(recSubFamily_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
              ToInactive_L := TRUE;

          IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

        UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
      END;

      Result_L := TRUE;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncSpecies@8053719() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053604 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053603 : Integer;
      ToProcess_L@8053602 : Boolean;
      Code_L@8053601 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053600 : Boolean;
      recSpecies_L@8053608 : Record 8052988;
      txtGuidSubFamily_L@8053609 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise les espSces
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //EspSce
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_species';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
      recSpecies_L.RESET;
      Count_L := recSpecies_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recSpecies_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
      REPEAT
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          i_L += 1;
          ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
        END;

        txtGuidSubFamily_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053650, recSpecies_L."Sub-Family Code"));

        IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(txtGuidSubFamily_L)) THEN BEGIN
          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053651, recSpecies_L.Code);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', '');
            //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
            IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
              IF (recSpecies_L.Code <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key')) OR
                 (recSpecies_L.Name <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code')) OR
                 (txtGuidSubFamily_L <> GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily'))
              THEN BEGIN
                ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recSpecies_L.Code);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recSpecies_L.Name);
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', txtGuidSubFamily_L);
                UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              END;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchSpecies(EntityName_L, recSpecies_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE
            IF NOT(SearchSpecies(EntityName_L, recSpecies_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', recSpecies_L.Code);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recSpecies_L.Name);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', txtGuidSubFamily_L);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053651, recSpecies_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL recSpecies_L.NEXT = 0;

      //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
      Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
      i_L := 0;
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
        END;
        REPEAT
          IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            i_L += 1;
            ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
          END;

          ToInactive_L := FALSE;
          Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_key');
          IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recSpecies_L.Code) THEN
            ToInactive_L := TRUE
          ELSE
            IF NOT(recSpecies_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
              ToInactive_L := TRUE;

          IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
            SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

        UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
      END;

      Result_L := TRUE;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncItemToCrm@8053622(recItem_P@8053602 : Record 27;ProductCode_P@8053608 : Code[54];recVariant_P@8053606 : Record 5401;UseVariant_P@8053607 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CrmGuid_L@8053603 : GUID;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      ItemToCreate_L@8053604 : Boolean;
      Count_L@8053610 : Integer;
      PriceGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      UpdatePrice_L@8053609 : Boolean;
      PriceLevelId_L@8053613 : Text[50];
      UomId_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      QuantitySellingCode_L@8053615 : Text[1];
      CodeGuid_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053000 : Record 8053601;
      ScheduleId_L@8053612 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise l'article avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recItem_P : Article
      //    - ProductCode_P : code produit
      //    - recVariant_P : variant
      //    - UseVariant_P : utilisation d'un variant
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Le code des piSces dans le CRM est du type CodeItem ou CodeItem-Variant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //initialisation
      EntityName_L := 'product';
      PriceLevelId_L := GetGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav);
      ScheduleId_L := GetGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav);

      IF recItem_P."Base Unit of Measure" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        //D'but synchronisation
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        ItemToCreate_L := FALSE;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053627, ProductCode_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
          IF NOT(GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            ItemToCreate_L := TRUE;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          ItemToCreate_L := TRUE;
        END;

        IF ItemToCreate_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          //Liaison entre l'article Nav et le produit CRM si celle-ci n'existe pas encore
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', '=', ProductCode_P);
          IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'));
            CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053627, ProductCode_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            ItemToCreate_L := FALSE;
          END;
        END;

        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', ProductCode_P);
        //Utilisation de la description du variant si on en utilise un sinon on prend celui de l'article
        IF UseVariant_P = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20150225-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2797-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //SetField(EntityName_L, 'Name', recItem_P.Description + recItem_P."Description 2");
          //AFTER
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'Name', recItem_P.Description);
          // <-- 20150225-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2797-FHS01
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'producttypecode', '1');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'Price', FormatDecToCrm(recItem_P."Reference Sales Price"));
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_indivisiblequantity', FormatDecToCrm(recItem_P."Indiv. Base Qty"));
        END ELSE BEGIN
          // --> 20150225-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2797-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //SetField(EntityName_L, 'Name', recVariant_P.Description + recVariant_P."Description 2");
          //AFTER
          // <-- 20150225-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2797-FHS01
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'Name', recVariant_P.Description);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'producttypecode', '2');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'Price', FormatDecToCrm(recVariant_P."Reference Sales Price"));
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_indivisiblequantity', FormatDecToCrm(recVariant_P."Indiv. Base Qty"));
        END;
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'defaultuomscheduleid', ScheduleId_L);
        TxtEntityGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053621, recItem_P."Base Unit of Measure");
        SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'defaultuomid', TxtEntityGuid_L);
        SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid', PriceLevelId_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantitydecimal', '3');

        IF recItem_P."Activity Code" <> '' THEN
          SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity', GetGuid(Text8053648, recItem_P."Activity Code"));
        IF recItem_P."Family Code" <> '' THEN
          SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family', GetGuid(Text8053649, recItem_P."Family Code"));
        IF recItem_P."Sub-Family Code" <> '' THEN
          SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', GetGuid(Text8053650, recItem_P."Sub-Family Code"));
        IF recItem_P."Species Code" <> '' THEN
          SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species', GetGuid(Text8053651, recItem_P."Species Code"));

        //Si la donn' n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF ItemToCreate_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Cr'ation r'ussie
            CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053627, ProductCode_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            END
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
          END;
        END;

        IF ((UseVariant_P = FALSE) AND (recItem_P."Blocked For Sales" = FALSE)) OR
           ((UseVariant_P = TRUE) AND (recVariant_P."Blocked For Sales" = FALSE))
        THEN BEGIN
          SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active
        END ELSE BEGIN
          SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2); //Not Active
        END;

        //Cr'ation du tarif
        //d'duction de la m'thode de prix dans le CRM
        EntityName_L := 'productpricelevel';
        UpdatePrice_L := TRUE;

        PriceGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053633, ProductCode_P);
        IF (PriceGuid_L = '') THEN BEGIN
          UomId_L := GetGuid(Text8053621, recItem_P."Base Unit of Measure");
          TxtCrmGuid_L := CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L);
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'productpricelevelid', '');

          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(PriceLevelId_L));
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'productid', '=', TxtCrmGuid_L);
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'uomid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(UomId_L));

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          IF Count_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
            //L''l'ment tarifaire n'existait pas => cr'ation
            UpdatePrice_L := FALSE;
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid', PriceLevelId_L);
            SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'productid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
            SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'uomid', UomId_L);
            SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', ScheduleId_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount', FormatDecToCrm(recItem_P."Reference Sales Price"));
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount_base', FormatDecToCrm(recItem_P."Reference Sales Price"));
            //Quantit' et fraction
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantitysellingcode', '3');
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, PriceGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Cr'ation r'ussie
              CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053633, ProductCode_P, PriceGuid_L);
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            PriceGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'productpricelevelid');
            CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053633, ProductCode_P, PriceGuid_L);
          END;
        END;

        IF UpdatePrice_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          //mise . jour de l''l'ment tarifaire uniquement s'il faut le mettre . jour
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          UpdatePrice_L := FALSE;
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantitysellingcode', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount', '');
          IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, PriceGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'quantitysellingcode') <> '3' THEN BEGIN
              QuantitySellingCode_L := '3';
              UpdatePrice_L := TRUE;
            END;
            // --> 20150626-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1769-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'amount') <> FORMAT(recItem_P."Reference Sales Price") THEN
              UpdatePrice_L := TRUE;

            IF UpdatePrice_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150626-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1769-FHS01
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

            IF QuantitySellingCode_L <> '' THEN
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantitysellingcode', QuantitySellingCode_L);
            SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', ScheduleId_L);
            IF UseVariant_P = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount', FormatDecToCrm(recVariant_P."Reference Sales Price"));
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount_base', FormatDecToCrm(recVariant_P."Reference Sales Price"));
            END
            ELSE BEGIN
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount', FormatDecToCrm(recItem_P."Reference Sales Price"));
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'amount_base', FormatDecToCrm(recItem_P."Reference Sales Price"));
            END;
            UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, PriceGuid_L);
            // --> 20150626-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1769-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //END;
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150626-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1769-FHS01
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAllFilteredItem@8053650(WindowsOpened_P@8053601 : Boolean);
    VAR
      recVariant_L@8053602 : Record 5401;
      recItem_L@8053600 : Record 27;
      Count_L@8053605 : Integer;
      i_L@8053603 : Integer;
      ValBufferLast_L@8053604 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053606 : Integer;
      Filter_L@8053607 : Text[1000];
      EntityName_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      ToInactive_L@8053609 : Boolean;
      ProductCode_L@8053610 : Text[54];
      ItemCode_L@8053611 : Code[20];
      VariantCode_L@8053612 : Code[20];
      UseVariant_L@8053613 : Boolean;
      recVariantSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053090;
      SyncItem_L@8053001 : Boolean;
      SyncVariant_L@8053002 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise tous les articles filtr's avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        GetSetup();

        recItem_L.RESET;
        Count_L := recItem_L.COUNT;
        i_L := 0;
        ValBufferLast_L := 0;

        //gestion de la fen^tre suivant qu'elle est d'j. initialis'e ou non
        IF WindowsOpened_P = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          OpenWindow();
          NewStepWindow(1);
        END;

        IF recItem_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
            IF (ValBuffer_L - ValBufferLast_L) >= 50 THEN BEGIN
              ValBufferLast_L := ValBuffer_L;
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBufferLast_L);
            END;

            SyncItemVariant(recItem_L);
          UNTIL recItem_L.NEXT = 0;

        //2Sme 'tape : d'sactivation des produits pr'sent uniquement c"t' CRM
        IF WindowsOpened_P = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          NewStepWindow(2);
        END;

        //pr'paration des filtres sur les articles
        recItem_L.RESET;
        IF Filter_L <> '' THEN
          recItem_L.SETVIEW(Filter_L);

        EntityName_L := 'product';

        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        SetField(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'producttypecode', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
        Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
        i_L := 0;
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);

            ToInactive_L := FALSE;
            ProductCode_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'productnumber');
            IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'producttypecode') = '1' THEN BEGIN
              //Produit
              recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", ProductCode_L);
              IF recItem_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //Variant
              SearchProductNo(ProductCode_L, UseVariant_L, ItemCode_L, VariantCode_L);

              //V'rification du produit
              recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", ItemCode_L);
              IF recItem_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF (UseVariant_L = TRUE) AND (ToInactive_L = FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                //c'est un variant
                IF NOT(recVariant_L.GET(ItemCode_L, VariantCode_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE;
              END;
            END;

            IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
              SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;

        //fermeture de la fen^tre uniquement si elle a 't' ouverte ici
        IF WindowsOpened_P = FALSE THEN
          CloseWindow();
      END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetTierMSFromCrm@1000000018(CrmGuid_P@1000000000 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@1000000001 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Text[250];
    VAR
      recThridPartyToCreate_L@1000000002 : Record 8053600;
      StatusCode_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      SynchroState_L@8053600 : Text[20];
      OwnerCode_L@1000000003 : Code[100];
      RecCRMSalesperson_L@1000000004 : Record 8053608;
      RecContactSalesperson_L@1000000005 : Record 8053160;
      RecContactSalesperson2_L@1000000006 : Record 8053160;
      ContactNo_L@1000000007 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'' le Tiers MS depuis les donn'es disponibles dans le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID du compte (contact) . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquel cr'er le contact
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recThridPartyToCreate_L.TABLECAPTION);
      EntityName_L := 'account';

      IF NOT(recThridPartyToCreate_L.GET(recCrmOrganisation_P.Code, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'Name', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line1', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line2', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'Telephone1', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod', '');

        SetField(EntityName_L, 'Fax', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line3', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationstate', '');
        //SetField(EntityName_L, 'telephone2', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_responsibilitywithinthecooperative', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_shortname', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl', '');

        // --> 20160316-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2847-BSI01
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber', '');
        // <-- 20160316-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2847-BSI01

        //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
        IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20160316-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2847-BSI01
          IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber') <> '' THEN
            EXIT('');
          // <-- 20160316-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2847-BSI01

          SynchroState_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationstate'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(SynchroState_L));
          IF (SynchroState_L = '175490000') OR (SynchroState_L = '175490003') THEN BEGIN //synchronised = demande de synchro
            //Cr'ation du tiers MS dans la soci't' . l'origine de la demande
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.INIT;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id" := CrmGuid_P;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.Name := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Name'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.Name));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.Address := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line1'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.Address));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Address 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line2'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Address 2"));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.City := COPYSTR(RemoveSeparator(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612,
              GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id'), FALSE)), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.City));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Phone No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Telephone1'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Phone No."));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Search Name" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_shortname'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Search Name"));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."E-Mail" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."E-Mail"));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Home Page" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Home Page"));

            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Country/Region Code" := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053611, GetAttribute(EntityName_L,
              'cmc_address1_country_id'), FALSE);

            // --> 20160114-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2628-NED01
            {before
            IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod') = '175490000' THEN BEGIN
              recThridPartyToCreate_L."Payment Method Code" := recCRMSetup_G."Account Payment";
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recThridPartyToCreate_L."Payment Method Code" := recCRMSetup_G."Levy Payment";
            END;
            after}
            // <-- 20160114-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2628-NED01

            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Fax No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Fax'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Fax No."));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Post Code" := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053613, GetAttribute(EntityName_L,
