              'cmc_address1_postalcode_id'), FALSE);
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.County := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line3'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.County));
            //recThridPartyToCreate_L."Extension No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'telephone2'), 1,
            //  MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Extension No."));

            //Gestion du StatusCode pour remplir l'Activity State
            StatusCode_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode);
            CASE StatusCode_L OF
              '2': // not active
                recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State"::"Not Active";
              '175490000': //actif sans activit'
                recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State"::"Active Without Activity";
              ELSE
                recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State"::Active;
            END;

            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Contact Type" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Contact Type"::Farm;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Company No." := recCrmOrganisation_P.Code;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.Status := recThridPartyToCreate_L.Status::" ";

            //synchronisation des responsabilit's
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Job Responsability" := COPYSTR(GetResponsability(GetAttribute(EntityName_L,
              'cmc_responsibilitywithinthecooperative')), 1, 20);

            IF recThridPartyToCreate_L.INSERT(TRUE) THEN
              Result_L := '';

            //mise . jour du CRM pour indiquer que le compte est synchronis'
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationstate', '175490003'); //mettre en cours
            UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            Result_L := '';
          END;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //le compte . d'j. 't' trait'
        Result_L := '';
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAdditionalAddress@8053003(CrmGuid_P@1000000000 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@1000000001 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Text[250];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      ShipToCode_L@8053008 : Code[54];
      Modify_L@8053000 : Boolean;
      recShip_L@8053001 : Record 222;
      Doc1_L@8053003 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053002 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053004 : Text[54];
      cduContactMgmt_L@8053005 : Codeunit 8053377;
      Cust_L@8053006 : Code[20];
      Text_L@8053007 : Text[110];
      Post_L@8053009 : Code[20];
      City_L@8053010 : Text[30];
      Country_L@8053011 : Code[10];
      i_L@8053012 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'' l'adresse de livraison sur le client (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'adresse de livraison . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquel cr'er l'adresse
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';

      //gestion de l'adresse de livraison
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_additionaladdress';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', '');

      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype') <> '175490003' THEN BEGIN
          Modify_L := FALSE;
          //utilisation d'une adresse de livraison uniquement si ce n'est pas une adresse principale
          ShipToCode_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053636, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);

          IF ShipToCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            Doc1_L := COPYSTR(ShipToCode_L, 1, STRPOS(ShipToCode_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
            Doc2_L := COPYSTR(ShipToCode_L, STRPOS(ShipToCode_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(ShipToCode_L));
            IF recShip_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L) THEN BEGIN
              Modify_L := TRUE;
            END;
          END;

          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id'); //contact
          Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
          GuidFinded(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recShip_L.FIELDCAPTION("Customer No."));
          // --> 20150618-FINA13-CMC02.02.00-2965-DRN01
          //BEFORE
          //Cust_L := cduContactMgmt_L.GetCustVentNoFromContNo(Text_L, 0);
          //AFTER
          Cust_L := cduContactMgmt_L.GetCustVendBankFromContact(Text_L, 0);
          // <-- 20150618-FINA13-CMC02.02.00-2965-DRN01

          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id');
          Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053613, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
          IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Post_L := Text_L;
          END;

          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id');
          Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
          IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            City_L := Text_L;
          END;

          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id');
          Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053611, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
          IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Country_L := Text_L;
          END;

          IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            i_L := 0;
            recShip_L.RESET;
            recShip_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", Cust_L);
            REPEAT
              i_L += 1;
              recShip_L.SETRANGE(Code, FORMAT(i_L));
            UNTIL recShip_L.ISEMPTY;
            CLEAR(recShip_L);
            recShip_L.INIT;
            recShip_L."Customer No." := Cust_L;
            recShip_L.Code := FORMAT(i_L);
          END;

          recShip_L."Post Code" := Post_L;
          recShip_L.City := City_L;
          recShip_L."Country/Region Code" := Country_L;
          recShip_L.Address := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recShip_L.Address));
          recShip_L."Address 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recShip_L."Address 2"));
          recShip_L.County := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recShip_L.County));

          IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            recShip_L.INSERT(TRUE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recShip_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
          UpdateGuid(Text8053636, recShip_L."Customer No." + GetSeparator() + recShip_L.Code, CrmGuid_P);
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSimpleOrder@8053648(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053603 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recSalesHeader_L@8053605 : Record 8053610;
      recGuid_L@8053606 : Record 8053601;
      recRelation_L@8053607 : Record 5054;
      AddressGuid_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      LineGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      crmLineCount_L@8053612 : Integer;
      recComment_L@8053613 : Record 44;
      Comment_L@8053614 : Text[250];
      ShipToCode_L@8053615 : Code[54];
      DeliveryType_L@8053616 : Boolean;
      OrderCode_L@8053608 : Code[20];
      LineNo_L@8053611 : Integer;
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053619 : Record 8053608;
      Salesperson_L@8053617 : Code[10];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053618 : Text[50];
      Text_L@8053620 : Text[110];
      Statut_L@8053621 : Code[10];
      recGuidTmp_L@8053000 : TEMPORARY Record 8053601;
      i_L@8053001 : Integer;
      LastCode_L@8053002 : Text[50];
      crmLineCountTotal_L@1000000000 : Integer;
      RecSpySynchro_L@1000000001 : Record 60089;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation de la commande simplifi'e dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID du compte (contact) . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquel cr'er la commande
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'salesorder';
      GetSetup();

      //Il faut 'viter de recr'er les commandes d'j. int'gr'es
      recGuid_L.RESET;
      recGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data",GUID);
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Simple Order Header");
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
      IF NOT(recGuid_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;

      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'customerid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_concernedcampaign_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'requestdeliveryby', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryaddress_id', '');

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'ownerid', '');

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_store_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverytype', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_logisticscomment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_userid', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Statut_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode);
        IF (Statut_L = '100001') OR (Statut_L = '3') OR (Statut_L = '100002')THEN BEGIN
          recRelation_L.RESET;
          recRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "Contact No.", "MC Company Code");
          recRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
          recRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'Customerid'), FALSE));
          recRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", recCrmOrganisation_P.Code);
          IF recRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            //Cr'ation de l'ent^te de la commande dans la soci't' . l'origine de la demande
            IF NOT(recSalesHeader_L.GET(FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P))) THEN BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.INIT;
              recSalesHeader_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P);
              recSalesHeader_L.Type := recSalesHeader_L.Type::"Simple Order";
              recSalesHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            END;

            recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := TRUE;
            recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := '';
            recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.", recRelation_L."No.");

            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_store_id');
            Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053623, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
            IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", Text_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
              recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesHeader_L.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code");
            END;

            recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Terms Code", recCRMSetup_G."Payment Term");

            IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod') = '175490000' THEN BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Method Code", recCRMSetup_G."Account Payment");
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Method Code", recCRMSetup_G."Levy Payment");
            END;

            IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverytype') = '175490001' THEN BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Method Code", recCRMSetup_G."Not Shipped");
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Method Code", recCRMSetup_G."Shipment Code");
            END;

            recSalesHeader_L."Requested Delivery Date" := FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'requestdeliveryby'));

            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_concernedcampaign_id');
            Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
            IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("CMC Campaign No.", Text_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
              recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesHeader_L.FIELDCAPTION("CMC Campaign No.");
            END;

            recSalesHeader_L."External Document No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recSalesHeader_L."External Document No."));

            recSalesHeader_L.Comment := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_logisticscomment'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recSalesHeader_L.Comment));

            //Recherche du GUID du vendeur
            Salesperson_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetSalespersonCode(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053631,
              GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_userid'), FALSE));
            IF Salesperson_L <> '' THEN
              recSalesHeader_L."Salesperson Code" := Salesperson_L;

            //gestion de l'adresse de livraison
            AddressGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryaddress_id'));
            IF AddressGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(AddressGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                EntityName_L := 'cmc_additionaladdress';
                ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '');
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', '');
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', '');
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '');
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', '');

                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, AddressGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype') <> '175490003' THEN BEGIN
                    //utilisation d'une adresse de livraison uniquement si ce n'est pas une adresse principale
                    ShipToCode_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053636, AddressGuid_L, FALSE);
                    IF NOT(GuidFindedWithoutError(ShipToCode_L, AddressGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                      recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
                      recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesHeader_L.FIELDCAPTION("Ship-to Code");
                    END;

                    IF STRPOS(ShipToCode_L, GetSeparator()) > 0 THEN BEGIN
                      ShipToCode_L := COPYSTR(ShipToCode_L, STRPOS(ShipToCode_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, STRLEN(ShipToCode_L));
                    END;
                    recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", ShipToCode_L);
                    recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to Address" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1'), 1,
                      MAXSTRLEN(recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to Address"));
                    recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to Address 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2'), 1,
                      MAXSTRLEN(recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to Address 2"));

                    TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id');
                    Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053613, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
                    IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                      recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to Post Code" := Text_L;
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
                      recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesHeader_L.FIELDCAPTION("Ship-to Post Code");
                    END;

                    TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id');
                    Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
                    IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                      // --> 20150527-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3012-FHS01
                      //BEFORE
                      //recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to City" := Text_L;
                      //AFTER
                      recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to City" := COPYSTR(RemoveSeparator(Text_L), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recSalesHeader_L."Ship-to City"))
      ;
                      // <-- 20150527-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3012-FHS01
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
                      recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesHeader_L.FIELDCAPTION("Ship-to City");
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END;

            // --> 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
            recSalesHeader_L."Creation Date" := CREATEDATETIME(WORKDATE,TIME);
            recSalesHeader_L."Old Shipment Method Code" := recSalesHeader_L."Shipment Method Code";
            recSalesHeader_L."Old Salesperson Code" := recSalesHeader_L."Salesperson Code";
            recSalesHeader_L."Old Payment Method Code" := recSalesHeader_L."Payment Method Code";
            recSalesHeader_L."Old Requested Delivery Date" := recSalesHeader_L."Requested Delivery Date";
            recSalesHeader_L."Old Linked Order No." := recSalesHeader_L."Linked Order No.";
            recSalesHeader_L."Old Comment" := recSalesHeader_L.Comment;
            // <-- 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01

            recSalesHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
            //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
            CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053629, recSalesHeader_L."No.", CrmGuid_P);
            Result_L := TRUE;
            i_L := 0;

            //recherche des lignes de la commande en parcourant toutes les pages
            EntityName_L := 'salesorderdetail';
            LastCode_L := '';
            REPEAT
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'salesorderdetailid', '');

              //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
              SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(CrmGuid_P));
              IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN
                SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'salesorderdetailid', '>', LastCode_L);
              SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, 'salesorderdetailid');

              //Remplissage du buffer permettant d'avoir la liste des lignes de la commande
              crmLineCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
              // --> 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01
              crmLineCountTotal_L += crmLineCount_L;
              // <-- 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01
              IF crmLineCount_L > 0 THEN
                REPEAT
                  i_L += 1;
                  LineGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'salesorderdetailid');
                  recGuidTmp_L.INIT;
                  recGuidTmp_L."Type Data" := recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Simple Order Line";
                  recGuidTmp_L."Code Nav" := FORMAT(i_L);
                  recGuidTmp_L.GUID := FormatGuid(LineGuid_L);
                  recGuidTmp_L.INSERT;
                  LastCode_L := LineGuid_L;
                  // --> 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01
                  CLEAR(RecSpySynchro_L);
                  RecSpySynchro_L."CRM Header Guid" := CrmGuid_P;
                  RecSpySynchro_L."CRM Guid" := recGuidTmp_L.GUID;
                  RecSpySynchro_L.Buffer := TRUE;
                  IF RecSpySynchro_L.INSERT THEN;
                  // <-- 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01
                UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
            UNTIL crmLineCount_L = 0;

            // --> 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01
            IF crmLineCountTotal_L <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              CLEAR(RecSpySynchro_L);
              RecSpySynchro_L."CRM Header Guid" := CrmGuid_P;
              RecSpySynchro_L."Total Number Lines" := crmLineCountTotal_L;
              RecSpySynchro_L.Buffer := TRUE;
              IF RecSpySynchro_L.INSERT THEN;
            END;
            // <-- 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01

            //R'cup'ration des lignes de la commande
            recGuidTmp_L.RESET;
            IF recGuidTmp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
              REPEAT
                IF NOT(GetSimpleOrderLine(FORMAT(recGuidTmp_L.GUID), recCrmOrganisation_P, recSalesHeader_L)) THEN
                  Result_L := FALSE;
              UNTIL recGuidTmp_L.NEXT = 0;

            IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              COMMIT;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            Text_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'name');
            ClearConnector();
            ERROR(Text8053647, Text_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //si la commande n'est pas au statut permettant la synchronisation, on ne traite pas cet ordre
          Result_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //La commande siimplifi'e a d'j. 't' supprim'e
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSimpleOrderLine@8053672(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075;recSalesHeader_P@8053602 : Record 8053610) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053609 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053608 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      recSalesLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053611;
      recGuid_L@8053605 : Record 8053601;
      recRelation_L@8053604 : Record 5054;
      Decimal_L@8053611 : Decimal;
      Variant_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      VariantNo_L@8053615 : Code[20];
      ItemNo_L@8053616 : Code[20];
      UserCode_L@8053613 : Code[20];
      UnloadingGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      UnloadingModeGuid_L@8053625 : Text[50];
      UnloadingScheduleGuid_L@8053626 : Text[50];
      UnloadingSizeGuid_L@8053627 : Text[50];
      AddressGuid_L@8053618 : Text[50];
      ShipToCode_L@8053619 : Code[54];
      UnloadingCode_L@8053620 : Code[54];
      recUnloadingPlace_L@8053621 : Record 8053169;
      Quantity_L@8053603 : Decimal;
      Price_L@8053622 : Decimal;
      CodeSize_L@8053628 : Code[10];
      CodeSchedule_L@8053629 : Code[10];
      CodeMode_L@8053630 : Code[10];
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053634 : Record 8053608;
      Salesperson_L@8053617 : Code[10];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053624 : Text[50];
      Text_L@8053623 : Text[110];
      recSeasonTmp_L@8053610 : TEMPORARY Record 8053154;
      UnloadingIssue_L@8053000 : Boolean;
      RecSpySynchro_L@1000000000 : Record 60089;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation des lignes de la commande simplifi'e dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID du compte (contact) . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquel cr'er la commande
      //    - recSalesHeader_P : Ent^te de commandde de SAS
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'salesorderdetail';

      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'productid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_concernedcampaign_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'salesrepid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'ispriceoverridden', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'priceperunit', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'requestdeliveryby', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdatedifference', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverymodality', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_store_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_locationunloading_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmode_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryaddress_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_schedule_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_size_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_logisticscomment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverymodality', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN

        //Cr'ation de l'ent^te de la commande dans la soci't' . l'origine de la demande
        IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesHeader_P."CRM Guid", FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P))) THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L.RESET;
          recSalesLine_L.INIT;
          recSalesLine_L."CRM Header Guid" := recSalesHeader_P."CRM Guid";
          recSalesLine_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P);
          recSalesLine_L."Secondary Header Key" := recSalesHeader_P."Secondary Key";
          recSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
          // --> 20170907-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-BSI01
          COMMIT;
          // <-- 20170907-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-BSI01
        END;

        // --> 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01
        CLEAR(RecSpySynchro_L);
        RecSpySynchro_L."CRM Header Guid" := recSalesHeader_P."CRM Guid";
        RecSpySynchro_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P);
        IF RecSpySynchro_L.INSERT THEN;
        // <-- 20170905-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-CJY01

        recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := TRUE;
        recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := '';

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_store_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053623, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Location Code" := Text_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code");
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'productid');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053627, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          SearchProductNo(Text_L, Variant_L, ItemNo_L, VariantNo_L);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("No.");
        END;

        recSalesLine_L."No." := ItemNo_L;
        IF Variant_L = TRUE THEN
          recSalesLine_L."Variant Code" := VariantNo_L
        ELSE
          IF recSalesLine_L."Variant Code" <> '' THEN
            recSalesLine_L."Variant Code" := '';


        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'uomid');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053621, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Unit of Measure" := Text_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Unit of Measure");
        END;
        Quantity_L := FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'quantity'), 1, 30));

        recSalesLine_L.Quantity := Quantity_L;

        Price_L := FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'priceperunit'), 1, 30));
        IF UPPERCASE(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ispriceoverridden')) = 'TRUE' THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Negociated Price" := TRUE;
          recSalesLine_L."Unit Price" := Price_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Negociated Price" := FALSE;
        END;

        // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        {
        //gestion de l'adresse de livraison
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryaddress_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053636, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          ShipToCode_L := COPYSTR(Text_L, STRPOS(Text_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, STRLEN(Text_L));
          recSalesLine_L."Ship-to Code", ShipToCode_L);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Ship-to Code");
        END;
        }
        //AFTER
        // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01

        IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverymodality') = '175490001' THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Shipment Method Code" := recCRMSetup_G."Not Shipped";
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Shipment Method Code" := recCRMSetup_G."Shipment Code";
        END;

        //Gestion du lieu de d'chargement => va permettre de remplir le lieu de d'chagement sur les lignes
        CodeSize_L := '';
        CodeSchedule_L := '';
        CodeMode_L := '';

        // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        UnloadingIssue_L := FALSE;
        // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_locationunloading_id');
        UnloadingCode_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053637, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(UnloadingCode_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF UnloadingCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            UnloadingCode_L := COPYSTR(UnloadingCode_L, STRPOS(UnloadingCode_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, STRLEN(UnloadingCode_L));
            IF recUnloadingPlace_L.GET(recSalesHeader_P."Sell-to Customer No.", recSalesLine_L."Ship-to Code",
               recSalesLine_L."Unloading Place Code")
            THEN BEGIN
              recSalesLine_L."Prod. Activity Code" := recUnloadingPlace_L."Production Activity Code";
              recSalesLine_L."Unloading Place Code" := UnloadingCode_L;

              CodeSize_L := recUnloadingPlace_L."Vehicle Size Code";
              CodeMode_L := recUnloadingPlace_L."Unloading Method Code";
              CodeSchedule_L := recUnloadingPlace_L."Time Slot Code";
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          //recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Unloading Place Code");
          //AFTER
          UnloadingIssue_L := TRUE;
          // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        END;

        //Synchronisation des modalit's des lieux de d'chargement
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmode_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053641, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF Text_L <> '' THEN
            CodeMode_L := Text_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Unloading Method Code");
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_schedule_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053643, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF Text_L <> '' THEN
            CodeSchedule_L := Text_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Time Slot Code");
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_size_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053642, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF Text_L <> '' THEN
            CodeSize_L := Text_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Vehicle Size Code");
        END;

        IF CodeSize_L <> '' THEN
          recSalesLine_L."Vehicle Size Code" := CodeSize_L
        ELSE
          recSalesLine_L."Vehicle Size Code" := CodeSize_L;

        IF CodeSchedule_L <> '' THEN
          recSalesLine_L."Time Slot Code" := CodeSchedule_L
        ELSE
          recSalesLine_L."Time Slot Code" := CodeSchedule_L;

        IF CodeMode_L <> '' THEN
          recSalesLine_L."Unloading Method Code" := CodeMode_L
        ELSE
          recSalesLine_L."Unloading Method Code" := CodeMode_L;

        //gestion des commentaires logistics
        recSalesLine_L.Comment := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_logisticscomment'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recSalesLine_L.Comment));

        recSalesLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" := FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'requestdeliveryby'));
        EVALUATE(recSalesLine_L."Due Pivot Date Gap", GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdatedifference'));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_concernedcampaign_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No." := Text_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
          recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("CMC Campaign No.");
        END;
        UserCode_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053631, GetAttribute(EntityName_L,
          'salesrepid'), FALSE);

        Salesperson_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetSalespersonCode(UserCode_L);
        IF Salesperson_L <> '' THEN
          recSalesLine_L."Salesperson Code" := Salesperson_L;

        // --> 20170907-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-BSI01
        {before
        IF recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          GetAvailableSeason(recSeasonTmp_L, recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.", recSalesLine_L);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          GetAvailableSeason(recSeasonTmp_L, recSalesHeader_P."CMC Campaign No.", recSalesLine_L);
        END;
        // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        IF recSeasonTmp_L.COUNT <> 1 THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Season ID No.", 0);
        END ELSE
        // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          IF recSeasonTmp_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Season ID No.", recSeasonTmp_L."ID No.");
          END;
        after}
        IF recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Season ID No." := ReturnAvailableSeason(recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.", recSalesLine_L);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L."Season ID No." := ReturnAvailableSeason(recSalesHeader_P."CMC Campaign No.", recSalesLine_L);
        END;
        // <-- 20170907-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-BSI01

        // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        //gestion de l'adresse de livraison
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryaddress_id');
        Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053636, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF UnloadingIssue_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
            recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Unloading Place Code");
          END;
          ShipToCode_L := COPYSTR(Text_L, STRPOS(Text_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, STRLEN(Text_L));
          recSalesLine_L."Ship-to Code" := ShipToCode_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
              EntityName_L := 'cmc_additionaladdress';
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '');
              IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, AddressGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype') <> '175490003' THEN BEGIN
                  recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := FALSE;
                  recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Ship-to Code");
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01

        // --> 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
        recSalesLine_L."Creation Date" := CREATEDATETIME(WORKDATE,TIME);
        recSalesLine_L."Old No." := recSalesLine_L."No.";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Location Code" := recSalesLine_L."Location Code";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Quantity" := recSalesLine_L.Quantity;
        recSalesLine_L."Old Unit Price" := recSalesLine_L."Unit Price";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Variant Code" := recSalesLine_L."Variant Code";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Requested Delivery Date" := recSalesLine_L."Requested Delivery Date";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Negociated Price" := recSalesLine_L."Negociated Price";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Salesperson Code" := recSalesLine_L."Salesperson Code";
        recSalesLine_L."Old CMC Campaign No." := recSalesLine_L."CMC Campaign No.";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Season ID No." := recSalesLine_L."Season ID No.";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Due Pivot Date Gap" := recSalesLine_L."Due Pivot Date Gap";
        recSalesLine_L."Old Comment" := recSalesLine_L.Comment;
        // <-- 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
        recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        Result_L := TRUE;
        COMMIT;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncInsertion@8053664(RecRef_P@1000000002 : RecordRef;CompanyCode_P@8053601 : Code[10];CompanyName_P@8053605 : Text[30];ThirdPartyNo_P@8053609 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      recCustomer_L@8053602 : Record 18;
      recVendor_L@8053603 : Record 23;
      recContact_L@8053604 : Record 5050;
      recThirdPartyToCreate_L@8053607 : Record 8053600;
      FieldRef_L@8053608 : FieldRef;
      recContactJobResponsability_L@8053600 : Record 5067;
      recContactRelation_L@8053606 : Record 5054;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lors de l'insertion d'un contacct, d'un client ou d'un fournisseur ayant un Tiers MS
      // cette fonction est appel'e pour r'cup'rer les valeurs du Tiers MS a cr'er
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - CompanyCode_P : No de la soci't' dans laquelle l'enregistrement est cr''
      //    - CompanyName_P : nom de la soci't'
      //    - ThirdPartyNo_P : Num'ro du contact
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InterfaceIsActiveOnCompany(CompanyName_P) THEN BEGIN
        CASE RecRef_P.NUMBER OF
          DATABASE::Customer: BEGIN
            //Synchronisation des clients
            //Recherche du Tiers MS a cr'er
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recCustomer_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
            recCustomer_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));

            //Recherche du Tiers MS . cr'er
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.RESET;
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Company No.", "No.");
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("Company No.", CompanyCode_P);
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("No.", ThirdPartyNo_P);
            IF recThirdPartyToCreate_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              // --> 20160114-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2628-NED01
              //recCustomer_L."Payment Method Code" := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Payment Method Code";
              // <-- 20160114-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2628-NED01
              recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";
              recCustomer_L.MODIFY;
            END;
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recContactRelation_L.RESET;
            recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CompanyCode_P);
            recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
            IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              recContact_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
              recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
              recContact_L."Contact CRM" := TRUE;
              recContact_L.MODIFY;
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recContactRelation_L."Contact No.", TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            END;
          END;
          DATABASE::Vendor: BEGIN
            //Synchronisation des fournisseurs
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recVendor_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
            recVendor_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            //Recherche du Tiers MS . cr'er
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.RESET;
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Company No.", "No.");
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("Company No.", CompanyCode_P);
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("No.", ThirdPartyNo_P);
            IF recThirdPartyToCreate_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              recVendor_L."Setting-off Allowed" := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Setting-off Allowed";
              recVendor_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";
              recVendor_L.MODIFY;
            END;
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recContactRelation_L.RESET;
            recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CompanyCode_P);
            recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
            IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              recContact_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
              recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
              recContact_L."Contact CRM" := TRUE;
              recContact_L.MODIFY;
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recContactRelation_L."Contact No.", TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            END;
          END;
          DATABASE::Contact: BEGIN
            //Synchronisation des contacts
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            recContact_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
            recContact_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            //Recherche du Tiers MS . cr'er
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.RESET;
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Company No.", "No.");
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("Company No.", CompanyCode_P);
            recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("No.", ThirdPartyNo_P);
            IF recThirdPartyToCreate_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              //FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(5061); //Mobile Phone No.

              //FieldRef_L.VALUE := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Extension No.";

              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(8061180); //Dynamics CRM id
              FieldRef_L.VALUE := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";

              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(3); //Search Name
              // --> 20171206-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30602-JCD01
              {BEFORE
              FieldRef_L.VALUE := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Search Name";
              AFTER}
              IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE) = '' THEN
              FieldRef_L.VALUE := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Search Name";
              // <-- 20171206-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30602-JCD01
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(8053176); //Contact CRM
              FieldRef_L.VALUE := TRUE;

              RecRef_P.MODIFY;

              IF recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Job Responsability" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                recContactJobResponsability_L.INIT;
                recContactJobResponsability_L."Contact No." := recContact_L."No.";
                recContactJobResponsability_L."Job Responsibility Code" := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Job Responsability";
                recContactJobResponsability_L.INSERT;
              END;
            END;
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), TextModify, CompanyName_P);
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncInsertItem@8053766(RecRef_P@1000000002 : RecordRef;CompanyCode_P@8053601 : Code[10];CompanyName_P@8053605 : Text[30]);
    VAR
      FieldRef_L@8053608 : FieldRef;
      VariantNo_L@8053611 : Code[10];
      ItemNo_L@8053610 : Code[20];
      RecFamily_L@1000000000 : Record 8052986;
      RecSubFamily_L@1000000001 : Record 8052987;
      RecSpecies_L@1000000003 : Record 8052988;
      RecItem_L@1000000004 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lors de l'insertion d'un article
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - CompanyCode_P : No de la soci't' dans laquelle l'enregistrement est cr''
      //    - CompanyName_P : nom de la soci't'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InterfaceIsActiveOnCompany(CompanyName_P) THEN BEGIN
        CASE RecRef_P.NUMBER OF
          DATABASE::Item: BEGIN
            //Synchronisation des Articles
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.

            // --> 20180618-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32182-BSI
            RecItem_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
            RecItem_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
            RecFamily_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
            IF RecFamily_L.GET(RecItem_L."Family Code") THEN
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Family, RecFamily_L.Code, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            RecSubFamily_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
            IF RecSubFamily_L.GET(RecItem_L."Sub-Family Code") THEN
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::SubFamily, RecSubFamily_L.Code, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            RecSpecies_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
            IF RecSpecies_L.GET(RecItem_L."Species Code") THEN
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Specie, RecSpecies_L.Code, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            // <-- 20180618-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32182-BSI
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE), TextModify, CompanyName_P);
          END;
          DATABASE::"Item Variant": BEGIN
            //Synchronisation des Articles
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
            VariantNo_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Item No.
            ItemNo_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            // --> 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, ItemNo_L + GetItemSeparator() + VariantNo_L, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            //AFTER
            IF ItemNo_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              // --> 20180607-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32182-BSI
              // --> 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              RecItem_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
              // <-- 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              RecItem_L.GET(ItemNo_L);
              // --> 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              RecFamily_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
              // <-- 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              IF RecFamily_L.GET(RecItem_L."Family Code") THEN
                AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Family, RecFamily_L.Code, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
              // --> 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              RecSubFamily_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
              // <-- 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              IF RecSubFamily_L.GET(RecItem_L."Sub-Family Code") THEN
                AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::SubFamily, RecSubFamily_L.Code, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
              // --> 20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              RecSpecies_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyCode_P);
              // <--20190131-WI00-ADV01.01.00-40283-CJY
              IF RecSpecies_L.GET(RecItem_L."Species Code") THEN
                AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Specie, RecSpecies_L.Code, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
              // <-- 20180607-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32182-BSI
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, ItemNo_L, TextModify, CompanyName_P);
            END;
            // <-- 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncModify@1000000004(RecRef_P@1000000002 : RecordRef;xRecRef_P@1000000001 : RecordRef);
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lors de la modification d'un enregistrement d'une table faisant l'objet d'un log,
      // cette fonction est appel'e pour r'aliser la synchro avec le CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - xRecRef_P : Ancien enregistrement
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InterfaceIsActive() THEN
        SyncFromTrigger(RecRef_P, xRecRef_P, TextModify);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncFromTrigger@8053717(RecRef_P@8053601 : RecordRef;xRecRef_P@8053600 : RecordRef;Action_P@8053602 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recCustomer_L@8053616 : Record 18;
      FieldRef_L@8053615 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef2_L@8053000 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef3_L@8053002 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef4_L@8053001 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef5_L@8053008 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef6_L@8053007 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef7_L@8053006 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef8_L@8053005 : FieldRef;
      FieldRef9_L@1000000001 : FieldRef;
      CustomerCode_L@8053614 : Code[20];
      AddressCode_L@8053613 : Code[10];
      RecRefCust_L@8053612 : RecordRef;
      OrderType_L@8053611 : Integer;
      OrderNo_L@8053610 : Code[20];
      OrderStatus_L@8053609 : Integer;
      recSalesHeader_L@8053608 : Record 36;
      RecRefOrderHeader_L@8053607 : RecordRef;
      xRecRefOrderHeader_L@8053606 : RecordRef;
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053605 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053604 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@8053603 : Record 8053167;
      xrecUnloadingMethod_L@8053619 : Record 8053168;
      xrecUnloadingVehSize_L@8053618 : Record 8053166;
      xrecUnloadingTime_L@8053617 : Record 8053167;
      recContactRelation_L@8053620 : Record 5054;
      CodeCompany_L@8053621 : Code[10];
      VariantNo_L@8053622 : Code[10];
      ItemNo_L@8053623 : Code[20];
      recGuid_L@8053624 : Record 8053601;
      Activity_L@8053625 : Code[10];
      Doc1_L@8053626 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053627 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053628 : Text[30];
      Doc4_L@8053629 : Text[30];
      Type1_L@8053630 : Integer;
      Type2_L@8053631 : Integer;
      Date1_L@8053632 : Date;
      Date2_L@8053633 : Date;
      TxtDate_L@8053634 : Text[8];
      recDetailLigneContrat_L@8053635 : Record 8053210;
      recLigneContrat_L@8053636 : Record 8053209;
      recGuid2_L@8053003 : Record 8053609;
      recSalesPriceTmp_L@8053637 : TEMPORARY Record 7002;
      recPostedReceiptTmp_L@8053638 : TEMPORARY Record 8053104;
      xrecPostedReceiptTmp_L@8053639 : TEMPORARY Record 8053104;
      recReceiptTmp_L@8053641 : TEMPORARY Record 8053103;
      xrecReceiptTmp_L@8053640 : TEMPORARY Record 8053103;
      recType_L@8053004 : Record 8053324;
      recCampaign_L@8053009 : Record 8052991;
      recLigneModele_L@8053010 : Record 8053235;
      recItem_L@8053011 : Record 27;
      recVarSetup_L@8053012 : Record 8053090;
      Doc5_L@1000000000 : Text[30];
      RecLigneAssolement_L@1000000002 : Record 8053195;
      RecCRMSetup_L@1000000003 : Record 8053602;
      RecContact_L@1000000004 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // gSre les actions appel'es psar les triggers OnGlobal...
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : Nouvel enregistrement
      //    - xRecRef_P : Ancien enregistrement
      //    - Action_P : D'fini l'action : MODIFY, DELETE ou RENAME
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CASE RecRef_P.NUMBER OF
        DATABASE::"CMC Campaign": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des campagnes
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien No.
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Campaign, FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Campaign, FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Campaign, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, '');
          //Synchronisation de toutes les lignes de modSles sur cette campagne
          recLigneModele_L.RESET;
          IF recLigneModele_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
            REPEAT
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::ModeleContratCadreAppro, recLigneModele_L."Frame Contract Type"
               + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recLigneModele_L."Line No.") +
                GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, '');
            UNTIL recLigneModele_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        DATABASE::Contact: BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des contacts
          //Gestion du renommage

          IF NOT RecCRMSetup_L.GET THEN
            CLEAR(RecCRMSetup_L);

          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien No.
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Account, FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Account, FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            END;
          END;

          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);

          CASE recCRMSetup_G."Deployment Type" OF
            recCRMSetup_G."Deployment Type"::"On-premise" : BEGIN
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, '');
            END;
            recCRMSetup_G."Deployment Type"::"On-line" : BEGIN
              // --> 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27147-BSI01
              IF NOT RecContact_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN
                CLEAR(RecContact_L);
              // <-- 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27147-BSI01

          IF RecContact_L.Type = RecContact_L.Type::Company THEN
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, '')
          ELSE IF RecContact_L.Type = RecContact_L.Type::Person THEN
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Contact, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, '');

            END;
          END;

          FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(8053176); //Contact CRM
          FieldRef3_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(8053176); //Ancien Contact CRM
          {
          IF (FORMAT(FieldRef2_L.VALUE) = FORMAT(TRUE)) AND (FieldRef2_L.VALUE <> FieldRef3_L.VALUE) THEN
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::History, FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, '');
          } // To add D365 ?
        END;

        DATABASE::Customer: BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des clients
          CodeCompany_L := GetCompanyMcCode();
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
          recContactRelation_L.RESET;
          recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
          recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CodeCompany_L);
          recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
          IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recContactRelation_L."Contact No.", Action_P, '');
        END;
        DATABASE::Vendor: BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des fournisseurs
          CodeCompany_L := GetCompanyMcCode();
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
          recContactRelation_L.RESET;
          recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
          recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CodeCompany_L);
          recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
          IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recContactRelation_L."Contact No.", Action_P, '');
        END;
        DATABASE::"Ship-to Address": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des adresses de livraison
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
            recCustomer_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Code
            AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Address Ship-to", recCustomer_L."No." + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L) THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
              recCustomer_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Code
              AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Address Ship-to", recCustomer_L."No." + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L);
            END;
          END;

          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
          IF FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE) <> '' THEN BEGIN
            recCustomer_L.GET(FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
            FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Code
            AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Ship-to Address", recCustomer_L."No." + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L, Action_P,
              COMPANYNAME);
          END;
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Unloading Place": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des lieux de d'chargement
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
            CustomerCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE);
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Address Code
            AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(3); //Code
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Place", CustomerCode_L + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L +
              GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L))
            THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
              CustomerCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE);
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Address Code
              AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(3); //Code
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Place", CustomerCode_L + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L +
                GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            END;
          END;

          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Customer No.
          CustomerCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Address Code
          AddressCode_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(3); //Code
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Place", CustomerCode_L + GetSeparator() + AddressCode_L +
            GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L), Action_P, COMPANYNAME);
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Unloading Method": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des m'thodes de d'chargement
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Method", FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE), Action_P, COMPANYNAME);
        END;

              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code

              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code

            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Time", FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE), Action_P, COMPANYNAME);
        END;
        DATABASE::Item: BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des Articles

              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef_L));

          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE), Action_P, '');
        END;
        // --> 20150608-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3047-FHS01
        DATABASE::"CMC Variant Entry Setup": BEGIN

            recItem_L.RESET;
            CASE recVarSetup_L.Type OF
              recVarSetup_L.Type::Activity: BEGIN
                recItem_L.SETRANGE("Activity Code", recVarSetup_L."No.");

                recItem_L.SETRANGE("Family Code", recVarSetup_L."No.");

              recVarSetup_L.Type::"Sub-family": BEGIN

              UNTIL recItem_L.NEXT = 0;

          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Type
          FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //No.
          recVarSetup_L.Type  := FieldRef_L.VALUE;
          recVarSetup_L."No." := FORMAT(FieldRef2_L.VALUE);
          recItem_L.RESET;
          CASE recVarSetup_L.Type OF
            recVarSetup_L.Type::Activity: BEGIN

            END;
            recVarSetup_L.Type::Family: BEGIN
              recItem_L.SETRANGE("Family Code", recVarSetup_L."No.");
            END;
            recVarSetup_L.Type::"Sub-family": BEGIN
              recItem_L.SETRANGE("Sub-Family Code", recVarSetup_L."No.");
            END;
            recVarSetup_L.Type::species: BEGIN
              recItem_L.SETRANGE("Species Code", recVarSetup_L."No.");
            END;
            recVarSetup_L.Type::Item: BEGIN
              recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", recVarSetup_L."No.");
            END;
          END;
          IF recItem_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
            REPEAT
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, recItem_L."No.", TextModify, '');
            UNTIL recItem_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
        // <-- 20150608-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3047-FHS01
        DATABASE::"Item Variant": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des Articles
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien No.
            FieldRef2_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Ancien Item No.
            // --> 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetItemSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN BEGIN
            //AFTER
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef2_L)) THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
              FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Item No.
              // --> 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
              //BEFORE
              //recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetItemSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
              //AFTER
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, FORMAT(FieldRef2_L));
              // <-- 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //No.
          VariantNo_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Item No.
          ItemNo_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          // --> 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, ItemNo_L + GetItemSeparator() + VariantNo_L, Action_P, '');
          //AFTER
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Item, ItemNo_L, Action_P, '');
          // <-- 20150401-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2880-FHS01
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Activity": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des activit's, familles, sous-familles et espSces
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien Code
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Activity, FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Activity, FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
          Activity_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Activity, Activity_L, Action_P, '');
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Family": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des familles, sous-familles et espSces
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien Code
            IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Family, FORMAT(FieldRef_L)) THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
              recGuid_L.RENAME(recGuid_L."Type Data"::Family, FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code

        DATABASE::"CMC Sub-Family": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des sous-familles et espSces

        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Species": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des espSces

        DATABASE::"CMC Sales Quota": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des quotas
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien Campaign No.
            FieldRef2_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Ancien Type
            FieldRef3_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(3); //Ancien No.
            FieldRef4_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(4); //Ancien Production Activity Code
            FieldRef5_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(5); //Ancien Sales Type
            FieldRef6_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(6); //Ancien Sales No.
            FieldRef7_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(7); //Ancien Starting Date
            Date1_L := FieldRef7_L.VALUE;
            FieldRef8_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(8); //Ancien Ending Date
            Date2_L := FieldRef8_L.VALUE;

            // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
            FieldRef9_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(60000); //Code Categorie Doc.
            Doc5_L := FieldRef9_L.VALUE;
            IF (Doc5_L = recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales") OR (Doc5_L = '') THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
              IF recGuid2_L.GET(recGuid2_L."Type Data"::Quota,
                FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetSeparator() +
                FORMAT(FieldRef3_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef4_L) + GetSeparator() +
                FORMAT(FieldRef5_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef6_L) + GetSeparator() +
                FORMAT(Date1_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>') + GetSeparator() +
                FORMAT(Date2_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'))
              THEN BEGIN
                FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
                FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Type
                FieldRef3_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(3); //No.
                FieldRef4_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(4); //Production Activity Code
                FieldRef5_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(5); //Sales Type
                FieldRef6_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(6); //Sales No.
                FieldRef7_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(7); //Starting Date
                Date1_L := FieldRef7_L.VALUE;
                FieldRef8_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(8); //Ending Date
                Date2_L := FieldRef8_L.VALUE;
                // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
                FieldRef9_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(60000); //Code Categorie Doc.
                Doc5_L := FieldRef9_L.VALUE;
                IF (Doc5_L = recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales") OR (Doc5_L = '') THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
                  recGuid2_L.RENAME(recGuid2_L."Type Data"::Quota,
                    FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(FieldRef3_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef4_L) + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(FieldRef5_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef6_L) + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(Date1_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>') + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(Date2_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'));
                END ELSE
                  AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Quota,
                    FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(FieldRef3_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef4_L) + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(FieldRef5_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef6_L) + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(Date1_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>') + GetSeparator() +
                    FORMAT(Date2_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'),
                    TextDelete, '');
              END;
            // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
            END ELSE Action_P := TextModify;
            // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Campaign No.
          Doc1_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Type
          Type1_L := FieldRef_L.VALUE;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(3); //No.
          Doc2_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(4); //Production Activity Code
          Doc3_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(5); //Sales Type
          Type2_L := FieldRef_L.VALUE;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(6); //Sales No.
          Doc4_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(7); //Starting Date
          Date1_L := FieldRef_L.VALUE;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(8); //Ending Date
          Date2_L := FieldRef_L.VALUE;
          // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(60000); //Code Categorie Doc.
          Doc5_L := FieldRef_L.VALUE;
          IF (Doc5_L = recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales") OR (Doc5_L = '') THEN
          // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Quota, Doc1_L + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(Type1_L) + GetSeparator() +
              Doc2_L + GetSeparator() + Doc3_L + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(Type2_L) + GetSeparator() + Doc4_L + GetSeparator() +
              FORMAT(Date1_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>') + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(Date2_L, 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'),
              Action_P, '');
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Analysis Report": BEGIN

        // --> 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
        DATABASE::"CMC Crop Rotation": BEGIN  //1
          //Synchronisation des 'l'ments de campagne

          END;

          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Code
          FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2);
          Doc1_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef2_L);
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::ElementCampaign, Doc1_L, Action_P, '');
          //pr'voir la suppression

          // Insertion des lignes assolement
          CLEAR(RecLigneAssolement_L);
          RecLigneAssolement_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.",FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
          RecLigneAssolement_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.",FORMAT(FieldRef2_L));
          IF RecLigneAssolement_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::DetailsElementCampaign, RecLigneAssolement_L."Contact No." + GetSeparator() +
                            RecLigneAssolement_L."Campaign No." + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(RecLigneAssolement_L."Line No."), Action_P, ''
      );
          UNTIL RecLigneAssolement_L.NEXT =0;
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Crop Rotation Line": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des d'tails 'l'ments de campagne
          //Gestion du renommage

          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien Ccontact
            FieldRef2_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Ancien campagne

            IF recGuid2_L.GET(recGuid2_L."Type Data"::elementcampaigndetail, FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() +

                + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef4_L));

          FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Campagne
          FieldRef4_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(4); //Ligne

                          FORMAT(FieldRef2_L) + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(FieldRef4_L), Action_P, '');
          {before
          CLEAR(RecLigneAssolement_L);
          RecLigneAssolement_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.",FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
          RecLigneAssolement_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.",FORMAT(FieldRef2_L));

          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien Campagne
            FieldRef2_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Ancien Code
            IF recGuid2_L.GET(recGuid2_L."Type Data"::collectagreementfunnelfilter, FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() +
              FORMAT(FieldRef2_L))
            THEN BEGIN
              FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Campagne
              FieldRef2_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Code
              recGuid2_L.RENAME(recGuid2_L."Type Data"::collectagreementfunnelfilter, FORMAT(FieldRef_L) + GetSeparator() +
                FORMAT(FieldRef2_L));
            END;
          END;
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Campaign
          Doc1_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Code
          Doc2_L := FORMAT(FieldRef_L);
          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::EntonnoirContratCollecte, Doc1_L + GetSeparator() + Doc2_L, Action_P, '');
        END;
        DATABASE::"CMC Commitment Contract": BEGIN
          //Synchronisation des engagements contrat cadre collecte
          //Gestion du renommage
          IF Action_P = TextRename THEN BEGIN
            FieldRef_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(1); //Ancien Campaign No.
            FieldRef2_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(2); //Ancien Item Funnel Code
            FieldRef3_L := xRecRef_P.FIELD(3); //Ancien Contract Type

      ;

              recPostedReceiptTmp_L."Contract No.")
            THEN BEGIN
              recDetailLigneContrat_L.RESET;
              recDetailLigneContrat_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Collection Contract No.");

          RecRef_P.SETTABLE(recReceiptTmp_L);
          xRecRef_P.SETTABLE(xrecReceiptTmp_L);
          IF recReceiptTmp_L."Contract Type" = recReceiptTmp_L."Contract Type"::Collecte THEN BEGIN

            IF (xrecReceiptTmp_L."Contract No." <> '') AND (xrecReceiptTmp_L."Contract No." <>
              recReceiptTmp_L."Contract No.")
            THEN BEGIN

              recDetailLigneContrat_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Collection Contract No.");
              recDetailLigneContrat_L.SETRANGE("Collection Contract No.", xrecReceiptTmp_L."Contract No.");

                  AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::DetailLigneContratCadreCollect, recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract No." +

        DATABASE::"Post Code": BEGIN
          RecCRMSetup_L.GET;
