    PROCEDURE DeleteEntityFromNav@8053636(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50];TypeEntity_P@8053602 : Integer;StatusCode_P@8053604 : Integer;TypeGuid_P@8053605 : Integer);
    VAR
      recGuid_L@8053603 : Record 8053601;
      recGuid2_L@8053606 : Record 8053609;
      Delete_L@8053000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gestion de la suppression d'une entit' dans CRM suite . une action dans Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : entit' de l'enregistrement
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'enregistrement
      //    - TypeEntity_P : Type de l'entit' . supprimer. Cette donn'e vient de la table de gestion des GUID
      //    - StatusCode_P : Le statut pour d'sactiver l'enregistrement
      //    - TypeGuid_P : Permet de choisir dans qui liste de GUID on supprime la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF ExistRecord(EntityName_P, Guid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Delete_L := FALSE;
        CASE EntityName_P OF
          'cmc_collectmasteragreement','cmc_productquota',
          // --> 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
          'cmc_saleshistory':
          // <-- 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01

            Delete_L := TRUE;
        END;
        IF Delete_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
          //si la donn'e existe dans le CRM, on le d'sactive et le d'synchronise
          IF SetState(EntityName_P, Guid_P, StatusCode_P) THEN BEGIN //Inactive
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          DeleteRecord(EntityName_P, Guid_P);
        END;
      END;
      //on va supprimer la valeur de la liste des GUID
      CASE TypeGuid_P OF
        1: BEGIN
          recGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
          recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", TypeEntity_P);
          recGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, Guid_P);

          IF recGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recGuid_L.DELETE(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
        2: BEGIN
          recGuid2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
          recGuid2_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", TypeEntity_P);
          recGuid2_L.SETRANGE(GUID, Guid_P);

          IF recGuid2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recGuid2_L.DELETE(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddErrorToSimpleOrder@8053685(Error_P@8053600 : Text[250];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Error_L@8053603 : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gestion des messages d'erreur sur la commande simplifi'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Error_P : Message d'erreur
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de la commande simplifi'e
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'salesorder';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationerrormessage', '');
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, Guid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Error_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationerrormessage');

        IF Error_L <> Error_P THEN BEGIN
          SetState(EntityName_L, Guid_P, 2); //En attente
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationerrormessage', Error_P);
          IF NOT(UpdateRecordWithoutError(EntityName_L, Guid_P)) THEN BEGIN
          END;
          SetState(EntityName_L, Guid_P, 100001); //Termin'
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<--Partie2 contrat-->"@8053722();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncElementCampagne@8053732(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053618 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053617 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Contact_L@8053604 : Text[54];
      Campaign_L@8053603 : Text[54];
      TranscriptionPattern_L@8053605 : Text[110];
      Modify_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      recElementCampagne_L@8053608 : Record 8053194;
      recAssolement_L@8053609 : Record 8053360;
      Assolement_L@8053606 : Code[10];
      Status_L@8053610 : Code[20];
      recTemp_L@8053611 : TEMPORARY Record 8053610;
      recContractCamp_L@1000000000 : Record 8053324;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation de l''l'ment de campagne dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquelle cr'er la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_elementcampaign';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpattern_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'statuscode', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_displaytype', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        //On ne gSre que les PV
        IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_displaytype') <> '175490000' THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
          EXIT(Result_L);
        END;

        //On ne gSre que les PV
        IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '175490001' THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
          EXIT(Result_L);
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id'); //contact
        Contact_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Contact_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recElementCampagne_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contact No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id'); //Campagne
        Campaign_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Campaign_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recElementCampagne_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpattern_id'); //transcription pattern
        TranscriptionPattern_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053667, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(TranscriptionPattern_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recElementCampagne_L.FIELDCAPTION("Crop Rotation Table Code"));

        Status_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode');

        EntityName_L := 'cmc_transcriptionpattern';
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpatterntype', '');
        IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpatterntype') <> '175490000' THEN BEGIN
            Result_L := TRUE;
            EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;

        IF (Contact_L = '') OR (Campaign_L = '') THEN BEGIN
          EXIT(FALSE);
        END;

        //l''l'ment de campagne existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF recElementCampagne_L.GET(Contact_L, Campaign_L) THEN BEGIN
          Modify_L := TRUE;
        END;

        //Cr'ation de l''lement de campagne
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recElementCampagne_L.INIT;
          recElementCampagne_L."Contact No." := Contact_L;
          recElementCampagne_L."Campaign No." := Campaign_L;
        END;
        recElementCampagne_L."Crop Rotation Table Code" := TranscriptionPattern_L;

        CASE Status_L OF
          '1': BEGIN
            recElementCampagne_L.Status  := recElementCampagne_L.Status::Open;
            recElementCampagne_L.Freezed := FALSE;
          END;
          '175490001': BEGIN
            recElementCampagne_L.Status := recElementCampagne_L.Status::Released;
            recElementCampagne_L.Freezed := FALSE;
          END;
          '2': BEGIN
            recElementCampagne_L.Freezed := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recElementCampagne_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recElementCampagne_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053659, recElementCampagne_L."Contact No." + GetSeparator() +
          recElementCampagne_L."Campaign No.", CrmGuid_P);

        //rechercher les lignes associ'es pour les ajouter . l'importation
        recTemp_L.DELETEALL;
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_elementcampaigndetail';
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaigndetailid', '');
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaign_id', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(CrmGuid_P));
        IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            recTemp_L.INIT;
            recTemp_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaigndetailid'));
            recTemp_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;
        recTemp_L.RESET;
        IF recTemp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            SyncDetailsElementCampagne(recTemp_L."CRM Guid", recCrmOrganisation_P);
          UNTIL recTemp_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
        // --> 20161006-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22265-BSI01
        recElementCampagne_L.ActionFromCrm(TRUE);
        // <-- 20161006-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22265-BSI01
        // --> 20160525-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2980-BSI01
        recElementCampagne_L.Reopen;
        recElementCampagne_L.Release;
        // <-- 20160525-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2980-BSI01

        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncDetailsElementCampagne@8053739(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053618 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053617 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Contact_L@8053604 : Text[54];
      Campaign_L@8053603 : Text[54];
      TranscriptionPattern_L@8053605 : Text[110];
      ElementCampagne_L@8053606 : Text[54];
      Modify_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      recDetailsElementCampagne_L@8053608 : Record 8053195;
      recElementCampagne_L@8053619 : Record 8053194;
      DetailsElementCampagne_L@8053609 : Text[110];
      Doc1_L@8053615 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053613 : Text[30];
      LineNo_L@8053610 : Integer;
      Code_L@8053611 : Text[250];
      ArticleTranscription_L@8053612 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@8053614 : Integer;
      Doc3_L@8053620 : Text[30];
      Separator_L@8053621 : Text[4];
      Doc4_L@8053622 : Text[30];
      Doc5_L@8053623 : Text[30];
      recArticleTrans_L@8053624 : Record 8053361;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation de l''l'ment de campagne dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquelle cr'er la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_elementcampaigndetail';
      Separator_L := GetSeparator();

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaign_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_articletranscript_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_autoconsumedarea', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectpotential', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_donotexecutecalcul', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_forceupdate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unit', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        DetailsElementCampagne_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053657, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaign_id'); //El'ment de campagne
        ElementCampagne_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053659, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(ElementCampagne_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recElementCampagne_L.TABLECAPTION);

        Code_L := ElementCampagne_L;
        Doc4_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Doc5_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        recElementCampagne_L.GET(Doc4_L, Doc5_L);

        IF DetailsElementCampagne_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          Code_L := DetailsElementCampagne_L;
          Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
          Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
          Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
          LineNo_L := FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_articletranscript_id'); //Article transcription
        ArticleTranscription_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053668, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(ArticleTranscription_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recDetailsElementCampagne_L.FIELDCAPTION("Crop Rotation Table Code"));

        Code_L := ArticleTranscription_L;
        Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
        recArticleTrans_L.GET(Doc3_L, Int1_L);

        //l''l'ment de campagne existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF DetailsElementCampagne_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recDetailsElementCampagne_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, LineNo_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du d'tail de l''lement de campagne
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          EVALUATE(LineNo_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber'));
          IF recDetailsElementCampagne_L.GET(recElementCampagne_L."Contact No.", recElementCampagne_L."Campaign No.", LineNo_L) THEN
      BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L.INIT;
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Contact No." := recElementCampagne_L."Contact No.";
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Campaign No." := recElementCampagne_L."Campaign No.";
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
          END;
        END;
        CASE recArticleTrans_L."Line Type" OF
          recArticleTrans_L."Line Type"::"Funnel Filter": BEGIN
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line Type" := recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line Type"::"Funnel Filter";
          END;
          recArticleTrans_L."Line Type"::Formula: BEGIN
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line Type" := recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line Type"::Formula;
          END;
          recArticleTrans_L."Line Type"::Title: BEGIN
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line Type" := recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line Type"::Title;
          END;
        END;
        recDetailsElementCampagne_L.Range := recArticleTrans_L.Range;

        recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Row Ref. No." := recArticleTrans_L."Row Ref. No.";
        recDetailsElementCampagne_L.Description := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name');
        recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value'));

        recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Crop Rotation Table Code" := Doc3_L;

        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053657, recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Contact No." + Separator_L +
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Campaign No." + Separator_L +
          FORMAT(recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Line No."), CrmGuid_P);

        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncContratCadreAppro@8053734(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recContratCadre_L@8053606 : Record 8053239;
      ElementCampaign_L@8053607 : Text[54];
      Contact_L@8053612 : Text[54];
      Campaign_L@8053611 : Text[54];
      Modify_L@8053608 : Boolean;
      Commitment_L@8053609 : Text[54];
      SupplyAgreement_L@8053610 : Text[54];
      MasterAgreementType_L@8053613 : Text[54];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      ContratCadreAppro_L@8053616 : Text[20];
      Doc1_L@8053615 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053617 : Text[30];
      CodeErp_L@8053602 : Text[30];
      recContractType_L@8053618 : Record 8053198;
      IsAliment_L@8053619 : Boolean;
      IsCollect_L@8053000 : Boolean;
      recLigneContratCadre_L@8053620 : Record 8053240;
      ModifyLine_L@8053621 : Boolean;
      recAbaque_L@8053630 : Record 8053237;
      Abaque_L@8053629 : Text[110];
      Funnel_L@8053628 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053626 : Text[110];
      Doc3_L@8053625 : Text[30];
      Doc4_L@8053624 : Text[30];
      Doc5_L@8053623 : Text[30];
      NewLine_L@8053622 : Boolean;
      recTemp_L@8053627 : TEMPORARY Record 8053610;
      Template_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
      recTemplate_L@1000000001 : Record 8053235;
      Doc6_L@1000000003 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@1000000002 : Integer;
      cduCreateBuy_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8053063;
      IsTAF_L@8053002 : Boolean;
      OnlyOneRemere_L@8053003 : Boolean;
      Salesperson_L@8053004 : Code[10];
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053005 : Record 8053608;
      recManageContract_L@8053006 : Codeunit 8053063;
      recCampaign_L@8053007 : Record 8052991;
      cduCVMan_L@8053008 : Codeunit 8053377;
      recElementCampagne_L@1000000004 : Record 8053194;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation du contrat cadre appro dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquelle cr'er la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_supplyagreement';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitmentid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaignid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_designation', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpcontractnumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'statuscode', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_abacustype', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_datesignature', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_signataire', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementtemplateid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tonnage', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_comment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'ownerid', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '175490000' THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
          EXIT(Result_L);
        END;

        // --> 20170808-WI00-ADV01.01.00-28632-BSI01
        IF GetRecord('cmc_masteragreementtype',GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid')) THEN BEGIN
          IF FormatBoolFromCrm(GetAttribute('cmc_masteragreementtype', 'cmc_notsynchronised')) = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            Result_L := TRUE;
            EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;
        // <-- 20170808-WI00-ADV01.01.00-28632-BSI01

        ContratCadreAppro_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053655, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);

        CodeErp_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpcontractnumber'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(CodeErp_L));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid'); //contact
        Contact_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Contact_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contact No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid'); //Campagne
        Campaign_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Campaign_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid');
        MasterAgreementType_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053670, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(MasterAgreementType_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract Type"));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitmentid');
        Commitment_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053660, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Commitment_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Commitment Code"));

        IsAliment_L := FALSE;
        IsCollect_L := FALSE;
        IsTAF_L := FALSE;
        OnlyOneRemere_L := FALSE;
        GetSetup();
        IF recContractType_L.GET(MasterAgreementType_L) THEN BEGIN
          CASE recContractType_L.Type OF
            recContractType_L.Type::Bovin: BEGIN
              Doc2_L := recCRMSetup_G."Def Commitment Bovin";
              IsAliment_L := TRUE;
              NewLine_L := TRUE;
            END;
            recContractType_L.Type::Remere: BEGIN
              Doc2_L := recCRMSetup_G."Def Commitment Remere";
              IsAliment_L := TRUE;
              NewLine_L := FALSE;
              IF recContractType_L."One Contract per Campaign" = TRUE THEN
                OnlyOneRemere_L := TRUE;
            END;
            recContractType_L.Type::TAF: BEGIN
              //Recherche du GUID du vendeur
              Salesperson_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetSalespersonCode(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053631,
                GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ownerid'), FALSE));
              Doc2_L := recCRMSetup_G."Def Commitment TAF";
              IsAliment_L := TRUE;
              NewLine_L := TRUE;
              IsTAF_L := TRUE;
            END;
            recContractType_L.Type::Collect: BEGIN
              //Recherche du GUID du vendeur
              Salesperson_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetSalespersonCode(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053631,
                GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ownerid'), FALSE));
              Doc2_L := recCRMSetup_G."Def Commitment Collect";
              NewLine_L := TRUE;
              IsCollect_L := TRUE;
              recCampaign_L.GET(Campaign_L);
              recCampaign_L.RESET;
              recCampaign_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code","Starting Date");
              recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '>%1', recCampaign_L."Ending Date");
              recCampaign_L.FINDFIRST;
              Campaign_L := recCampaign_L."No.";
            END;
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF Commitment_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Commitment_L, 1, STRPOS(Commitment_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
                Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Commitment_L, STRPOS(Commitment_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Commitment_L));
              END;
            END;
          END;
        END;

        //le contrat cadre appro existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF ContratCadreAppro_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recContratCadre_L.GET(ContratCadreAppro_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du contrat cadre appro
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF recContratCadre_L.GET(CodeErp_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Si contrat r'm'r', il n'y a qu'un contrat par agriculteur par campagne
            IF OnlyOneRemere_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              // --> 20150506-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2976-FHS01
              recContractType_L.RESET;
              recContractType_L.SETRANGE(Type, recContractType_L.Type::Remere);
              IF recContractType_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
              // <-- 20150506-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2976-FHS01
                  recContratCadre_L.RESET;
                  recContratCadre_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", Campaign_L);
                  recContratCadre_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Contact_L);
                  // --> 20150506-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2976-FHS01
                  recContratCadre_L.SETRANGE("Frame Contract Type", recContractType_L.Code);
                  // <-- 20150506-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2976-FHS01
                  IF NOT recContratCadre_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                    EXIT;
              // --> 20150506-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2976-FHS01
                UNTIL recContractType_L.NEXT = 0;
              // <-- 20150506-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2976-FHS01
            END;
            recContratCadre_L.RESET;
            recContratCadre_L.INIT;
            recContratCadre_L."No." := COPYSTR(CodeErp_L, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recContratCadre_L."No."));
          END;
        END;
        recContratCadre_L.Description := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_designation'), 1,
          MAXSTRLEN(recContratCadre_L.Description));

        // --> 20160428-Gen23-ADV01.01.00-2952-OAN01
        // BEFORE
        {recContratCadre_L."Frame Contract Type" := MasterAgreementType_L;}
        // AFTER
        recContratCadre_L.VALIDATE("Frame Contract Type",MasterAgreementType_L);
        // <-- 20160428-Gen23-ADV01.01.00-2952-OAN01

        recContratCadre_L."Campaign No." := Campaign_L;
        recContratCadre_L."Commitment Code" := Doc2_L;
        recContratCadre_L."Contact No." := Contact_L;
        // --> 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21879-BSI01
        //recContratCadre_L."Creation Date" := WORKDATE;
        // <-- 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21879-BSI01
        recContratCadre_L."Last Modification Date" := WORKDATE;

        IF IsTAF_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          recContratCadre_L."Total Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tonnage'));
        END;

        recContratCadre_L.SystemCreated(TRUE);
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21879-BSI01
          recContratCadre_L."Creation Date" := WORKDATE;
          // <-- 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21879-BSI01
          recContratCadre_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recContratCadre_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053655, recContratCadre_L."No.", CrmGuid_P);

        IF (IsAliment_L = TRUE) OR (IsCollect_L = TRUE) THEN BEGIN
          IF NewLine_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_abacustype');
            Abaque_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053669, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
            GuidFinded(Abaque_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Rate Code"));
            IF Abaque_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              Code_L := Abaque_L;
              Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
              Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
              Doc4_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
              Doc5_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
            END;

            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid');
            Funnel_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053673, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ISNULLGUID(FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                Funnel_L := '';
              END ELSE BEGIN
                GuidFinded(Funnel_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));
              END;
            END;

            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementtemplateid');
            Template_L := COPYSTR(GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053666, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Template_L));
            GuidFinded(Template_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION(Description));
            IF Template_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              Doc6_L := COPYSTR(Template_L, 1, STRPOS(Template_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
              Template_L := COPYSTR(Template_L, STRPOS(Template_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Template_L));
              EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(Template_L, 1, STRPOS(Template_L, GetSeparator()) - 1));
              recTemplate_L.GET(Doc6_L, Int1_L);
            END;

            //la ligne du contrat cadre appro existe-elle ?
            ModifyLine_L := FALSE;
            recLigneContratCadre_L.RESET;
            recLigneContratCadre_L.SETRANGE("Frame Contract No.", recContratCadre_L."No.");
            IF recLigneContratCadre_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              ModifyLine_L := TRUE;
            END;

            //Cr'ation du contrat cadre appro
            IF ModifyLine_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L.INIT;
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Frame Contract No." := recContratCadre_L."No.";
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Line No." := 10;
            END;

            recLigneContratCadre_L."Campaign No." := recContratCadre_L."Campaign No.";
            recLigneContratCadre_L."Item Funnel Code" := Funnel_L;

            IF (IsCollect_L = FALSE) AND (IsTAF_L = FALSE) THEN
              recLigneContratCadre_L.Commitment := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tonnage'))
            ELSE BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tonnage'));
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Item Funnel Code" := recTemplate_L."Item Funnel Code";
            END;

            IF recAbaque_L.GET(Doc3_L, Doc4_L, Doc5_L) THEN BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Rate Code" := recAbaque_L.Code;
            END;

            // --> 20170927-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01
            IF Doc5_L = '' THEN
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Rate Code" := '';
            // <-- 20170927-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01

            IF Template_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L.Description := recTemplate_L.Description;
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Limit Qty for Item Disc." := recTemplate_L."Limit Qty for Item Disc.";
            END;

            // --> 20150618-FINA13-CMC02.02.00-2965-DRN01
            //BEFORE
            // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2869-FHS01
            //recLigneContratCadre_L."Customer No." := cduCVMan_L.GetCustVentNoFromContNo(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.", 0);
            // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2869-FHS01
            //AFTER
            recLigneContratCadre_L."Customer No." := cduCVMan_L.GetCustVendBankFromContact(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.", 0);
            // <-- 20150618-FINA13-CMC02.02.00-2965-DRN01

            recLigneContratCadre_L.Comment := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_comment');

            recLigneContratCadre_L.SystemCreated(TRUE);
            IF ModifyLine_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
            END;

            IF (IsCollect_L = TRUE) OR (IsTAF_L = TRUE) THEN BEGIN
              //Cr'ation du contrat collecte et v'rouillage du contrat
              recManageContract_L.SetSalesperson(Salesperson_L);
              recManageContract_L.RUN(recContratCadre_L);
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //rechercher les lignes associ'es pour les ajouter . l'importation
          recTemp_L.DELETEALL;
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_supplyagreementline';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementlineid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementid', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(CrmGuid_P));
          IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              recTemp_L.INIT;
              recTemp_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementlineid'));
              recTemp_L.INSERT;
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
          recTemp_L.RESET;
          IF recTemp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2869-FHS01
              //BEFORE
              //SyncLigneContratCadreAppro(recTemp_L."CRM Guid", recCrmOrganisation_P);
              //AFTER
              SyncLigneContratCadreAppro(recTemp_L."CRM Guid", recCrmOrganisation_P, recContratCadre_L);
              // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2869-FHS01
            UNTIL recTemp_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

        recContratCadre_L.Status := recContratCadre_L.Status::Released;
        recContratCadre_L.MODIFY;

        IF CodeErp_L <> recContratCadre_L."No." THEN BEGIN
          //Envoyer le num'ro ERP
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_supplyagreement';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpcontractnumber', recContratCadre_L."No.");
          IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
          END;
        END;

        IF IsCollect_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          //Cr'ation du contrat d'achat collecte
          COMMIT;
          IF cduCreateBuy_L.RUN(recContratCadre_L) THEN ;
        END;

        Result_L := TRUE;

        // --> 20170927-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01
        {
        // --> 20170413-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01
        // --> 20170707-WI00-ADV01.01.00-28214-CDX01
        //recElementCampagne_L.GET(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.",recContratCadre_L."Campaign No.");
        IF recElementCampagne_L.GET(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.",recContratCadre_L."Campaign No.") THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20170707-WI00-ADV01.01.00-28214-CDX01
        recElementCampagne_L.ActionFromCrm(TRUE);
        recElementCampagne_L.Reopen;
        recElementCampagne_L.Release;
        // --> 20170707-WI00-ADV01.01.00-28214-CDX01
        END;
        // <-- 20170707-WI00-ADV01.01.00-28214-CDX01
        // <-- 20170413-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01
        }
        // <-- 20170927-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        GetLastConnectorError();
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncLigneContratCadreAppro@8053735(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075;recContratCadre_P@8053000 : Record 8053239) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recLigneContratCadre_L@8053607 : Record 8053240;
      LigneContratCadreAppro_L@8053606 : Text[110];
      Modify_L@8053608 : Boolean;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053611 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@8053609 : Integer;
      recContratCadre_L@8053612 : Record 8053239;
      recAbaque_L@8053613 : Record 8053237;
      Abaque_L@8053614 : Text[110];
      Funnel_L@8053615 : Text[50];
      Contract_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053617 : Text[110];
      Doc3_L@8053618 : Text[30];
      Doc4_L@8053619 : Text[30];
      Doc5_L@8053620 : Text[30];
      LineNo_L@8053602 : Integer;
      cduCVMan_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8053377;
      recTemplateLine_L@1000000000 : Record 8053235;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation de la ligne de contrat cadre appro dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquelle cr'er la donn'e
      //    - recContratCadre_P : Ent^te contrat cadre
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_supplyagreementline';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_abacustypeid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_comment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_computedpotential', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_previouscampaignactivity', '');
      // --> 20150903-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2194-FHS01
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_previouscampaignactivityqte', '');
      // <-- 20150903-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2194-FHS01
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementtemplateid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_theoreticalrate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'statuscode', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        LigneContratCadreAppro_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053664, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
        IF LigneContratCadreAppro_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          Doc1_L := COPYSTR(LigneContratCadreAppro_L, 1, STRPOS(LigneContratCadreAppro_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
          EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(LigneContratCadreAppro_L, STRPOS(LigneContratCadreAppro_L, GetSeparator()) + 1,
            MAXSTRLEN(LigneContratCadreAppro_L)));
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_abacustypeid');
        Abaque_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053669, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Abaque_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Rate Code"));
        IF Abaque_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          Code_L := Abaque_L;
          Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
          Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
          Doc4_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
          Doc5_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid');
        Funnel_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053673, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF ISNULLGUID(FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            Funnel_L := '';
          END ELSE BEGIN
            GuidFinded(Funnel_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));
          END;
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementid');
        Contract_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053655, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Contract_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract No."));

        //le contrat cadre appro existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF LigneContratCadreAppro_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recLigneContratCadre_L.GET(Doc1_L, Int1_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du contrat cadre appro
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          LineNo_L := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber'));
          IF recLigneContratCadre_L.GET(Contract_L, LineNo_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLigneContratCadre_L.INIT;
            recLigneContratCadre_L."Frame Contract No." := Contract_L;
            recLigneContratCadre_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
          END;
        END;
        recLigneContratCadre_L.Description := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name');
        IF recContratCadre_L.GET(Contract_L) THEN BEGIN
          recLigneContratCadre_L."Campaign No." := recContratCadre_L."Campaign No.";
        END;
        recLigneContratCadre_L."Item Funnel Code" := Funnel_L;
        recLigneContratCadre_L."Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface'));
        IF recAbaque_L.GET(Doc3_L, Doc4_L, Doc5_L) THEN BEGIN
          recLigneContratCadre_L."Rate Code" := recAbaque_L.Code;
        END;

        // --> 20170927-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01
        IF Doc5_L = '' THEN
          recLigneContratCadre_L."Rate Code" := '';
        // <-- 20170927-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22813-BSI01

        recLigneContratCadre_L."Theorical Rate" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_theoreticalrate'));
        recLigneContratCadre_L."Calculated Need" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_computedpotential'));

        // --> 20150903-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2194-FHS01
        IF recTemplateLine_L.GET(recContratCadre_P."Frame Contract Type", recLigneContratCadre_L."Line No.") THEN
          IF recTemplateLine_L."Activity Type" = recTemplateLine_L."Activity Type"::Quantity THEN BEGIN
            recLigneContratCadre_L."Previous Campaign Activity" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L,
              'cmc_previouscampaignactivityqte'));
          END ELSE
            IF recTemplateLine_L."Activity Type" = recTemplateLine_L."Activity Type"::"LCY Amount" THEN
        // <-- 20150903-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2194-FHS01
              recLigneContratCadre_L."Previous Campaign Activity" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L,
                'cmc_previouscampaignactivity'));
        recLigneContratCadre_L.Commitment := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment'));
        recLigneContratCadre_L.Comment := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_comment');
        // --> 20150618-FINA13-CMC02.02.00-2965-DRN01
        //BEFORE
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2869-FHS01
        //recLigneContratCadre_L."Customer No." := cduCVMan_L.GetCustVentNoFromContNo(recContratCadre_P."Contact No.", 0);
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2869-FHS01
        //AFTER
        recLigneContratCadre_L."Customer No." := cduCVMan_L.GetCustVendBankFromContact(recContratCadre_P."Contact No.", 0);
        // <-- 20150618-FINA13-CMC02.02.00-2965-DRN01

        recLigneContratCadre_L.SystemCreated(TRUE);
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recLigneContratCadre_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recLigneContratCadre_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053664, recLigneContratCadre_L."Frame Contract No." + GetSeparator() +
          FORMAT(recLigneContratCadre_L."Line No."), CrmGuid_P);

        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncContratCadreCollecte@8053736(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      ContratCadreCollect_L@8053606 : Text[30];
      recContratCadre_L@8053618 : Record 8053208;
      ElementCampaign_L@8053617 : Text[54];
      Contact_L@8053616 : Text[54];
      Campaign_L@8053615 : Text[54];
      Modify_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      Commitment_L@8053613 : Text[54];
      SupplyAgreement_L@8053612 : Text[54];
      MasterAgreementType_L@8053611 : Text[54];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      CommitmentCode_L@8053607 : Text[30];
      recCampaign_L@8053602 : Record 8052991;
      recCampaign2_L@8053608 : Record 8052991;
      CodeErp_L@8053609 : Text[10];
      recContractCamp_L@8053000 : Record 8053324;
      Erreur_L@8053001 : Text[250];
      recTemp_L@8053619 : TEMPORARY Record 8053610;
      recCMCSetup_L@8053002 : Record 8052982;
      DateSignature_L@8053003 : Date;
      DateSignaturetxt_L@8053004 : Text[30];
      recAssolemment_L@1000000000 : Record 8053195;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation du contrat cadre collecte dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquelle cr'er la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectmasteragreement';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaignid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linesdetailsgenerated', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linesgenerated', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentcompliance', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_datesignature', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'statuscode', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '175490000' THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
          EXIT(Result_L);
        END;
        recCMCSetup_L.GET();

        ContratCadreCollect_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053656, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid');
        Contact_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Contact_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contact No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid');
        Campaign_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Campaign_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));
        recCampaign_L.GET(Campaign_L);
        recCampaign2_L.RESET;
        recCampaign2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code","Starting Date");
        recCampaign2_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '>%1', recCampaign_L."Ending Date");
        recCampaign2_L.FINDFIRST;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment_id');
        Commitment_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053660, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Commitment_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Commitment Code"));
        IF Commitment_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          MasterAgreementType_L := COPYSTR(Commitment_L, 1, STRPOS(Commitment_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
          CommitmentCode_L := COPYSTR(Commitment_L, STRPOS(Commitment_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Commitment_L));
        END;

        IF recContractCamp_L.GET(recCampaign2_L."No.", MasterAgreementType_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
            IF WORKDATE > recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" THEN BEGIN
              Result_L := TRUE;
              EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          END;
        END;

        // --> 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
        CLEAR(recAssolemment_L);
        recAssolemment_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Contact_L);
        recAssolemment_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", Campaign_L);
        recAssolemment_L.SETFILTER(Range,'<>%1','');
        // --> 20161006-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22265-BSI01
        {before
        recAssolemment_L.FINDFIRST;
        after}
        IF NOT recAssolemment_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          ERROR(Text8053701,Campaign_L,Contact_L);
        // <-- 20161006-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22265-BSI01
        // <-- 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01

        //le contrat cadre appro existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF ContratCadreCollect_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recContratCadre_L.GET(ContratCadreCollect_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du contrat cadre collecte
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          CodeErp_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recContratCadre_L."Contract No."));
          IF recContratCadre_L.GET(CodeErp_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recContratCadre_L.INIT;
            recContratCadre_L."Contract No." := CodeErp_L;
          END;
        END;
        recContratCadre_L.Description := COPYSTR(Text8053699, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recContratCadre_L.Description));

        recContratCadre_L."Contract Type" := MasterAgreementType_L;
        IF recCMCSetup_L."Frame Contract On Next Campaig" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recContratCadre_L."Campaign No." := recCampaign_L."No.";
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recContratCadre_L."Campaign No." := recCampaign2_L."No.";
        END;
        recContratCadre_L."Commitment Code" := CommitmentCode_L;
        recContratCadre_L."Contact No." := Contact_L;
        recContratCadre_L."Crop Rotation Campaign No." := Campaign_L;
        DateSignaturetxt_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_datesignature');
        IF EVALUATE(DateSignature_L, DateSignaturetxt_L) THEN
          recContratCadre_L.VALIDATE("Date Signed", DateSignature_L);

        recContratCadre_L.Status  := recContratCadre_L.Status::Open;
        recContratCadre_L.SystemCreated(TRUE);
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recContratCadre_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recContratCadre_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053656, recContratCadre_L."Contract No.", CrmGuid_P);

        //rechercher les lignes associ'es pour les ajouter . l'importation
        recTemp_L.DELETEALL;
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectagreementline';
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectagreementlineid', '');
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid', '');
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(CrmGuid_P));
        IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            recTemp_L.INIT;
            recTemp_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectagreementlineid'));
            recTemp_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;
        recTemp_L.RESET;
        IF recTemp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            SyncLigneContratCadreCollecte(recTemp_L."CRM Guid", recCrmOrganisation_P);
          UNTIL recTemp_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        Erreur_L := recContratCadre_L.GetInfoSynchro();
        //Mise . jour du message d'erreur sur le contrat
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectmasteragreement';
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rejectionreason', Erreur_L);
        IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN ;
        IF Erreur_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          ClearConnector();
          ERROR(TextDeletevent);
        END;

        //Ajout du contrat cadre . l'export pour mettre . jour le n› ERP
        AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::ContratCadreCollect, recContratCadre_L."Contract No.", TextModify, '');

        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;


      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncLigneContratCadreCollecte@8053737(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recLigneContrat_L@8053606 : Record 8053209;
      Contract_L@8053607 : Text[30];
      Funnel_L@8053608 : Text[30];
      LigneContrat_L@8053609 : Text[110];
      Doc1_L@8053610 : Text[30];
      LineNo_L@8053611 : Integer;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053613 : Boolean;
      recContratCadre_L@8053602 : Record 8053208;
      FrameContractLineDetail_L@8053614 : Record 8053210;
      recAssolemment_L@1000000000 : Record 8053195;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation de la ligne de contrat cadre collecte dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquelle cr'er la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectagreementline';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amendment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_assignedquantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_homeconsumptionsurface', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_maxamendment', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pfpotential', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potential', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plannedpfquantity', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        LigneContrat_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053665, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
        IF LigneContrat_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          Doc1_L := COPYSTR(LigneContrat_L, 1, STRPOS(LigneContrat_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
          LineNo_L := FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(LigneContrat_L, STRPOS(LigneContrat_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(LigneContrat_L)));
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid');
        Funnel_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053673, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Funnel_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid');
        Contract_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053656, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Contract_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contract No."));

        recContratCadre_L.GET(Contract_L);

        //le contrat cadre appro existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF LigneContrat_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recLigneContrat_L.GET(Doc1_L, LineNo_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        // --> 20161006-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22265-BSI01
        {before
        IF LineNo_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
          recLigneContrat_L.RESET;
          recLigneContrat_L.SETRANGE("Contract No.", Contract_L);
          IF recLigneContrat_L.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            LineNo_L := recLigneContrat_L."Line No." + 10000;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            LineNo_L := 10000;
          END;
        END;
        after}
        // <-- 20161006-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22265-BSI01

        // --> 20150312-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2818-FHS01
        recAssolemment_L.RESET;
        recAssolemment_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", recContratCadre_L."Contact No.");
        recAssolemment_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", recContratCadre_L."Crop Rotation Campaign No.");
        recAssolemment_L.SETRANGE(Range, Funnel_L);
        IF recAssolemment_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          LineNo_L := recAssolemment_L."Line No.";
        // <-- 20150312-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2818-FHS01

        //Cr'ation du contrat cadre appro
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF recLigneContrat_L.GET(Contract_L, LineNo_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLigneContrat_L.INIT;
            recLigneContrat_L."Contract No." := Contract_L;
            recLigneContrat_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
          END;
        END;
        recLigneContrat_L.Description := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLigneContrat_L.Description));
        recLigneContrat_L."Item Funnel Code" := Funnel_L;
        recLigneContrat_L."Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface'));
        recLigneContrat_L."Autoconsumed Surface (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_homeconsumptionsurface'));
        recLigneContrat_L."% Rate" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rate'));
        recLigneContrat_L."Quantity (T)":= FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity'));
        recLigneContrat_L.Potential := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potential'));
        recLigneContrat_L."Potential PF" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pfpotential'));
        recLigneContrat_L."Qty. PF (Expected)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plannedpfquantity'));

        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recLigneContrat_L.INSERT;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          FrameContractLineDetail_L.RESET;
          FrameContractLineDetail_L.SETRANGE("Contract No.", recLigneContrat_L."Contract No.");
          FrameContractLineDetail_L.SETRANGE("Contract Line No.", recLigneContrat_L."Line No.");
          IF FrameContractLineDetail_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              IF FrameContractLineDetail_L."Collection Contract No." <> '' THEN
                EXIT(TRUE);
            UNTIL FrameContractLineDetail_L.NEXT=0;
          recLigneContrat_L.MODIFY;
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053665, recLigneContrat_L."Contract No." + GetSeparator() +
          FORMAT(recLigneContrat_L."Line No."), FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncParcel@8053773(CrmGuid_P@8053611 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053616 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053615 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      recParcel_L@8053613 : Record 8053122;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053610 : Boolean;
      Parcel_L@8053609 : Code[10];
      Farmer_L@8053605 : Code[20];
      Owner_L@8053600 : Code[20];
      ParcelCRM_L@8053601 : Code[10];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Export des parcelles
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_plot';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_farmeraccount_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_owneraccount_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_description', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_area', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Parcel_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053690, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
        ParcelCRM_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
        IF (Parcel_L <> '') AND (Parcel_L <> ParcelCRM_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF recParcel_L.GET(ParcelCRM_L) THEN BEGIN
            recParcel_L.DELETE;
          END;
          DeleteGuid(Text8053690, Parcel_L, CrmGuid_P);
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_farmeraccount_id');
        Farmer_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Farmer_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Farmer No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_owneraccount_id');
        Owner_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Owner_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Owner No."));

        //la parcelle existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF ParcelCRM_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recParcel_L.GET(ParcelCRM_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du contrat cadre appro
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recParcel_L.INIT;
          recParcel_L.Code := ParcelCRM_L;
        END;
        recParcel_L."Farmer No." := Farmer_L;
        recParcel_L."Owner No." := Owner_L;
        recParcel_L."Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_area'));
        recParcel_L.Description := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_description');

        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recParcel_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recParcel_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053690, recParcel_L.Code, FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncParcellaire@8053769(CrmGuid_P@8053603 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053614 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053613 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      recParcellaire_L@8053611 : Record 8053123;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053604 : Boolean;
      Parcel_L@8053600 : Code[10];
      Species_L@8053601 : Code[10];
      Camp_L@8053602 : Code[20];
      Product_L@8053615 : Code[20];
      Farmer_L@8053616 : Code[20];
      Parcellaire_L@8053617 : Code[50];
      Doc1_L@8053606 : Code[10];
      Doc2_L@8053607 : Code[20];
      Date1_L@8053608 : Date;
      Code_L@8053609 : Code[54];
      StartDate_L@8053610 : Date;
      recCampaign_L@8053001 : Record 8052991;
      recCampaign2_L@8053000 : Record 8052991;
      Doc3_L@8053002 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation d'un parcellaire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_plotplan';

      //initialisation de la connexion et du dialogue
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_farmeraccount_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plot_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species_id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plotplanreturndate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Parcellaire_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053691, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
        IF Parcellaire_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          Code_L := Parcellaire_L;
          Doc1_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Doc1_L));
          Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
          Doc2_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Doc2_L));
          // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //EVALUATE(Date1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
          ///AFTER
          Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
          EVALUATE(Date1_L, COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Doc2_L)));
          Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
          recParcellaire_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Date1_L, Doc3_L);
          // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_farmeraccount_id');
        Farmer_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Farmer_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcellaire_L.FIELDCAPTION("Farmer No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plot_id');
        Parcel_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053690, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Parcel_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcellaire_L.FIELDCAPTION(Code));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product_id');
        Product_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053627, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Product_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcellaire_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id');
        Camp_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Camp_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcellaire_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));
        recCampaign_L.GET(Camp_L);
        recCampaign2_L.RESET;
        recCampaign2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code","Starting Date");
        recCampaign2_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '>%1', recCampaign_L."Ending Date");
        recCampaign2_L.FINDFIRST;

        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species_id');
        Species_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053651, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        GuidFinded(Species_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recParcellaire_L.FIELDCAPTION("Species Code"));

        StartDate_L := FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate'));

        //le parcellaire existe-il ?
        Modify_L := FALSE;
        IF Parcellaire_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //IF recParcellaire_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Date1_L) THEN BEGIN
          //  IF (Doc1_L <> Parcel_L) OR (Doc2_L <> Farmer_L) OR (Date1_L <> StartDate_L) THEN BEGIN
          //AFTER
          IF recParcellaire_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Date1_L, Doc3_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF (Doc1_L <> Parcel_L) OR (Doc2_L <> Farmer_L) OR (Date1_L <> StartDate_L) OR (Doc3_L <> Product_L) THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
              recParcellaire_L.DELETE;
            END;
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //IF recParcellaire_L.GET(Parcel_L, Farmer_L, StartDate_L) THEN BEGIN
          //AFTER
          IF recParcellaire_L.GET(Parcel_L, Farmer_L, StartDate_L, Product_L) THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //Cr'ation du contrat cadre appro
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recParcellaire_L.INIT;
          recParcellaire_L.Code := Parcel_L;
          recParcellaire_L."Farmer No." := Farmer_L;
          // --> 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2952-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //recParcellaire_L."Starting Date" := recCampaign2_L."Starting Date";
          //AFTER
          recParcellaire_L."Starting Date" := StartDate_L;
          // <-- 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2952-FHS01
          // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
          recParcellaire_L."Item No." := Product_L;
          // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        END;
        recParcellaire_L."Area (Ha)" := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface'));
        recParcellaire_L."Species Code" := Species_L;
        // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //recParcellaire_L."Item No." := Product_L;
        //AFTER
        // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        recParcellaire_L."Campaign No." := recCampaign2_L."No.";
        recParcellaire_L."Parcel Card Return Date" := FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plotplanreturndate'));
        recParcellaire_L."End Date" := recCampaign2_L."Ending Date";

        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          recParcellaire_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recParcellaire_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053691, recParcellaire_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recParcellaire_L."Farmer No."  + GetSeparator() +
          // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //FORMAT(recParcellaire_L."Starting Date", 8, '<Day,2><Month,2><Year4>'), FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
          //AFTER
          FORMAT(recParcellaire_L."Starting Date", 8, '<Day,2><Month,2><Year4>') + GetSeparator() + recParcellaire_L."Item No.",
          FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
          // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitSyncContrat@8053753();
    VAR
      recCommitment_L@8053600 : Record 8053199;

    PROCEDURE InitAssolement@1000000006();
    VAR
      Count_L@1000000000 : Integer;
      TxtGuid_L@1000000001 : Text[50];
      recJournal_L@1000000002 : Record 8053603;
      EntityName_L@1000000003 : Text[50];
      LastCode_L@1000000004 : Text[50];
      i_L@1000000005 : Integer;
      Key_L@1000000006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Initialisation de la synchro des contrats. Met en file d'attente toutes les donn'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        Key_L := 1;
        IF recJournal_L.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
          Key_L := recJournal_L."Entry No." + 1;
        END;
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_elementcampaign';

        LastCode_L := '';
        REPEAT
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '>', LastCode_L);
          END;
          SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode) <> '2' THEN BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
                recJournal_L.INIT;
                recJournal_L."Entry No." := Key_L;
                Key_L += 1;
                recJournal_L.Status := recJournal_L.Status::" ";
                recJournal_L.Error_ := '';
                recJournal_L.Origine := 'CRM_CMC_ELEMENTCAMPAIGN_EVENT';
                recJournal_L.Events := 'MODIFICATION';
                recJournal_L.Data := recJournal_L.Origine + ';' + recJournal_L.Events + ';' + CleanGuidToCRM(TxtGuid_L) + ';;' + USERID;
                recJournal_L.Guid := FormatGuid(TxtGuid_L);
                recJournal_L.INSERT;
              END;
              LastCode_L := TxtGuid_L;
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        UNTIL Count_L = 0;
      END;

      ClearConnector();
    END;
