    PROCEDURE DeleteAddress@8053006(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID);
    VAR
      recAddress_L@8053608 : Record 222;
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053600 : Text[30];
      Address_L@8053606 : Text[110];
      recGuid_L@8053000 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Suppression de l'adresse suppl'mentaire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Address_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053636, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Address_L, 1, STRPOS(Address_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Address_L, STRPOS(Address_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Address_L));
      IF recAddress_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L) THEN BEGIN
        recAddress_L.DELETE(TRUE);
        DeleteGuid(Text8053636, Address_L, CrmGuid_P);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteElementCampagne@8053764(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID);
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Contact_L@8053604 : Text[54];
      Campaign_L@8053603 : Text[54];
      recElementCampagne_L@8053608 : Record 8053194;
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053600 : Text[30];
      ElementCampagne_L@8053606 : Text[110];
      recDetailsElementCampagne_L@8053000 : Record 8053195;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Suppression de l''l'ment de campagne dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      ElementCampagne_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053659, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(ElementCampagne_L, 1, STRPOS(ElementCampagne_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(ElementCampagne_L, STRPOS(ElementCampagne_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(ElementCampagne_L));
      IF recElementCampagne_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L) THEN BEGIN
        IF recElementCampagne_L.Status <> recElementCampagne_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L.RESET;
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Doc1_L);
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", Doc2_L);
          IF NOT(recDetailsElementCampagne_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);
          END;
          recElementCampagne_L.DELETE(TRUE);
          DeleteGuid2(Text8053659, ElementCampagne_L, CrmGuid_P);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteDetailsElementCampagne@8053763(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID);
    VAR
      Contact_L@8053604 : Text[54];
      Campaign_L@8053603 : Text[54];
      TranscriptionPattern_L@8053605 : Text[110];
      ElementCampagne_L@8053606 : Text[54];
      Modify_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      recDetailsElementCampagne_L@8053608 : Record 8053195;
      recElementCampagne_L@8053619 : Record 8053194;
      DetailsElementCampagne_L@8053609 : Text[110];
      Doc1_L@8053615 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053613 : Text[30];
      LineNo_L@8053610 : Integer;
      Code_L@8053611 : Text[250];
      Separator_L@8053621 : Text[4];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Suppression de l''l'ment de campagne dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Separator_L := GetSeparator();
      DetailsElementCampagne_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053657, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
      Code_L := DetailsElementCampagne_L;
      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      LineNo_L := FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
      IF recDetailsElementCampagne_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, LineNo_L) THEN BEGIN
        IF recElementCampagne_L.GET(recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Contact No.", recDetailsElementCampagne_L."Campaign No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recElementCampagne_L.Status <> recElementCampagne_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            recDetailsElementCampagne_L.DELETE(TRUE);
            DeleteGuid2(Text8053657, DetailsElementCampagne_L, CrmGuid_P);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recDetailsElementCampagne_L.DELETE(TRUE);
          DeleteGuid2(Text8053657, DetailsElementCampagne_L, CrmGuid_P);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteLigneContratCadreAppro@8053761(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID);

      LigneContratCadreAppro_L@8053606 : Text[110];
      Doc1_L@8053611 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@8053609 : Integer;
      recContratCadre_L@8053612 : Record 8053239;

        EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(LigneContratCadreAppro_L, STRPOS(LigneContratCadreAppro_L, GetSeparator()) + 1,
          MAXSTRLEN(LigneContratCadreAppro_L)));

            IF recContratCadre_L.Status <> recContratCadre_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
              recLigneContratCadre_L.DELETE(TRUE);

            recLigneContratCadre_L.DELETE(TRUE);

        END;
      END;
    END;

      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Parcellaire_L := GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053691, CrmGuid_P, FALSE);
      IF Parcellaire_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        Code_L := Parcellaire_L;
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Doc1_L));
        Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        Doc2_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Doc2_L));
        // --> 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //EVALUATE(Date1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
        //recParcellaire_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Date1_L);
        //AFTER
        Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        EVALUATE(Date1_L, COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(Doc2_L)));
        Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        recParcellaire_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Date1_L, Doc3_L);
        // <-- 20150325-Gen04-CMC02.02.00-2844-FHS01
        recParcellaire_L.DELETE(TRUE);
        DeleteGuid2(Text8053691, Parcellaire_L, CrmGuid_P);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<--Commande-->"@8053741();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportSimpleOrder@8053758(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      cduImportOrder_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recSASHeaderUpd_L@8053602 : Record 8053610;
      recSASHeaderLine_L@8053601 : Record 8053611;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // import d'une commande SAS
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeader_P : ent^te de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //tentative d'import de la commande
      CLEAR(cduImportOrder_L);
      // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      //cduImportOrder_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::Order, NavBPMConnector_G);
      //AFTER
      cduImportOrder_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::Order, NavBPMConnector_G, NavBPMConnector2_G);
      // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
      cduImportOrder_L.SetParameterOrder(recSASHeader_P);
      IF NOT(cduImportOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
        recSASHeaderUpd_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeader_P."SAS Status"::Error;
        recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
        recSASHeaderUpd_L.MODIFY;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recSASHeaderUpd_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        //suppression des lignes termin'es
        recSASHeaderLine_L.RESET;
        recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        // --> 20180216-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33314-BSI
        //recSASHeaderLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASHeaderLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
        recSASHeaderLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1&<>%2', recSASHeaderLine_L."SAS Status"::Created,
                                                                recSASHeaderLine_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
        // <-- 20180216-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33314-BSI
        // --> 20171114-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
        recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE(Deleted,FALSE);
        // <-- 20171114-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
        IF recSASHeaderLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
          //la commande est int'gr'e et il n'y a plus de ligne
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status"::Created;
          // --> 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."Treatment Date" := CREATEDATETIME(WORKDATE,TIME);
          // <-- 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          recSASHeaderUpd_L.MODIFY;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //il reste des lignes en erreur
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status"::Error;
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error" := Text8053683;
          recSASHeaderUpd_L.MODIFY;
        END;
      END;

      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportAllSimpleOrder@8053747();
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053600 : Record 8053610;
      cduImportOrder_L@8053601 : Codeunit 8053608;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Import de toutes les commandes dans le SAS
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.RESET;
      recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("SAS Status");
      // --> 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      //recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1&<>%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created,recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
      // <-- 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      // --> 20171113-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      recSASHeader_L.SETRANGE(Deleted,FALSE);
      // <-- 20171113-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      IF NOT(recSASHeader_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
        IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
          IF recSASHeader_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              //tentative d'import de la commande
              ImportSimpleOrder(recSASHeader_L);
            UNTIL recSASHeader_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
          CleanSAS();
        END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportSimpleOrderWithoutInErro@8053746();
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053600 : Record 8053610;
      cduImportOrder_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053608;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Import des commandes du SAS n''tant pas en erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.RESET;
      recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("SAS Status");
      // --> 20150724-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2018-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      //recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1&<>%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Error,
      //  recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      //AFTER
      recCRMSetup_G.GET();
      IF (recCRMSetup_G."Begin Time SAS" <> 0T) AND (recCRMSetup_G."End Time SAS" <> 0T) THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT((recCRMSetup_G."Begin Time SAS" < TIME) AND (recCRMSetup_G."End Time SAS" > TIME)) THEN
        // --> 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3167-JCD01
        {BEFORE
          recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '%1|%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::" ");
        AFTER}
          recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '%1|%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::" ")
        ELSE BEGIN
          // --> 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
          {before
          recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Secondary Key");
          after}
          recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Secondary Key","SAS Status");
          // --> 20171106-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30758-BSI01
          recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '%1', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Error);
          // <-- 20171106-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30758-BSI01
          // <-- 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
        END;
        // <-- 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3167-JCD01
      END ELSE
        // --> 20171017-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
        //recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created);
        recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1&<>2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created,recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
        // <-- 20171017-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      // <-- 20150724-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2018-FHS01
      // --> 20171113-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      recSASHeader_L.SETRANGE(Deleted,FALSE);
      // <-- 20171113-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      IF NOT(recSASHeader_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
        IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
          IF recSASHeader_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              //tentative d'import de la commande
              ImportSimpleOrder(recSASHeader_L);
            UNTIL recSASHeader_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
          CleanSAS();
        END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportSimpleOrderInError@8053744();
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053600 : Record 8053610;
      cduImportOrder_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053608;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Import uniquement des commandes du SAS 'tant en cours de progression (mode manuel direct)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recCRMSetup_G.GET();
      recSASHeader_L.RESET;
      recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("SAS Status");
      // --> 20150518-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2994-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      //recSASHeader_L.SETRANGE("SAS Status", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress");
      //AFTER
      IF (recCRMSetup_G."Begin Time SAS" <> 0T) AND (recCRMSetup_G."End Time SAS" <> 0T) THEN BEGIN
        IF (recCRMSetup_G."Begin Time SAS" < TIME) OR (recCRMSetup_G."End Time SAS" > TIME) THEN
        // --> 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3167-JCD01
        {BEFORE
          recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '%1|%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::" ");
        AFTER}
          recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '%1|%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::" ")
        ELSE
          recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Secondary Key");
        // <-- 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3167-JCD01

      END ELSE
        // --> 20150724-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2018-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '%1|%2', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::" ");
        //AFTER
        recSASHeader_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created);
        // <-- 20150724-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2018-FHS01
      // <-- 20150518-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2994-FHS01
      IF NOT(recSASHeader_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
        IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
          IF recSASHeader_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              //tentative d'import de la commande
              ImportSimpleOrder(recSASHeader_L);
            UNTIL recSASHeader_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertSimpleOrder@8053742(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053601 : Record 8053610;
      recHeader_L@8053602 : Record 36;
      Modify_L@8053603 : Boolean;
      recComment_L@8053604 : Record 44;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053611;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053607 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduImportLine_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recLineUpd_L@8053609 : Record 8053611;
      StatutCode_L@8053610 : Code[10];
      Statut_L@8053612 : Integer;
      EntityName_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053613 : Codeunit 414;
      recSalesLine_L@8053614 : Record 37;
      recShipmentMethod_L@8053000 : Record 10;
      ApprovalManagement_L@8053001 : Codeunit 439;
      MailMgt_L@8053002 : Codeunit 8060999;
      Module_L@8053004 : 'Purchase,Sales,Service,Reminder,Finance Charge';
      DocumentType_L@8053003 : ' ,Quote,Order,Return Order,Blanket Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Shipment,Receipt';
      ModifyHeader_L@8053005 : Boolean;
      recAddr_L@8053006 : Record 222;
      recCustomer_L@1000000000 : Record 18;
      RecSalesRecSetup_L@1000000001 : Record 311;
      RecSalesHead_L@1000000002 : Record 36;
      StatusReleased_L@1000000003 : Boolean;
      RecSalesLineCount_L@1000000004 : Record 37;
      LineCount_L@1000000005 : Integer;
      CUReleaseSalesDoc_L@1000000006 : Codeunit 414;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une commande du SAS
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeader_P : ent^te de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //v'rification ent^te complSte
      IF recSASHeader_P."Read Complete" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        GetCompanyMcCode();
        GetSimpleOrder(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid", recCompagny_G);
      END;

      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
      IF recSASHeader_L."Read Complete" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ERROR(Text8053678);
      END;

      IF recSASHeader_P.Type = recSASHeader_P.Type::"TPG Contract" THEN BEGIN
        InsertTPGContract(recSASHeader_P);
        EXIT;
      END;

      // --> 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01
      StatusReleased_L := FALSE;
      LineCount_L := 0;
      // <-- 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01

      //Une commande en cours existe d'j.
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      IF recSASHeader_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, recSASHeader_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01
          IF recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            StatusReleased_L := TRUE;
            CLEAR(RecSalesLineCount_L);
            RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",recHeader_L."Document Type");
            RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",recHeader_L."No.");
            LineCount_L := RecSalesLineCount_L.COUNT;
          END;
          // <-- 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01

          // --> 20150817-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2083-FHS01
          IF recHeader_L."Freezed Document" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20150817-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2083-FHS01
            IF recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
              //R'ouverture de la commande pour int'grer la suite
              CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
              cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(recHeader_L."No.");
              cduRunOrder_L.SetReopenOnly(TRUE);
              IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
                ERROR(Text8053680, GETLASTERRORTEXT());
              END;
              // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
              COMMIT;
              // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
            END;
            Modify_L := TRUE;
            recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, recSASHeader_L."No.");
          // --> 20150817-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2083-FHS01
          END;
          // <-- 20150817-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2083-FHS01
        END;
      END;

      GetSetup();

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ent^te
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2313-FHS01
        CLEAR(recHeader_L);
        // <-- 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2313-FHS01
        recHeader_L.INIT;
        recHeader_L."Document Type" := recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order;
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", WORKDATE);
        recHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        recSASHeader_L."No." := recHeader_L."No.";
        recSASHeader_L.MODIFY;

        IF recHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." THEN BEGIN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.", recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
        // --> 20150727-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2024-FHS01
          recCustomer_L.GET(recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
          IF recCustomer_L."Group Account Code" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Group Account Code", recCustomer_L."Group Account Code");
        END;
        // <-- 20150727-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2024-FHS01

        IF recSASHeader_L."Payment Terms Code" <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."Payment Terms Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Payment Terms Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Terms Code", recSASHeader_L."Payment Terms Code");

        IF recSASHeader_L."Payment Method Code" <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."Payment Method Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Payment Method Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Method Code", recSASHeader_L."Payment Method Code");

        IF recSASHeader_L."Requested Delivery Date" <> 0D THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date", recSASHeader_L."Requested Delivery Date");
        IF recSASHeader_L."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."CMC Campaign No." <> recSASHeader_L."CMC Campaign No." THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("CMC Campaign No.", recSASHeader_L."CMC Campaign No.");

        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("External Document No.", recSASHeader_L."External Document No.");

        // --> 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2995-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //IF recSASHeader_L."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN
        //  recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Method Code", recSASHeader_L."Shipment Method Code");
        ///AFTER
        // <-- 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2995-FHS01

        // --> 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01
        {before
        // --> 20151125-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2503-NED01
        IF recHeader_L."Document Source" <> recCRMSetup_G."Document Source Simple Order" THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Source", recCRMSetup_G."Document Source Simple Order");
        // <-- 20151125-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2503-NED01

        IF recSASHeader_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."Location Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Location Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", recSASHeader_L."Location Code");
        after}
        // <-- 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01

        IF (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> '') AND (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> recHeader_L."Salesperson Code") THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Salesperson Code", recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code");

        //utilisation d'une adresse de livraison uniquement si ce n'est pas une adresse principale
        IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recHeader_L."Ship-to Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code");
          // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address" <> '' THEN
          // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Address", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address");
          // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address 2" <> '' THEN
          // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Address 2", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address 2");
          // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN
          // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
            // --> 20150527-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3012-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Post Code", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code");
            //AFTER
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Post Code", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Post Code");
            // <-- 20150527-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3012-FHS01
          // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to City" <> '' THEN
          // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to City", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to City");
        // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recAddr_L.RESET;
          recAddr_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", recSASHeader_P."Sell-to Customer No.");
          IF recAddr_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", recAddr_L.Code);
        // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        END;

        // --> 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2995-FHS01
        IF recSASHeader_L."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Method Code", recSASHeader_L."Shipment Method Code");
        // <-- 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2995-FHS01

        IF recHeader_L."Doc. Category Code" <> recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales" THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Doc. Category Code", recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales");

        // --> 20150518-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1838-FHS01
        IF recHeader_L."Document Source" <> recCRMSetup_G."Document Source Simple Order" THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Source", recCRMSetup_G."Document Source Simple Order");
        // <-- 20150518-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1838-FHS01

        // --> 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01
        RecSalesRecSetup_L.GET;
        IF recSASHeader_L."Location Code" <> RecSalesRecSetup_L."Drop Shipment Location Code" THEN
        // <-- 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01
          // --> 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
          IF recSASHeader_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN
            IF recHeader_L."Location Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Location Code" THEN
              recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", recSASHeader_L."Location Code");
          // <-- 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01

        recHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053629, recHeader_L."No.", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");

        //gestion des commentaires logistics
        LineNo_L := 0;
        IF (recSASHeader_L.Comment <> '') AND (Modify_L = FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          LineNo_L += 10000;
          CLEAR(recComment_L);
          recComment_L.INIT;
          recComment_L."Document Type" := recHeader_L."Document Type";
          recComment_L."No." := recHeader_L."No.";
          recComment_L."Document Line No." := 0;
          recComment_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
          recComment_L.Date := TODAY;
          recComment_L.Comment := COPYSTR(recSASHeader_L.Comment, 1, 80);
          recComment_L.Type := recComment_L.Type::Logistic;
          recComment_L.INSERT(TRUE);

          IF STRLEN(recSASHeader_L.Comment) > 80 THEN
            recSASHeader_L.Comment := COPYSTR(recSASHeader_L.Comment, 81, STRLEN(recSASHeader_L.Comment) - 80)
          ELSE
            recSASHeader_L.Comment := '';
        UNTIL STRLEN(recSASHeader_L.Comment) = 0;
      // --> 20150518-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2983-FHS01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // --> 20150518-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
        ModifyHeader_L := FALSE;
        recSalesLine_L.RESET;
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recHeader_L."Document Type");
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recHeader_L."No.");
        IF recSalesLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Order Date", WORKDATE);
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", WORKDATE);
          ModifyHeader_L := TRUE;
        END;
        // <-- 20150518-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
        IF recSASHeader_L."Linked Order No." <> '' THEN
          IF recSASHeader_L."Linked Order No." <> recHeader_L."Linked Order No." THEN BEGIN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Linked Order No.", recSASHeader_L."Linked Order No.");
            ModifyHeader_L := TRUE;
          END;
        IF ModifyHeader_L = TRUE THEN
          recHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
      // <-- 20150518-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2983-FHS01
      END;

      //Si tout c'est bien pass', on sauvegarde l'ent^te de la commande avant d'ins'rer les lignes
      //Sauvegarde de l'int'gration de la commande
      COMMIT;

      //recherche des lignes de la commande
      recLine_L.RESET;
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      // --> 20171113-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",'');
      recLine_L.SETRANGE(Deleted,FALSE);
      // <-- 20171113-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      // --> 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      //recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1&<>%2', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created,recLine_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
      // <-- 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      IF recLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //tentative d'import de la ligne
          CLEAR(cduImportLine_L);
          cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::OrderLine, NavBPMConnector_G, NavBPMConnector2_G);
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterOrderLine(recLine_L);
          IF NOT(cduImportLine_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Error;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
            // --> 20161220-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23622-BSI01
            //SetErrorOnSimpleOrderLine(recLineUpd_L);
            // <-- 20161220-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23622-BSI01
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
            // --> 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
            recLineUpd_L."Treatment Date" := CREATEDATETIME(WORKDATE,TIME);
            // <-- 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          END;
          recLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
          COMMIT;
        UNTIL recLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      // --> 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01
      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
      IF (recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Open) AND StatusReleased_L THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(RecSalesLineCount_L);
        RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",recHeader_L."Document Type");
        RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",recHeader_L."No.");
        IF (RecSalesLineCount_L.COUNT = LineCount_L) AND (RecSalesLineCount_L.COUNT <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          recHeader_L.Status := recHeader_L.Status::Released;
          recHeader_L.MODIFY;
          // --> 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-24473-BSI01
          CUReleaseSalesDoc_L.ReleaseSalesLines(recHeader_L);
          // <-- 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-24473-BSI01
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01

      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
      IF (recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Open) THEN BEGIN
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recHeader_L."Document Type");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recHeader_L."No.");
      IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
        recHeader_L."Is Manual Release" := TRUE;
        recHeader_L.MODIFY;
        // --> 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01
        {before
        CLEAR(cduReleaseSalesDoc_L);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
        after}
        COMMIT;
        CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
        cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(recHeader_L."No.");
        cduRunOrder_L.SetReopenOnly(FALSE);
        IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN    //lancement
          ERROR(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text8053679,GETLASTERRORTEXT), 1, 250));
        // <-- 20160908-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21274-BSI01
        END;
      END;

      recLine_L.RESET;
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      // --> 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      //recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1&<>%2', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created,recLine_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
      // <-- 20171018-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30250-BSI01
      // --> 20171114-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      recLine_L.SETRANGE(Deleted,FALSE);
      // <-- 20171114-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
      IF NOT(recLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        COMMIT;
        ERROR(Text8053683);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertSimpleOrderLine@8053743(recSASLine_P@8053600 : Record 8053611);
    VAR
      recSASLine_L@8053602 : Record 8053611;
      recSASHeader_L@8053603 : Record 8053610;
      recSalesLine_L@8053604 : Record 37;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Comment_L@8053606 : Text[250];
      CommentLineNo_L@8053607 : Integer;
      recComment_L@8053608 : Record 44;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053609 : Codeunit 8053602;
      recCmcSetup_L@8053611 : Record 8052982;
      recSalesParam_L@8053610 : Record 311;
      recSeason_L@8053613 : Record 8053154;
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053601 : Codeunit 414;
      recHeader_L@8053612 : Record 36;
      EntityName_L@8053000 : Text[50];
      AddPermissionMgt_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8061000;
      CanUpdateLocation_L@8053002 : Boolean;
      LocationCode_L@1000000000 : Code[20];
      RecItem_L@60000 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une ligne de commande de SAS
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASLine_P : ligne de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //v'rification ligne complSte
      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid");
      IF recSASLine_P."Read Complete" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        GetCompanyMcCode();
        GetSimpleOrderLine(recSASLine_P."CRM Guid", recCompagny_G, recSASHeader_L);
      END;

      recSASLine_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_P."CRM Guid");

      IF recSASLine_L."Read Complete" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ERROR(Text8053678);
      END;

      IF recSASLine_L."No." = '' THEN BEGIN
        ERROR(Text8053685);
      END;

      recCmcSetup_L.GET();
      recSalesParam_L.GET();

      //Recherche du prochain num'ro de ligne
      LineNo_L := 10000;
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order);
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      IF recSalesLine_L.FINDLAST THEN
        LineNo_L := recSalesLine_L."Line No." + 10000;

      //R'ouverture si la commande est lanc'e
      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, recSASHeader_L."No.");
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Open THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2806-FHS01
        //cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.PerformManualReopen(recHeader_L); //r'ouverture
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.Reopen(recHeader_L); //lancement
        // <-- 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2806-FHS01
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        COMMIT;
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
      END;

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ligne
      recSalesLine_L.INIT;
      recSalesLine_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
      recSalesLine_L."Document Type" := recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order;
      recSalesLine_L."Document No." := recSASHeader_L."No.";
      recSalesLine_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
      recSalesLine_L.Type := recSalesLine_L.Type::Item;
      //Insertion de la ligne
      recSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);

      recSalesLine_L."System Created" := TRUE;
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("No.", recSASLine_L."No.");
      // --> 20180108-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      recSalesLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
      // <-- 20180108-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", recSASLine_L."Variant Code");
      // --> 20180108-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      recSalesLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(FALSE);
      // <-- 20180108-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01

      IF recSASLine_L."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Method Code", recSASLine_L."Shipment Method Code");

      // --> 20180109-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      recSalesLine_L.AutoValidBuildKitOnLineChange(recSalesLine_L.FIELDNO("Location Code"));
      // <-- 20180109-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01

      // --> 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01
      {before
      // --> 20150901-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      {
      IF (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code") AND
         (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> '') AND (recSalesLine_L."Location Code" <> recSASLine_L."Location Code")
      THEN BEGIN
      }
      //AFTER
      {IF (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> '') AND (recSalesLine_L."Location Code" <> recSASLine_L."Location Code") THEN BEGIN
      // <-- 20150901-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-FHS01
        // --> 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
        CanUpdateLocation_L := TRUE;
        IF (recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility" = recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility"::"None (Item)") OR
          (recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility" = recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility"::"None (Logistic)")
        THEN
          IF AddPermissionMgt_L.ModifyPurchasingFlexibility THEN
            CanUpdateLocation_L := FALSE;
        IF CanUpdateLocation_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
          // --> 20150505-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2970-FHS01
          CheckLocationUpdate(recSalesLine_L, recSalesParam_L);
          // <-- 20150505-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2970-FHS01
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", recSASLine_L."Location Code");
          // --> 20150901-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-FHS01
          IF recSASLine_L."Location Code" = recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code" THEN
            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Purchasing Code", recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Purchasing Code");
          // <-- 20150901-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-FHS01
        // --> 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
        END;
        // <-- 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
      END;
      }
      // --> 20150901-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      {
      IF (recSASLine_L."Location Code" = recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code") AND
         (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> '') AND (recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Code" <> recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Purchasing Code")
      THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Purchasing Code", recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Purchasing Code");
      END;
      }
      //AFTER
      // <-- 20150901-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-FHS01
      after}
      IF recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility" <> recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility"::"None (Logistic)" THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", recSASLine_L."Location Code");
        IF recSASLine_L."Location Code" = recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code" THEN
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Purchasing Code", recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Purchasing Code");
      END;
      // <-- 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01

      IF recSASLine_L."Unit of Measure" <> '' THEN
        // --> 20150609-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3051-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Unit of Measure", recSASLine_L."Unit of Measure");
        //AFTER
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Unit of Measure Code", recSASLine_L."Unit of Measure");
        // <-- 20150609-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3051-FHS01
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity, recSASLine_L.Quantity);

      // --> 20150313-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
      IF recSASLine_L."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("CMC Campaign No.", recSASLine_L."CMC Campaign No.");
      // <-- 20150313-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
      //Gestion des saisons
      IF recCmcSetup_L."Activate Season" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        IF recSASLine_L."Season ID No." = 0 THEN BEGIN
          ERROR(Text8053681);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Season ID No.", recSASLine_L."Season ID No.");
        END;

        recSeason_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Season ID No.");
        // --> 20151117-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2458-NED01
        {before
        IF recSalesLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
          IF (recSalesLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" < recSeason_L."Delivery Starting Date") OR
             (recSalesLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" > recSeason_L."Delivery Ending Date")
          THEN BEGIN
            ERROR(Text8053682, recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Requested Delivery Date"));
          END;
        after}
        IF recSASLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
          IF (recSASLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" < recSeason_L."Delivery Starting Date") OR
             (recSASLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" > recSeason_L."Delivery Ending Date")
          THEN BEGIN
            ERROR(Text8053682, recSASLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Requested Delivery Date"));
          END;
        // <-- 20151117-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2458-NED01

        END;
        // --> 20151117-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2458-NED02
        {before
        IF recSalesLine_L."Due Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
          IF (recSalesLine_L."Due Date" < recSeason_L."Due Date Starting Date") OR
             (recSalesLine_L."Due Date" > recSeason_L."Due Date Ending Date")
          THEN BEGIN
            ERROR(Text8053682, recSalesLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Due Date"));
          END;
        after}
        IF recSASLine_L."Due Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
          IF (recSASLine_L."Due Date" < recSeason_L."Due Date Starting Date") OR
             (recSASLine_L."Due Date" > recSeason_L."Due Date Ending Date")
          THEN BEGIN
            ERROR(Text8053682, recSASLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Due Date"));
          END;
        // <-- 20151117-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2458-NED02

        END;
      END;

      //Gestion du lieu de d'chargement => va permettre de remplir le lieu de d'chargement sur les lignes
      IF recSASLine_L."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", recSASLine_L."Ship-to Code");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        // --> 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
        IF recHeader_L."Ship-to Address" <> '' THEN
        // <-- 20150522-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3006-FHS01
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", recHeader_L."Ship-to Code");
      END;
      // --> 20150702-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1862-FHS01
      IF recSASLine_L."Prod. Activity Code" <> '' THEN
      // <-- 20150702-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1862-FHS01
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Prod. Activity Code", recSASLine_L."Prod. Activity Code");
      IF recSASLine_L."Unloading Place Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Unloading Place Code", recSASLine_L."Unloading Place Code");
      END;
      IF recSASLine_L."Vehicle Size Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Vehicle Size Code", recSASLine_L."Vehicle Size Code");
      END;
      IF recSASLine_L."Time Slot Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Time Slot Code", recSASLine_L."Time Slot Code");
      END;
      IF recSASLine_L."Unloading Method Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Unloading Method Code", recSASLine_L."Unloading Method Code");
      END;

      //gestion des commentaires logistics
      Comment_L := recSASLine_L.Comment;
      CommentLineNo_L := 0;
      IF Comment_L <> '' THEN
      REPEAT
        CommentLineNo_L += 10000;
        CLEAR(recComment_L);
        recComment_L.INIT;
        recComment_L."Document Type" := recSalesLine_L."Document Type";
        recComment_L."No." := recSalesLine_L."Document No.";
        recComment_L."Document Line No." := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
        recComment_L."Line No." := CommentLineNo_L;
        recComment_L.Date := TODAY;
        recComment_L.Comment := COPYSTR(Comment_L, 1, 80);
        recComment_L.Type := recComment_L.Type::Logistic;
        recComment_L.INSERT(TRUE);

        IF STRLEN(Comment_L) > 80 THEN
          Comment_L := COPYSTR(Comment_L, 81, STRLEN(Comment_L) - 80)
        ELSE
          Comment_L := '';
      UNTIL STRLEN(Comment_L) = 0;

      IF recSASLine_L."Requested Delivery Date" <> 0D THEN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date", recSASLine_L."Requested Delivery Date");
      IF recSASLine_L."Due Date" <> 0D THEN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Due Date", recSASLine_L."Due Date");
      // --> 20150313-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
      //IF recSASLine_L."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN
      //  recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("CMC Campaign No.", recSASLine_L."CMC Campaign No.");
      // <-- 20150313-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
      IF recSASLine_L."Salesperson Code" <> '' THEN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Salesperson Code", recSASLine_L."Salesperson Code");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Due Pivot Date Gap", recSASLine_L."Due Pivot Date Gap");

      // --> 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01
      {before
      // --> 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
      IF (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code") AND
         (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> '') AND (recSalesLine_L."Location Code" <> recSASLine_L."Location Code")
      THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
        CanUpdateLocation_L := TRUE;
        IF (recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility" = recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility"::"None (Item)") OR
          (recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility" = recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Flexibility"::"None (Logistic)")
        THEN
          IF NOT(AddPermissionMgt_L.ModifyPurchasingFlexibility) THEN
            CanUpdateLocation_L := FALSE;
        IF CanUpdateLocation_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
          // --> 20150505-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2970-FHS01
          CheckLocationUpdate(recSalesLine_L, recSalesParam_L);
          // <-- 20150505-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2970-FHS01
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", recSASLine_L."Location Code");
        // --> 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
        END;
        // <-- 20150528-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3014-FHS01
      END;

      IF (recSASLine_L."Location Code" = recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code") AND
         (recSASLine_L."Location Code" <> '') AND (recSalesLine_L."Purchasing Code" <> recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Purchasing Code")
      THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Purchasing Code", recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Purchasing Code");
      END;
      // <-- 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2808-FHS01
      after}
      // <-- 20151215-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2178-BSI01

      recSalesLine_L."System Created" := FALSE;

      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Negotiated Price", recSASLine_L."Negociated Price");
      // --> 20150731-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2061-FHS01
      IF recSalesLine_L."Negotiated Price" = TRUE THEN
      // <-- 20150731-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2061-FHS01
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Net Unit Price", recSASLine_L."Unit Price");

      // --> 20170919-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25849-BSI01
      RecItem_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."No.");
      IF RecItem_L."Drop Shipment Status" = RecItem_L."Drop Shipment Status"::Always THEN
        recSalesLine_L."Location Code" := recSalesParam_L."Drop Shipment Location Code";
      // <-- 20170919-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25849-BSI01

      // --> 20161205-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3169-BSI01
      LocationCode_L := recSalesLine_L."Location Code";
      CheckItemBlockingSetupForSAS(recSASLine_L,LocationCode_L);
      // <-- 20161205-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3169-BSI01

      recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

      //Si tout c'est bien pass', on essaie de lancer la commande et de la rouvrir avant d'ins'rer les lignes suivantes
      recHeader_L.GET(recSalesLine_L."Document Type", recSalesLine_L."Document No.");
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1944-FHS01
        recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
        recHeader_L."Is Manual Release" := TRUE;
        recHeader_L.MODIFY;
        // <-- 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1944-FHS01
        // --> 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2806-FHS01
        //cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.PerformManualRelease(recHeader_L); //lancement
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(TRUE);
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
        // <-- 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2806-FHS01
      END;

      //Mise . jour de la liste des GUID
      CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053630, COPYSTR(recSalesLine_L."Document No." + GetSeparator() +
        FORMAT(recSalesLine_L."Line No."), 1, 54), recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");

      //Mise . jour de la ligne de SAS
      recSASLine_L."Document No." := recSalesLine_L."Document No.";
      recSASLine_L."Line No." := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
      recSASLine_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
      recSASLine_L."Synchro Error" := '';
      recSASLine_L.MODIFY;

      EntityName_L := 'salesorder';
      SetState(EntityName_L, recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid", 2); //En attente

      //Mise . jour de la ligne de commande simplifi'e
      EntityName_L := 'salesorderdetail';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpordernum', recSalesLine_L."Document No.");
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erplinenum', FORMAT(recSalesLine_L."Line No."));
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rejectionreason', '');
      IF UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, recSASLine_L."CRM Guid") THEN BEGIN

      END;

      EntityName_L := 'salesorder';
      SetState(EntityName_L, recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid", 100001); //Termin'

      //Sauvegarde de l'int'gration de la ligne de commande
      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckLocationUpdate@8053058(recSalesLine_P@8053003 : Record 37;recSalesSetup_P@8053004 : Record 311);
    VAR
      SalesSetup_L@8053000 : Record 311;
      Text8053001_L@8053001 : TextConst 'FRA=En livraison directe, le magasin doit ^tre le magasin %1.';
      Text8053002_L@8053002 : TextConst 'FRA=Le code magasin %1 est r'serv' . la livraison directe.';
    BEGIN
      // --> 20150505-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2970-FHS01
      IF recSalesLine_P."Drop Shipment" AND (recSalesLine_P."Location Code" <> recSalesSetup_P."Drop Shipment Location Code") THEN
        ERROR(Text8053001_L, recSalesSetup_P."Drop Shipment Location Code");
      IF (NOT recSalesLine_P."Drop Shipment") AND (recSalesLine_P."Location Code" = recSalesSetup_P."Drop Shipment Location Code") THEN
        ERROR(Text8053002_L, recSalesSetup_P."Drop Shipment Location Code");
      // <-- 20150505-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2970-FHS01
    END;
