    END;

    PROCEDURE CleanSAS@8053740();
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053603 : Record 8053610;
      recSASHeaderLine_L@8053600 : Record 8053611;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Effacement des ent^tes qui n'ont plus de ligne . int'grer et des lignes associ'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.RESET;
      recSASHeader_L.SETCURRENTKEY("SAS Status");
      recSASHeader_L.SETRANGE("SAS Status", recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF recSASHeader_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          recSASHeaderLine_L.RESET;
          recSASHeaderLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("SAS Status", "CRM Header Guid");
          recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
          // --> 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
          //recSASHeaderLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASHeaderLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
          recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE("SAS Status", recSASHeaderLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
          recSASHeaderLine_L.MODIFYALL("SAS Status",recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
          // <-- 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
          {
          IF recSASHeaderLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
            recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE("SAS Status");
            recSASHeaderLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("CRM Header Guid");
            recSASHeaderLine_L.DELETEALL;
          END;
          }
        UNTIL recSASHeader_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // --> 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
      //recSASHeader_L.DELETEALL;
      recSASHeader_L.MODIFYALL("SAS Status",recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::Treated);
      // <-- 20170530-WI00-TER01.01.00-3243-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportManualSASOrder@8053751(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053601 : Record 8053610;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Import manuel d'une ligne de commande
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeaderVar_P : enregistrement d'une commande SAS
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetSetup();
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        IF recCRMSetup_G."SAS Manual after Error" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          IF recSASHeader_L."SAS Status" <> recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress" THEN BEGIN
            recSASHeader_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeader_L."SAS Status"::"In Progress";
            recSASHeader_L.MODIFY;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //tentative d'import de la commande
          ImportSimpleOrder(recSASHeader_L);
        END;
      END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportManualSASOrderList@8053752(VAR recSASHeaderVar_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053601 : Record 8053610;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Import manuel d'une liste de commande
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeaderVar_P : enregistrement contenant le filtre . utiliser
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.RESET;
      recSASHeader_L.COPYFILTERS(recSASHeaderVar_P);
      IF recSASHeader_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          ImportManualSASOrder(recSASHeader_L);
        UNTIL recSASHeader_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetTPGContract@8053776(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@8053600 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      StateCode_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      StatusCode_L@8053603 : Text[30];
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recSalesHeader_L@8053605 : Record 8053610;
      recGuid_L@8053606 : Record 8053601;
      recRelation_L@8053607 : Record 5054;
      crmLineCount_L@8053612 : Integer;
      OrderCode_L@8053608 : Code[20];
      LineNo_L@8053611 : Integer;
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053619 : Record 8053608;
      Salesperson_L@8053617 : Code[10];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053618 : Text[50];
      Text_L@8053620 : Text[110];
      Statut_L@8053621 : Code[10];
      Variant_L@8053624 : Boolean;
      VariantNo_L@8053623 : Code[20];
      ItemNo_L@8053622 : Code[20];
      StartDate_L@8053609 : Date;
      EndDate_L@8053613 : Date;
      NbMonth_L@8053614 : Code[10];
      TotalQuantity_L@8053615 : Decimal;
      recSalesLine_L@8053616 : Record 8053611;
      recTemp_L@8053625 : TEMPORARY Record 8053610;
      Guid_L@1000000000 : GUID;
      recDetSalesLine_L@8053000 : Record 8053611;
      recDetSalesLineUpd_L@8053001 : Record 8053611;
      RecCRMGUID2_L@1000000001 : Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation du contrat TPG dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgagreement';

      //Il faut 'viter de recr'er les commandes d'j. int'gr'es
      // --> 20160322-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2874-BSI01
      {before
      recGuid_L.RESET;
      recGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Simple Order Header");
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
      IF NOT(recGuid_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      after}
      CLEAR(RecCRMGUID2_L);
      RecCRMGUID2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data",GUID);
      RecCRMGUID2_L.SETRANGE("Type Data",RecCRMGUID2_L."Type Data"::TPGHeader);
      RecCRMGUID2_L.SETRANGE(GUID,FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P));
      IF NOT(RecCRMGUID2_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      // <-- 20160322-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2874-BSI01
      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountname', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_crmagreementnumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linesgenerated', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_monthsduration', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_totaldeliveredquantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_totalquantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'ownerid', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Statut_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode);
        IF (Statut_L = '175490000') THEN BEGIN //ne synchronise que ceux au statut lanc'
          recRelation_L.RESET;
          recRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "Contact No.", "MC Company Code");
          recRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
          recRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid'), FALSE));
          recRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", recCrmOrganisation_P.Code);
          IF recRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            //Cr'ation de l'ent^te de la commande dans la soci't' . l'origine de la demande
            IF NOT(recSalesHeader_L.GET(FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P))) THEN BEGIN
              recSalesHeader_L.INIT;
              recSalesHeader_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P);
              recSalesHeader_L.Type := recSalesHeader_L.Type::"TPG Contract";
              recSalesHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            END;

            recSalesHeader_L."Read Complete" := TRUE;
            recSalesHeader_L."NoSynchronised Field" := '';
            recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.", recRelation_L."No.");

            recSalesHeader_L."External Document No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_crmagreementnumber'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recSalesHeader_L."External Document No."));

            StartDate_L := FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate'));
            NbMonth_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_monthsduration');
            EndDate_L := FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate'));
            TotalQuantity_L := FormatDecFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_totalquantity'));

            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid');
            Text_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053627, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
            IF GuidFindedWithoutError(Text_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              SearchProductNo(Text_L, Variant_L, ItemNo_L, VariantNo_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //r'cup'ration du num'ro de produit dans la CRM pour le message d'erreur
              Text_L := TxtCrmGuid_L;
              EntityName_L := 'product';
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'productnumber', '');
              IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                Text_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'productnumber');
              END;
              ClearConnector();
              ERROR(Text8053693, Text_L);
            END;

            //Recherche du GUID du vendeur
            Salesperson_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetSalespersonCode(
              GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053631, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ownerid'), FALSE));
            IF Salesperson_L <> '' THEN
              recSalesHeader_L.VALIDATE("Salesperson Code", Salesperson_L);

            recSalesHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
            //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
            CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053629, recSalesHeader_L."No.", CrmGuid_P);
            Result_L := TRUE;

            //Il n'y a pas de ligne
            CLEAR(Guid_L);
            IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesHeader_L."CRM Guid", Guid_L)) THEN BEGIN
              recSalesLine_L.RESET;
              recSalesLine_L.INIT;
              recSalesLine_L."CRM Header Guid" := recSalesHeader_L."CRM Guid";
              recSalesLine_L."CRM Guid" := Guid_L;
              recSalesLine_L."Secondary Header Key" := recSalesHeader_L."Secondary Key";
              recSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            END;
            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("No.", ItemNo_L);
            IF Variant_L = TRUE THEN
              recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", VariantNo_L)
            ELSE
              IF recSalesLine_L."Variant Code" <> '' THEN
                recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", '');

            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity, TotalQuantity_L);
            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Starting Date", StartDate_L);
            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Ending Date", EndDate_L);
            recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := TRUE;
            recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

            //recherche des lignes de la commande
            recTemp_L.DELETEALL;
            EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgagreementline';
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

            //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tpgagreementlineid', '');

            //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tpgagreementid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(CrmGuid_P));

            crmLineCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
            IF crmLineCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
                recTemp_L.INIT;
                recTemp_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_tpgagreementlineid'));
                recTemp_L.INSERT;
              UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
              recTemp_L.RESET;
              IF recTemp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  IF NOT(GetTPGContractLine(recTemp_L."CRM Guid", recSalesHeader_L, Variant_L, ItemNo_L, VariantNo_L)) THEN
                    Result_L := FALSE;
                UNTIL recTemp_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
            END;
            IF Result_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              // --> 20150625-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3083-FHS01
              {
              recDetSalesLine_L.RESET;
              recDetSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSalesHeader_L."CRM Guid");
              recDetSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("CRM Guid", '<>%1', Guid_L);
              IF recDetSalesLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
                  IF ((recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date" < recSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date") OR
                    (recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date" > recSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date")) AND
                    (recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date" > recSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date") AND
                    (recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date" < recSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date")
                  THEN BEGIN
                    recDetSalesLineUpd_L := recDetSalesLine_L;
                    IF (recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date" < recSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date") THEN
                      recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date" := recSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date";
                    IF (recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date" > recSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date") THEN
                      recDetSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date" := recSalesLine_L."Contract Ending Date";
                    recDetSalesLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
                  END;
                UNTIL recDetSalesLine_L.NEXT = 0;
              }
              // <-- 20150625-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3083-FHS01

              COMMIT;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            Text_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountname');
            ClearConnector();
            ERROR(Text8053647, Text_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //si la commande n'est pas au statut permettant la synchronisation, on ne traite pas cet ordre
          Result_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //La commande TPG a d'j. 't' supprim'e
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetTPGContractLine@8053775(CrmGuid_P@8053601 : Text[50];recSalesHeader_P@8053602 : Record 8053610;Variant_P@8053615 : Boolean;ItemNo_P@8053604 : Code[20];VariantNo_P@8053614 : Code[20]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      recSalesLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053611;
      recGuid_L@8053605 : Record 8053601;
      Quantity_L@8053603 : Decimal;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053624 : Text[50];
      Month_L@8053600 : Integer;
      Year_L@8053617 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation des lignes de la commande simplifi'e dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID du compte (contact) . synchroniser
      //    - recSalesHeader_P : Ent^te de commandde de SAS
      //    - Variant_P : Variant ou non
      //    - ItemNo_P : nu'mro de produit
      //    - VariantNo_P := No de variant
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgagreementline';

      ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveredquantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_month', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_standardprice', '');
      SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_year', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        //Cr'ation de l'ent^te de la commande dans la soci't' . l'origine de la demande
        IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.GET(recSalesHeader_P."CRM Guid", FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P))) THEN BEGIN
          recSalesLine_L.RESET;
          recSalesLine_L.INIT;
          recSalesLine_L."CRM Header Guid" := recSalesHeader_P."CRM Guid";
          recSalesLine_L."CRM Guid" := FormatGuid(CrmGuid_P);
          recSalesLine_L."Secondary Header Key" := recSalesHeader_P."Secondary Key";
          recSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END;

        recSalesLine_L."Read Complete" := TRUE;
        recSalesLine_L."NoSynchronised Field" := '';

        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("No.", ItemNo_P);
        IF Variant_P = TRUE THEN
          recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", VariantNo_P)
        ELSE
          IF recSalesLine_L."Variant Code" <> '' THEN
            recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", '');
        Quantity_L := FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity'), 1, 30));
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity, Quantity_L);

        EVALUATE(Month_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_month'));
        EVALUATE(Year_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_year'));
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Starting Date", DMY2DATE(1, Month_L, Year_L));
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Ending Date", CALCDATE('<CD + 1M - 1D>', recSalesLine_L."Contract Starting Date"));

        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Unit Price", FormatDecFromCrm(COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_standardprice'), 1, 30)));
        recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        Result_L := TRUE;
        COMMIT;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertTPGContract@8053778(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053601 : Record 8053610;
      recHeader_L@8053602 : Record 36;
      Modify_L@8053603 : Boolean;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053611;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053607 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduImportLine_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recLineUpd_L@8053609 : Record 8053611;
      StatutCode_L@8053610 : Code[10];
      Statut_L@8053612 : Integer;
      EntityName_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053613 : Codeunit 414;
      recSalesLine_L@8053614 : Record 37;
      ApprovalManagement_L@8053000 : Codeunit 439;
      MailMgt_L@8053003 : Codeunit 8060999;
      Module_L@8053002 : 'Purchase,Sales,Service,Reminder,Finance Charge';
      DocumentType_L@8053001 : ' ,Quote,Order,Return Order,Blanket Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Shipment,Receipt';
      recCustomer_L@1000000000 : Record 18;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une commande du SAS (contrat TPG)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeader_P : ent^te de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //v'rification ent^te complSte
      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
      GetSetup();

      //Une commande en cours existe d'j.
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      IF recSASHeader_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            //R'ouverture de la commande pour int'grer la suite
            CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
            cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(recHeader_L."No.");
            cduRunOrder_L.SetReopenOnly(TRUE);
            // --> 20150729-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2045-FHS01
            cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter2(1);
            // <-- 20150729-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2045-FHS01
            IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
              ERROR(Text8053680, GETLASTERRORTEXT());
            END;
            // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
            COMMIT;
            // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
          END;
          Modify_L := TRUE;
          recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.");
        END;
      END;

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ent^te
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        recHeader_L.INIT;
        recHeader_L."Document Type" := recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order";
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", WORKDATE);
        recHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        recSASHeader_L."No." := recHeader_L."No.";
        recSASHeader_L.MODIFY;
      END;

      IF recHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." THEN BEGIN
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.", recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
        // --> 20150727-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2024-FHS01
          recCustomer_L.GET(recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
          IF recCustomer_L."Group Account Code" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Group Account Code", recCustomer_L."Group Account Code");
        END;
        // <-- 20150727-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2024-FHS01

      recHeader_L.VALIDATE("External Document No.", recSASHeader_L."External Document No.");
      {
      // --> 20150518-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1838-FHS01
      IF recHeader_L."Document Source" <> recCRMSetup_G."TPG Document Source" THEN
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Source", recCRMSetup_G."TPG Document Source");
      // <-- 20150518-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1838-FHS01
      }

      IF (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> '') AND (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> recHeader_L."Salesperson Code") THEN
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Salesperson Code", recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code");
      recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Responsibility Center", recCRMSetup_G."TPG Responsibility Center");
      recHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

      //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
      CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053694, recHeader_L."No.", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      COMMIT;

      //recherche des lignes de la commande
      CLEAR(recLine_L."CRM Guid");
      IF recLine_L.GET(recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid") THEN BEGIN
        //recherche des lignes de la commande
        IF recLine_L."SAS Status" <> recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created THEN BEGIN
          //tentative d'import de la ligne
          CLEAR(cduImportLine_L);
          // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::BlanketOrderLine, NavBPMConnector_G);
          //AFTER
          cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::BlanketOrderLine, NavBPMConnector_G, NavBPMConnector2_G);
          // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterOrderLine(recLine_L);
          IF NOT(cduImportLine_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Error;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          END;
          recLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
          COMMIT;
        END;
      END;

      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");

      // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
      //on relance la commande
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.RESET;
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recHeader_L."Document Type");
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recHeader_L."No.");
        IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
        END;
      END ELSE
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        MailMgt_L.InsertRec(recHeader_L."No.", Module_L::Sales, DocumentType_L::Order);
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        ApprovalManagement_L.CancelSalesLineAppReqOnDocRel(recHeader_L);
      // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01

      //COMMIT car on vient d'int'grer ce que l'on peut sans erreur et qu'il reste des lignes . int'grer
      recLine_L.RESET;
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF NOT(recLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        COMMIT;
        ERROR(Text8053683);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertTPGContractLine@8053777(recSASLine_P@8053600 : Record 8053611);
    VAR
      recSASLine_L@8053602 : Record 8053611;
      recSASHeader_L@8053603 : Record 8053610;
      recSalesLine_L@8053604 : Record 37;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053609 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053601 : Codeunit 414;
      recHeader_L@8053612 : Record 36;
      EntityName_L@8053000 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      cduImportLine_L@8053606 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recLineUpd_L@8053607 : Record 8053611;
      SalesPriceDiscMgt_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053017;
      AllOk_L@8053610 : Boolean;
      recLineDetails_L@8053611 : Record 8053170;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une ligne de commande de SAS (TPG avec GUID ligne . NULL)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASLine_P : ligne de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid");
      recSASLine_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_P."CRM Guid");
      GetSetup();

      //Recherche du prochain num'ro de ligne
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      LineNo_L := 10000;
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recSalesLine_L.Type::Item);
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("No.", recSASLine_L."No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code", recSASLine_L."Variant Code");
      IF recSalesLine_L.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
        LineNo_L := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
        Modify_L := TRUE;
      END;

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ligne
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.INIT;
        recSalesLine_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
        recSalesLine_L."Document Type" := recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order";
        recSalesLine_L."Document No." := recSASHeader_L."No.";
        recSalesLine_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
        recSalesLine_L.Type := recSalesLine_L.Type::Item;
        //Insertion de la ligne
        recSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
      END;

      recSalesLine_L."System Created" := TRUE;
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("No.", recSASLine_L."No.");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", recSASLine_L."Variant Code");

      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity, recSASLine_L.Quantity);
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Starting Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Starting Date");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Ending Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Ending Date");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Starting Date");

      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Responsibility Center", recCRMSetup_G."TPG Responsibility Center");

      recSalesLine_L."System Created" := FALSE;
      recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

      //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
      CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053695, recSalesLine_L."Document No." + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recSalesLine_L."Line No."),
        recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");

      //Si tout c'est bien pass', on essaie de lancer la commande et de la rouvrir avant d'ins'rer les lignes suivantes
      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.PerformManualRelease(recHeader_L); //lancement
        //AFTER
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
      END;
      COMMIT;
      //R'ouverture si la commande est lanc'e
      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Open THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2806-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.PerformManualReopen(recHeader_L); //r'ouverture
        //AFTER
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.Reopen(recHeader_L); //r'ouverture
        // <-- 20150302-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2806-FHS01
      END;

      //Mise . jour de la ligne de SAS
      recSASLine_L."Document No." := recSalesLine_L."Document No.";
      recSASLine_L."Line No." := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
      //recSASLine_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
      recSASLine_L."Synchro Error" := '';
      recSASLine_L.MODIFY;
      //Sauvegarde de l'int'gration de la ligne de commande
      COMMIT;

      //recherche des lignes de la commande
      AllOk_L := TRUE;
      CLEAR(recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
      recSASLine_L.RESET;
      recSASLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      recSASLine_L.SETFILTER(recSASLine_L."CRM Guid", '<>%1', recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
      recSASLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF recSASLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //tentative d'import de la ligne
          CLEAR(cduImportLine_L);
          // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::BlanketOrderSubLine, NavBPMConnector_G);
          //AFTER
          cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::BlanketOrderSubLine, NavBPMConnector_G, NavBPMConnector2_G);
          // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterOrderLine(recSASLine_L);
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterBlanketOrderLine(recSalesLine_L);
          IF NOT(cduImportLine_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recSASLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Error;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
            AllOk_L := FALSE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recSASLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          END;
          recLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
          COMMIT;
        UNTIL recSASLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      IF AllOk_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        //Si tout c'est bien pass', on essaie de lancer la commande
        recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recHeader_L."No.");
        IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.PerformManualRelease(recHeader_L); //lancement
          //AFTER
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
          // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2863-FHS01
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        ERROR(Text8053696);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertTPGContractSubLine@8053780(recSASLine_P@8053600 : Record 8053611;recLine_P@8053606 : Record 37);
    VAR
      recSASLine_L@8053602 : Record 8053611;
      recSASHeader_L@8053603 : Record 8053610;
      recSalesLine_L@8053604 : Record 37;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053609 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053601 : Codeunit 414;
      recHeader_L@8053612 : Record 36;
      EntityName_L@8053000 : Text[50];
      recLine_L@8053614 : Record 8053170;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une ligne de quantit' d'une ligne de commande ouverte
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASLine_P : ligne de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //    - recLine_P : Ligne de commande de vente ouverte
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid");
      recSASLine_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_P."CRM Guid");
      recSalesLine_L.GET(recLine_P."Document Type", recLine_P."Document No.", recLine_P."Line No.");

      IF NOT(recLine_L.GET(recLine_L."Sales/Purchase"::Sales, recLine_L."Line Type"::Both, recSalesLine_L."Document No.",
        recSalesLine_L."Line No.", recSASLine_L."Contract Starting Date"))
      THEN BEGIN
        recLine_L.INIT;
        recLine_L."Sales/Purchase" := recLine_L."Sales/Purchase"::Sales;
        recLine_L."Line Type" := recLine_L."Line Type"::Both;
        recLine_L."Blanket Order No." := recSalesLine_L."Document No.";
        recLine_L."Blanket Order Line No." := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
        recLine_L."Starting Date" := recSASLine_L."Contract Starting Date";
        recLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
      END;

      recLine_L.VALIDATE("Ending Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Ending Date");
      recLine_L.VALIDATE(Type, recLine_L.Type::Item);
      recLine_L.VALIDATE("No.", recSalesLine_L."No.");
      recLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", recSalesLine_L."Variant Code");
      recLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity, recSASLine_L.Quantity);
      recLine_L.VALIDATE("Price/Cost", recSASLine_L."Unit Price");
      recLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

      //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
      CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053695, recLine_L."Blanket Order No." + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recLine_L."Blanket Order Line No.") +
        GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recLine_L."Starting Date", 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>'), recSASLine_P."CRM Guid");

      //Mise . jour de la ligne de SAS
      recSASLine_L."Document No." := recSalesLine_L."Document No.";
      recSASLine_L."Line No." := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
      recSASLine_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
      recSASLine_L."Synchro Error" := '';
      recSASLine_L.MODIFY;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAvailableSeason@8053064(VAR TmpSeason2_L@8053000 : Record 8053154;Campagne_P@8053600 : Code[10];Rec@8053601 : Record 8053611);
    VAR
      Season_L@8053001 : Record 8053154;
      TmpCampaign_L@1000000000 : TEMPORARY Record 8052991;
      i_L@1000000001 : Integer;
      LevelFound_L@1000000002 : Boolean;
      TmpSeason_L@1000000003 : TEMPORARY Record 8053154;
      recSalesHeader_L@8053602 : Record 8053610;
      recItem_L@8053603 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053604 : Record 5401;
      DateRef_L@1000000004 : Date;
      RecNavSalesHeader_L@1000000005 : Record 36;
      TableauType_L@1000000006 : ARRAY [6] OF Integer;
    BEGIN
      recSalesHeader_L.GET(Rec."CRM Header Guid");
      // --> 20211105-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3580-CJY01
      DateRef_L := 0D;
      IF RecNavSalesHeader_L.GET(RecNavSalesHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, recSalesHeader_L."No.") THEN
        DateRef_L := RecNavSalesHeader_L."Order Date";
      //si on a pas r'ussis . obtenir de date, on utilise la WORKDATE
      IF DateRef_L = 0D THEN
        DateRef_L := WORKDATE;

      // <-- 20211105-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3580-CJY01

      TmpSeason_L.RESET;
      TmpSeason_L.DELETEALL;

      TmpCampaign_L.RESET;
      TmpCampaign_L.DELETEALL;

      Season_L.RESET;
      Season_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"No.","Variant Code","Order Starting Date");
      // --> 20211105-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3580-CJY01
      {BEFORE
      Season_L.SETFILTER("Order Starting Date", '<=%1', WORKDATE);
      Season_L.SETFILTER("Order Ending Date", '>=%1', WORKDATE);
      AFTER}
      Season_L.SETFILTER("Order Starting Date", '<=%1', DateRef_L);
      Season_L.SETFILTER("Order Ending Date", '>=%1', DateRef_L);

      // <-- 20211105-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3580-CJY01

      IF Campagne_P <> '' THEN
        Season_L.SETRANGE(Season_L."Campaign No.", Campagne_P);
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Unactive,FALSE);

      recItem_L.GET(Rec."No.");
      //Search by Item and Variant Code
      IF Rec."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recVariant_L.GET(Rec."No.", Rec."Variant Code");
        Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Item);
        Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", Rec."No.");
        Season_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code", Rec."Variant Code");
        IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            TmpSeason_L.INIT;
            TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
            TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
            TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
            TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
            IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
          UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      //Search by Item
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Item);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", Rec."No.");
      Season_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          TmpSeason_L.INIT;
          TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
          TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
          TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
          TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
          IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
        UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;

      // --> 20230315-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3614-CJY01
      //search by rate code
      IF recItem_L."Code tarif" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::"Rate Code");
        Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Code tarif");
        IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            TmpSeason_L.INIT;
            TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
            TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
            TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
            TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
            IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
          UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // <-- 20230315-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3614-CJY01

      //Search by Species
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Species);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Species Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          TmpSeason_L.INIT;
          TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
          TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
          TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
          TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
          IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
        UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;

      //Search by Subfamily
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Subfamily);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Sub-Family Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          TmpSeason_L.INIT;
          TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
          TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
          TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
          TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
          IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
        UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;

      //Search by Family
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Family);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Family Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          TmpSeason_L.INIT;
          TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
          TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
          TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
          TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
          IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
        UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;

      //Search by Activity
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Activity);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Activity Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          TmpSeason_L.INIT;
          TmpSeason_L := Season_L;
          TmpSeason_L.INSERT;
          TmpCampaign_L.INIT;
          TmpCampaign_L."No." := Season_L."Campaign No.";
          IF TmpCampaign_L.INSERT THEN;
        UNTIL Season_L.NEXT = 0;
      // --> 20230315-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3614-CJY01
      {BEFORE
      IF TmpCampaign_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          LevelFound_L := FALSE;
          FOR i_L:=5 DOWNTO 1 DO
            IF LevelFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              TmpSeason_L.RESET;
              TmpSeason_L.SETRANGE(Type,i_L-1);
              TmpSeason_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.",TmpCampaign_L."No.");
              IF TmpSeason_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  TmpSeason2_L.INIT;
                  TmpSeason2_L := TmpSeason_L;
                  TmpSeason2_L.INSERT;
                UNTIL TmpSeason_L.NEXT = 0;
                LevelFound_L := TRUE;
              END;
            END;
        UNTIL TmpCampaign_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      AFTER}
      TableauType_L[6] := 4;
      TableauType_L[5] := 5;
      TableauType_L[4] := 3;
      TableauType_L[3] := 2;
      TableauType_L[2] := 1;
      TableauType_L[1] := 0;

      IF TmpCampaign_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          LevelFound_L := FALSE;
          FOR i_L:=6 DOWNTO 1 DO
            IF LevelFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              TmpSeason_L.RESET;
              TmpSeason_L.SETRANGE(Type,TableauType_L[i_L]);
              TmpSeason_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.",TmpCampaign_L."No.");
              IF TmpSeason_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  TmpSeason2_L.INIT;
                  TmpSeason2_L := TmpSeason_L;
                  TmpSeason2_L.INSERT;
                UNTIL TmpSeason_L.NEXT = 0;
                LevelFound_L := TRUE;
              END;
            END;
        UNTIL TmpCampaign_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      // <-- 20230315-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3614-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<--Initialisation-->"@8053691();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitContactList@8053692();
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      Count_L@8053601 : Integer;
      recJournalInit_L@8053602 : Record 8053605;
      i_L@8053603 : Integer;
      recContact_L@8053604 : Record 5050;
      CodeCrm_L@8053605 : Text[20];
      GuidTxt_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Name_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      Fin_L@8053608 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // initialisation de la synchronisation des comptes (CRM) avec les contacts (Nav)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        //1Sre 'tape : lecture dans le CRM
        Name_L := '';
        GuidTxt_L := '';
        Fin_L := FALSE;
        EntityName_L := 'account';
        REPEAT
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'accountid', ''); //GUID
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber', ''); //Code
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', ''); //Description
          IF GuidTxt_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'accountid', '>', CleanGuidToCRM(GuidTxt_L)); //CleanGuidToCRM(GuidTxt_L));
          END;
          SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, 'accountid');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          IF Count_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
            Fin_L := TRUE;
          END;
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 4999, 1, '='));

            CodeCrm_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber'), 1, 20);
            GuidTxt_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'accountid'));
            Name_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'name'), 1, 50);

            IF CodeCrm_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              //cherche dans le journal par le code
              recJournalInit_L.RESET;
              recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM", Type);
              recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE("Code CRM", CodeCrm_L);
              recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
              IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                IF recJournalInit_L.Guid <> GuidTxt_L THEN
                  recJournalInit_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, GuidTxt_L);
                recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := Name_L;
                recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                //cherche si le compte utilis' est un compte existant
                recJournalInit_L.RESET;
                recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
                recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Code, CodeCrm_L);
                IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                  IF recJournalInit_L.Guid <> GuidTxt_L THEN
                    recJournalInit_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code,
                      GuidTxt_L);
                  recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := CodeCrm_L;
                  recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := Name_L;
                  recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                  recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                  recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  //cherche dans le journal par le GUID
                  recJournalInit_L.RESET;
                  recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
                  recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Guid, GuidTxt_L);
                  IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := CodeCrm_L;
                    recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := Name_L;
                    recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                    recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                    recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    //cr'ation
                    CLEAR(recJournalInit_L);
                    recJournalInit_L.INIT;
                    recJournalInit_L.Type := recJournalInit_L.Type::Account;
                    recJournalInit_L.Guid := GuidTxt_L;
                    recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := CodeCrm_L;
                    recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := Name_L;
                    recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                    recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                    recJournalInit_L.INSERT;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //cherche dans le journal par le GUID
              recJournalInit_L.RESET;
              recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
              recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Guid, GuidTxt_L);
              IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := CodeCrm_L;
                recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := Name_L;
                recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                //cr'ation
                CLEAR(recJournalInit_L);
                recJournalInit_L.INIT;
                recJournalInit_L.Type := recJournalInit_L.Type::Account;
                recJournalInit_L.Guid := GuidTxt_L;
                recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := CodeCrm_L;
                recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := Name_L;
                recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                recJournalInit_L.INSERT;
              END;
            END;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        UNTIL Fin_L;

        //2Sme 'tape : ajout des donn'es NAV sans correspondance
        //recherche dans les contacts
        recContact_L.RESET;
        Count_L := recContact_L.COUNT;
        i_L := 0;
        IF recContact_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 5000 + 4999, 1, '='));
          IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            recJournalInit_L.RESET;
            recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
            recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Code, recContact_L."No.");
            IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
              recJournalInit_L."Description Nav" := recContact_L.Name;
              recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
              recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recJournalInit_L.RESET;
              recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM", Type);
              recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE("Code CRM", recContact_L."No.");
              recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
              IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                IF recJournalInit_L.Code <> recContact_L."No." THEN
                  recJournalInit_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, recContact_L."No.", recJournalInit_L.Guid);
                recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                recJournalInit_L."Description Nav" := recContact_L.Name;
                recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                CLEAR(recJournalInit_L);
                recJournalInit_L.INIT;
                recJournalInit_L.Type := recJournalInit_L.Type::Account;
                recJournalInit_L.Code := recContact_L."No.";
                recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                recJournalInit_L."Description Nav" := recContact_L.Name;
                recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
                recJournalInit_L.INSERT;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL recContact_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitSalesListFromCrm@8053694();
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      Count_L@8053602 : Integer;
      recJournalInit_L@8053601 : Record 8053605;
      recSalesOrder_L@8053600 : Record 36;
      recArchSalesOrder_L@8053604 : Record 5107;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      LinkedGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      CrmCode_L@8053607 : Text[20];
      Finded_L@8053608 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Initialisation des commandes simplifi'es (CRM) pour importer des commandes
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
      //1Sre 'tape : lecture dans le CRM
        EntityName_L := 'salesorder';
        ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid', ''); //GUID
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber', ''); //N› de commande
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber', ''); //Doc No
        SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_valid_order', ''); //lien 'ventuel vers une commande valid'e
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '100001'); //Synchronis' avec Nav
        Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
        i_L := 0;
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 3999, 1, '='));
          //cherche dans le journal par le code
          Finded_L := FALSE;
          CrmCode_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber'), 1, 20);

          IF CrmCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            //L'enregistrement CRM doit avoir le code de renseign' pour le lier . une commande existante
            recJournalInit_L.RESET;
            recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM", Type);
            recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE("Code CRM", CrmCode_L);
            recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
            IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              Finded_L := TRUE;
              IF recJournalInit_L.Guid <> FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid')) THEN
              recJournalInit_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code,
                FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid')));
              recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber'), 1, 50);
              LinkedGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_valid_order');
              IF LinkedGuid_L <> '' THEN
                recJournalInit_L."Linked To" := FormatGuid(LinkedGuid_L);
              recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
              recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
              recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;

          IF Finded_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            //cherche dans le journal par le GUID
            recJournalInit_L.RESET;
            recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
            recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Guid, FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid')));
            IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber'), 1, 20);
              recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber'), 1, 50);
              LinkedGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_valid_order');
              IF LinkedGuid_L <> '' THEN
                recJournalInit_L."Linked To" := FormatGuid(LinkedGuid_L);
              recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
              recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
              recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //cr'ation
              CLEAR(recJournalInit_L);
              recJournalInit_L.INIT;
              recJournalInit_L.Type := recJournalInit_L.Type::"Simple Order";
              recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber'), 1, 20);
              recJournalInit_L.Guid := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'salesorderid'));
              recJournalInit_L."Description CRM" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber'), 1, 50);
              LinkedGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_valid_order');
              IF LinkedGuid_L <> '' THEN
                recJournalInit_L."Linked To" := FormatGuid(LinkedGuid_L);
              recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
              recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
              recJournalInit_L.INSERT;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));

        //2Sme 'tape : recherche des correspondances
        //recherche dans les commandes ouvertes
        CLEAR(recSalesOrder_L);
        recSalesOrder_L.INIT;
        recSalesOrder_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Sell-to Customer No.","External Document No.", "Document Type");
        recSalesOrder_L.SETFILTER("Sell-to Customer No.", '<>%1', '');
        recSalesOrder_L.SETFILTER("External Document No.", '<>%1', '');
        recSalesOrder_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesOrder_L."Document Type"::Order);
        i_L := 0;
        Count_L := recSalesOrder_L.COUNT;
        IF recSalesOrder_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 3000 + 3999, 1, '='));
          recJournalInit_L.RESET;
          recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM", Type);
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE("Code CRM", recSalesOrder_L."No.");
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
          IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            IF recJournalInit_L.Code <> recSalesOrder_L."No." THEN
              recJournalInit_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, recSalesOrder_L."No.", recJournalInit_L.Guid);
            recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
            recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
            recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL recSalesOrder_L.NEXT = 0;

        //recherche dans les commandes archiv'es
        recArchSalesOrder_L.RESET;
        recArchSalesOrder_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recArchSalesOrder_L."Document Type"::Order);
        recArchSalesOrder_L.SETFILTER("External Document No.", '<>%1', '');
        i_L := 0;
        Count_L := recArchSalesOrder_L.COUNT;
        IF recArchSalesOrder_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 3000 + 6999, 1, '='));
          recJournalInit_L.RESET;
          recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM", Type);
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE("Code CRM", recArchSalesOrder_L."No.");
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
          IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            IF recJournalInit_L.Code <> recArchSalesOrder_L."No." THEN
              recJournalInit_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, recArchSalesOrder_L."No.", recJournalInit_L.Guid);
            recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
            recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
            recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL recArchSalesOrder_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitSalesListToCrm@8053695();
    VAR
      recJournalInit_L@8053602 : Record 8053605;
      recSalesOrder_L@8053601 : Record 36;
      recArchSalesOrder_L@8053600 : Record 5107;
      i_L@8053603 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053604 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Initialisation des commandes valid'es (CRM) par Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
      //1Sre 'tape : ajout des donn'es NAV sans correspondance
        //recherche dans les commandes ouvertes
        recSalesOrder_L.RESET;
        recSalesOrder_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesOrder_L."Document Type"::Order);
        recSalesOrder_L.SETRANGE(Status, recSalesOrder_L.Status::Released);
        i_L := 0;
        Count_L := recSalesOrder_L.COUNT;
        IF recSalesOrder_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 4999, 1, '='));
          recJournalInit_L.RESET;
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Validated Order");
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Code, recSalesOrder_L."No.");
          IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
            recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
            recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            CLEAR(recJournalInit_L);
            recJournalInit_L.INIT;
            recJournalInit_L.Type := recJournalInit_L.Type::"Validated Order";
            recJournalInit_L.Code := recSalesOrder_L."No.";
            recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
            recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
            recJournalInit_L.INSERT;
          END;
        UNTIL recSalesOrder_L.NEXT = 0;

        //recherche dans les commandes archiv'es
        recArchSalesOrder_L.RESET;
        recArchSalesOrder_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recArchSalesOrder_L."Document Type"::Order);
        i_L := 0;
        Count_L := recArchSalesOrder_L.COUNT;
        IF recArchSalesOrder_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 5000 + 4999, 1, '='));
          recJournalInit_L.RESET;
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Validated Order");
          recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Code, recArchSalesOrder_L."No.");
          IF recJournalInit_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
            recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
            recJournalInit_L.MODIFY;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            CLEAR(recJournalInit_L);
            recJournalInit_L.INIT;
            recJournalInit_L.Type := recJournalInit_L.Type::"Validated Order";
            recJournalInit_L.Code := recArchSalesOrder_L."No.";
            recJournalInit_L.State := recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize";
            recJournalInit_L.New := TRUE;
            recJournalInit_L.INSERT;
          END;
        UNTIL recArchSalesOrder_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitContact@8053704(recCrmOrganisation_P@8053607 : Record 8053075);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      Count_L@8053603 : Integer;
      recJournalInit_L@8053602 : Record 8053605;
      recContact_L@8053601 : Record 5050;
      i_L@8053600 : Integer;
      recJournalInitUpd_L@8053605 : Record 8053605;
      recGuid_L@8053606 : Record 8053601;
      recContactRelation_L@8053608 : Record 5054;
      recCustomer_L@8053609 : Record 18;
      recVendor_L@8053610 : Record 23;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lance la synchronisation de l'initialisation des contacts entre Nav et MS CRM depuis le journal d'initialisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Soci't' . l'origine de la demande
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Synchronisation des comptes (CRM) avec les contacts (Nav)
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        i_L := 0;
        recJournalInit_L.RESET;
        recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY(State, New, Action, "Linked To", Type);
        recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(State, recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize");
        recJournalInit_L.SETFILTER(Action, '%1|%2', recJournalInit_L.Action::Delete, recJournalInit_L.Action::Synchronize);
        recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::Account);
        Count_L := recJournalInit_L.COUNT;
        IF recJournalInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '='));
            IF recJournalInit_L.Action = recJournalInit_L.Action::Delete THEN BEGIN
              //Suppression
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recJournalInit_L.Guid)) THEN
                DeleteEntityFromNav('account', recJournalInit_L.Guid, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Account, 2, 1);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //Synchronisation
              recJournalInitUpd_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recJournalInit_L.Guid)) THEN BEGIN
                //ajout du GUID dans la liste
                IF recJournalInit_L.Code <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
                  IF recContact_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
                    recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := recJournalInit_L.Guid;
                    recContact_L.MODIFY;

                    //recherche du client
                    recContactRelation_L.RESET;
                    recContactRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "Contact No.", "MC Company Code");
                    recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
                    recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", recJournalInit_L.Code);
                    recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", recCrmOrganisation_P.Code);
                    IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                      IF recCustomer_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
                        recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := recJournalInit_L.Guid;
                        recCustomer_L.MODIFY;
                      END;
                    END;

                    //Recherche du fournisseur
                    recContactRelation_L.RESET;
                    recContactRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "Contact No.", "MC Company Code");
                    recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
                    recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", recJournalInit_L.Code);
                    recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", recCrmOrganisation_P.Code);
                    IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                      IF recVendor_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
                        recVendor_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := recJournalInit_L.Guid;
                        recVendor_L.MODIFY;
                      END;
                    END;
                  END;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  EntityName_L := 'account';
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationstate', '175490000'); //synchronised = demande de synchro
                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
                END;

                //cr'ation du Tiers MS . cr'er
                ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_ := GetTierMSFromCrm(recJournalInit_L.Guid, recCrmOrganisation_P);
              END;
              IF recJournalInit_L.Code <> '' THEN BEGIN
                IF recContact_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
                  AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recContact_L."No.", TextModify, '');
                END;
              END;
            END;
            //Mise . jour de la ligne du journal pour indiquer quelle a 't' synchronis'e
            IF recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_ = '' THEN BEGIN
              recJournalInitUpd_L.State := recJournalInitUpd_L.State::Synchronized;
              recJournalInitUpd_L.New := FALSE;
              recJournalInitUpd_L.Action := recJournalInitUpd_L.Action::" ";
            END;
            recJournalInitUpd_L.MODIFY;
            COMMIT;
          UNTIL recJournalInit_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitSimpleOrder@8053705(recCrmOrganisation_P@8053608 : Record 8053075);
    VAR
      recJournalInit_L@8053600 : Record 8053605;
      recJournalInitUpd_L@8053604 : Record 8053605;
      SalesHeaderCode_L@8053606 : Code[20];
      recOrder_L@8053603 : Record 36;
      recArchOrder_L@8053602 : Record 5107;
      Finded_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053607 : Integer;
      cduInit_L@8053609 : Codeunit 8053606;
      recRef_L@8053610 : RecordRef;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lance la synchronisation de l'initialisation des commandes simplifi'es entre Nav et MS CRM depuis le journal d'initialisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Soci't' . l'origine de la demande
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Synchronisation des comptes (CRM) avec les contacts (Nav)
      i_L := 0;
      recJournalInit_L.RESET;
      recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY(State, New, Action, "Linked To", Type);
      recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(State, recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize");
      recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Action, recJournalInit_L.Action::Synchronize);
      recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
      Count_L := recJournalInit_L.COUNT;
      IF recJournalInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '='));
          Finded_L := FALSE;
          //Synchronisation
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recJournalInit_L.Guid)) THEN BEGIN
            IF recJournalInit_L.Code <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF recOrder_L.GET(recOrder_L."Document Type"::Order, recJournalInit_L.Code) THEN
                Finded_L := TRUE;
            END;
            IF recJournalInit_L."Code CRM" <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF recOrder_L.GET(recOrder_L."Document Type"::Order, recJournalInit_L."Code CRM") THEN
                Finded_L := TRUE;
            END;
          END;

          IF Finded_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            cduInit_L.SetParameter(1, recJournalInit_L.Guid, recCrmOrganisation_P, recRef_L);
            IF cduInit_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
              //Mise . jour de la ligne du journal pour indiquer quelle a 't' synchronis'e
              recJournalInitUpd_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
              IF recJournalInitUpd_L.Code <> SalesHeaderCode_L THEN
                recJournalInitUpd_L.RENAME(recJournalInit_L.Type, SalesHeaderCode_L, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
              recJournalInitUpd_L.State := recJournalInitUpd_L.State::Synchronized;
              recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_ := '';
              recJournalInitUpd_L.Action := recJournalInitUpd_L.Action::" ";
              recJournalInitUpd_L.New := FALSE;
              recJournalInitUpd_L.MODIFY;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //il y a une eu une erreur
              recJournalInitUpd_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
              recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_ := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_));
              recJournalInitUpd_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //la commande existe d'j.
            recJournalInitUpd_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
            recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_ := Text8053646;
            recJournalInitUpd_L.MODIFY;
          END;
          COMMIT;
        UNTIL recJournalInit_L.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitValidOrder@8053707();
    VAR
      recJournalInit_L@8053603 : Record 8053605;
      recJournalInitUpd_L@8053600 : Record 8053605;
      RecRef_L@8053601 : RecordRef;
      recOrder_L@8053602 : Record 36;
      recArchOrder_L@8053604 : Record 5107;
      Finded_L@8053605 : Boolean;
      i_L@8053606 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053607 : Integer;
      cduInit_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053609 : Record 8053075;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lance la synchronisation de l'initialisation des commandes valid'es entre Nav et MS CRM depuis le journal d'initialisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      i_L := 0;
      recJournalInit_L.RESET;
      recJournalInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY(State, New, Action, "Linked To", Type);
      recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(State, recJournalInit_L.State::"To Synchronize");
      recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Action, recJournalInit_L.Action::Synchronize);
      recJournalInit_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalInit_L.Type::"Validated Order");
      Count_L := recJournalInit_L.COUNT;
      IF recJournalInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '='));
          Finded_L := FALSE;
          IF recOrder_L.GET(recOrder_L."Document Type"::Order, recJournalInit_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
            //Synchronisation d'une commande ouverte
            RecRef_L.GETTABLE(recOrder_L);
            RecRef_L.SETPOSITION(recOrder_L.GETPOSITION);
            Finded_L := TRUE;
          END;
          IF Finded_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            cduInit_L.SetParameter(2, '', recCrmOrganisation_L, RecRef_L);
            IF cduInit_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
              //Mise . jour de la ligne du journal pour indiquer quelle a 't' synchronis'e
              recJournalInitUpd_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
              recJournalInitUpd_L.State := recJournalInitUpd_L.State::Synchronized;
              recJournalInitUpd_L.New := FALSE;
              recJournalInitUpd_L.Action := recJournalInitUpd_L.Action::" ";
              recJournalInitUpd_L.MODIFY;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //il y a une eu une erreur
              recJournalInitUpd_L.GET(recJournalInit_L.Type, recJournalInit_L.Code, recJournalInit_L.Guid);
              recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_ := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalInitUpd_L.Error_));
              recJournalInitUpd_L.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
          COMMIT;
        UNTIL recJournalInit_L.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunSynchroInitList@8053696();

      recJournalInit_L@8053600 : Record 8053605;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lance la synchronisation de l'initialisation entre Nav et MS CRM en remplissant le journal d'initialisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      OpenWindow();

      NewStepWindow(2);

      NewStepWindow(3);
