    END;

    PROCEDURE RunSynchroInitFromList@8053706();
    VAR
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053601 : Record 8053075;
      CodeCompany_L@8053602 : Code[10];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lance la synchronisation de l'initialisation entre Nav et MS CRM en fonction du journal d'initialisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF CONFIRM(Text8053644) = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        //s'il y a des commandes de ventes valid'es dans le CRM, c'est que l'initialisation a d'j. 't' effecut'e
        OpenWindow();
        NewStepWindow(1);

        CodeCompany_L := GetCompanyMcCode();
        recCrmOrganisation_L.GET(CodeCompany_L);

        recCRMSetup_G.GET();
        IF recCRMSetup_G."Init Finished" = TRUE THEN
          ERROR(Text8053645);

        InitContact(recCrmOrganisation_L);

        NewStepWindow(2);
        InitSimpleOrder(recCrmOrganisation_L);

        NewStepWindow(3);
        InitValidOrder();

        CloseWindow();
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CleanInit@8053715();
    VAR
      recJrnInit_L@8053600 : Record 8053605;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Vide le journal d'initialisation de toutes les lignes synchronis'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.SETRANGE(State, recJrnInit_L.State::Synchronized);
      IF NOT(recJrnInit_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
        recJrnInit_L.DELETEALL;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ComputeCorrespondence@8053652();
    VAR
      recJrnInit_L@8053600 : Record 8053605;
      recJrnInit2_L@8053601 : Record 8053605;
      recJrnInit3_L@8053608 : Record 8053605;
      Count_L@8053602 : Integer;
      i_L@8053603 : Integer;
      CountGbl_L@8053604 : Integer;
      TypeFilter_L@8053605 : Text[250];
      SearchCorrespondance_L@8053606 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Calcul le nombre de correspondance potentiel trouv'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.MODIFYALL("Correspondence Finded", 0);

      //Construction du filtre
      GetSetup();
      IF recCRMSetup_G."Account Use Integer" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        TypeFilter_L := FORMAT(recJrnInit_L.Type::Account);
      END;

      IF recCRMSetup_G."Simple Order Use Integer" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        IF TypeFilter_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          TypeFilter_L := FORMAT(recJrnInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
        END ELSE BEGIN
          TypeFilter_L += '|' + FORMAT(recJrnInit_L.Type::"Simple Order");
        END;
      END;

      IF recCRMSetup_G."Valid Order Use Integer" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        IF TypeFilter_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          TypeFilter_L := FORMAT(recJrnInit_L.Type::"Validated Order");
        END ELSE BEGIN
          TypeFilter_L += '|' + FORMAT(recJrnInit_L.Type::"Validated Order");
        END;
      END;

      //Remise . 0 des correspondances
      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.MODIFYALL("Correspondance Nav", 0);
      recJrnInit_L.MODIFYALL("Correspondance CRM", 0);

      IF TypeFilter_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recJrnInit_L.RESET;
        recJrnInit_L.SETFILTER(Type, TypeFilter_L);
        IF recJrnInit_L.FINDSET(TRUE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            //Calcul des correspondances
            IF EVALUATE(recJrnInit_L."Correspondance Nav", recJrnInit_L.Code) THEN ;
            IF EVALUATE(recJrnInit_L."Correspondance CRM", recJrnInit_L."Code CRM") THEN ;
            recJrnInit_L.MODIFY;
          UNTIL recJrnInit_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      //Recherche des correspondances des code Nav
      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.SETFILTER(Code, '<>%1', '');
      CountGbl_L := recJrnInit_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recJrnInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L / CountGbl_L * 9999, 1, '='));

          SearchCorrespondance_L := FALSE;
          CASE recJrnInit_L.Type OF
            recJrnInit_L.Type::Account:
              IF recCRMSetup_G."Account Use Integer" THEN BEGIN
                SearchCorrespondance_L := TRUE;
              END;
            recJrnInit_L.Type::"Simple Order":
              IF recCRMSetup_G."Simple Order Use Integer" THEN BEGIN
                SearchCorrespondance_L := TRUE;
              END;
            recJrnInit_L.Type::"Validated Order":
              IF recCRMSetup_G."Valid Order Use Integer" THEN BEGIN
                SearchCorrespondance_L := TRUE;
              END;
          END;

          recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
          recJrnInit2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM");
          recJrnInit2_L.SETFILTER("Code CRM", recJrnInit_L.Code);
          IF recJrnInit2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            IF (recJrnInit_L.Guid <> recJrnInit2_L.Guid) OR (recJrnInit_L.Code <> recJrnInit2_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
              recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid);
              recJrnInit3_L.RENAME(recJrnInit3_L.Type, recJrnInit3_L.Code, recJrnInit2_L.Guid);
              recJrnInit3_L."Code CRM" := recJrnInit2_L."Code CRM";
              recJrnInit3_L."Description CRM" := recJrnInit2_L."Description CRM";
              recJrnInit3_L.Action := recJrnInit3_L.Action::Synchronize;
              recJrnInit3_L.MODIFY;
              recJrnInit2_L.DELETE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF SearchCorrespondance_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              IF recJrnInit_L."Correspondance Nav" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
                recJrnInit2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Correspondance CRM");
                recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE("Correspondance CRM", recJrnInit_L."Correspondance Nav");
                IF recJrnInit2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                  IF (recJrnInit_L.Guid <> recJrnInit2_L.Guid) OR (recJrnInit_L.Code <> recJrnInit2_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
                    recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid);
                    recJrnInit3_L.RENAME(recJrnInit3_L.Type, recJrnInit3_L.Code, recJrnInit2_L.Guid);
                    recJrnInit3_L."Code CRM" := recJrnInit2_L."Code CRM";
                    recJrnInit3_L."Description CRM" := recJrnInit2_L."Description CRM";
                    recJrnInit3_L.Action := recJrnInit3_L.Action::Synchronize;
                    recJrnInit3_L.MODIFY;
                    recJrnInit2_L.DELETE;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL recJrnInit_L.NEXT = 0;

      //Recherche des correspondances des code CRM
      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM");
      recJrnInit_L.SETFILTER("Code CRM", '<>%1', '');
      CountGbl_L := recJrnInit_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recJrnInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L / CountGbl_L * 9999, 1, '='));
          SearchCorrespondance_L := FALSE;
          CASE recJrnInit_L.Type OF
            recJrnInit_L.Type::Account:
              IF recCRMSetup_G."Account Use Integer" THEN BEGIN
                SearchCorrespondance_L := TRUE;
              END;
            recJrnInit_L.Type::"Simple Order":
              IF recCRMSetup_G."Simple Order Use Integer" THEN BEGIN
                SearchCorrespondance_L := TRUE;
              END;
            recJrnInit_L.Type::"Validated Order":
              IF recCRMSetup_G."Valid Order Use Integer" THEN BEGIN
                SearchCorrespondance_L := TRUE;
              END;
          END;

          recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
          recJrnInit2_L.SETFILTER(Code, recJrnInit_L."Code CRM");
          IF recJrnInit2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            IF (recJrnInit_L.Guid <> recJrnInit2_L.Guid) OR (recJrnInit_L.Code <> recJrnInit2_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
              recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid);
              recJrnInit3_L.RENAME(recJrnInit3_L.Type, recJrnInit2_L.Code, recJrnInit3_L.Guid);
              recJrnInit3_L."Description Nav" := recJrnInit2_L."Description Nav";
              recJrnInit3_L.Action := recJrnInit3_L.Action::Synchronize;
              recJrnInit3_L.MODIFY;
              recJrnInit2_L.DELETE;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF SearchCorrespondance_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              IF recJrnInit_L."Correspondance CRM" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
                recJrnInit2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Correspondance Nav");
                recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE("Correspondance Nav", recJrnInit_L."Correspondance CRM");
                IF recJrnInit2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                  IF (recJrnInit_L.Guid <> recJrnInit2_L.Guid) OR (recJrnInit_L.Code <> recJrnInit2_L.Code) THEN BEGIN
                    recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid);
                    recJrnInit3_L.RENAME(recJrnInit3_L.Type, recJrnInit2_L.Code, recJrnInit3_L.Guid);
                    recJrnInit3_L."Description Nav" := recJrnInit2_L."Description Nav";
                    recJrnInit3_L.Action := recJrnInit3_L.Action::Synchronize;
                    recJrnInit3_L.MODIFY;
                    recJrnInit2_L.DELETE;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL recJrnInit_L.NEXT = 0;

      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.SETFILTER(Code, '<>%1', '');
      CountGbl_L := recJrnInit_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recJrnInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L / CountGbl_L * 9999, 1, '='));
          //Recherche du nombre de correspondance
          IF recJrnInit_L."Description Nav" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
            recJrnInit2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Description CRM", Type);
            recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE("Description CRM", recJrnInit_L."Description Nav");
            recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnInit_L.Type);
            recJrnInit2_L.SETFILTER("Code CRM", '%1', '');
            Count_L := recJrnInit2_L.COUNT;
            recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid);
            recJrnInit3_L."Correspondence Finded" := Count_L;
            recJrnInit3_L.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL recJrnInit_L.NEXT = 0;

      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      recJrnInit_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Code CRM");
      recJrnInit_L.SETFILTER("Code CRM", '<>%1', '');
      CountGbl_L := recJrnInit_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF recJrnInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L / CountGbl_L * 9999, 1, '='));
          //Recherche du nombre de correspondance
          IF recJrnInit_L."Description CRM" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
            recJrnInit2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Description Nav", Type);
            recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE("Description Nav", recJrnInit_L."Description CRM");
            recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnInit_L.Type);
            recJrnInit2_L.SETFILTER(Code, '%1', '');
            Count_L := recJrnInit2_L.COUNT;
            recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid);
            recJrnInit3_L."Correspondence Finded" := Count_L;
            recJrnInit3_L.Action := recJrnInit3_L.Action::Synchronize;
            recJrnInit3_L.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL recJrnInit_L.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE TreatOneOffCorrespondence@8053709();
    VAR
      recJrnInit_L@8053603 : Record 8053605;
      recJrnInit2_L@8053602 : Record 8053605;
      recJrnInit3_L@8053604 : Record 8053605;
      Count_L@8053601 : Integer;
      i_L@8053600 : Integer;
      CountGbl_L@8053605 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // R'cupSre toutes les correspondance unique et fait le lien
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      OpenWindow();

      i_L := 0;
      recJrnInit_L.RESET;
      CountGbl_L := recJrnInit_L.COUNT;
      //parcours du journal
      IF recJrnInit_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ROUND(i_L / CountGbl_L * 9999, 1, '='));

          Count_L := 0;
          //actualisation du nombre de relation
          IF (recJrnInit_L.Code = '') OR (recJrnInit_L."Code CRM" = '') THEN BEGIN
            IF recJrnInit_L."Description Nav" <> '' THEN BEGIN
              recJrnInit2_L.RESET;
              recJrnInit2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Description CRM", Type);
              recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE("Description CRM", recJrnInit_L."Description Nav");
              recJrnInit2_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnInit_L.Type);
              Count_L := recJrnInit2_L.COUNT;
            END;

            IF recJrnInit_L."Description CRM" <> '' THEN BEGIN
              recJrnInit3_L.RESET;
              recJrnInit3_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Description Nav", Type);
              recJrnInit3_L.SETRANGE("Description Nav", recJrnInit_L."Description CRM");
              recJrnInit3_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnInit_L.Type);
              Count_L := Count_L + recJrnInit3_L.COUNT;
            END;
          END;

          //s'il y a une seule relation, on va la r'aliser
          IF Count_L = 1 THEN BEGIN
            IF recJrnInit2_L.COUNT = 1 THEN BEGIN
              IF recJrnInit2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                IF (recJrnInit2_L.Code = '') AND (recJrnInit2_L."Code CRM" <> '') THEN BEGIN
                  IF recJrnInit3_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid) THEN BEGIN
                    recJrnInit2_L.RENAME(recJrnInit2_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit2_L.Guid);
                    recJrnInit2_L."Description Nav" := recJrnInit_L."Description Nav";
                    recJrnInit2_L.State := recJrnInit2_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                    recJrnInit2_L.Action := recJrnInit2_L.Action::Synchronize;
                    recJrnInit2_L.Error_ := '';
                    recJrnInit2_L."Correspondence Finded" := 0;
                    IF ISNULLGUID(recJrnInit2_L."Linked To") AND NOT(ISNULLGUID(recJrnInit_L."Linked To")) THEN
                      recJrnInit2_L."Linked To" := recJrnInit_L."Linked To";
                    recJrnInit2_L.MODIFY;

                    recJrnInit3_L.DELETE;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF recJrnInit3_L.COUNT = 1 THEN BEGIN
                IF recJrnInit3_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                  IF (recJrnInit3_L.Code <> '') AND (recJrnInit3_L."Code CRM" = '') THEN BEGIN
                    IF recJrnInit2_L.GET(recJrnInit_L.Type, recJrnInit_L.Code, recJrnInit_L.Guid) THEN BEGIN
                      recJrnInit2_L.RENAME(recJrnInit2_L.Type, recJrnInit3_L.Code, recJrnInit2_L.Guid);
                      recJrnInit2_L."Description Nav" := recJrnInit3_L."Description Nav";
                      recJrnInit2_L.State := recJrnInit2_L.State::"To Synchronize";
                      recJrnInit2_L.Action := recJrnInit2_L.Action::Synchronize;
                      recJrnInit2_L.Error_ := '';
                      recJrnInit2_L."Correspondence Finded" := 0;
                      IF ISNULLGUID(recJrnInit2_L."Linked To") AND NOT(ISNULLGUID(recJrnInit3_L."Linked To")) THEN
                        recJrnInit2_L."Linked To" := recJrnInit3_L."Linked To";
                      recJrnInit2_L.MODIFY;

                      recJrnInit3_L.DELETE;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recJrnInit_L."Correspondence Finded" := Count_L;
            recJrnInit_L.MODIFY;
          END;
        UNTIL recJrnInit_L.NEXT = 0;

      CloseWindow();
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitQuotas@1000000011();
    VAR
      recQuota_L@1000000000 : Record 8053260;
      recRecordRef_L@1000000001 : RecordRef;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20151015-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
      recQuota_L.RESET;
      IF recQuota_L.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          recRecordRef_L.GETTABLE(recQuota_L);
          SyncModify(recRecordRef_L, recRecordRef_L);
        UNTIL recQuota_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // <-- 20151015-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<--Recherche entit'-->"@8053616();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE FindCountry@8053002(CodeINSEE_P@8053000 : Code[10]) Result_L : Code[10];
    VAR
      recCoutry_L@8053001 : Record 9;
      Text_L@8053002 : Code[2];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche le pays d'une ville
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CodeINSEE_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Code du pays trouv'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      IF STRLEN(CodeINSEE_P) > 2 THEN BEGIN
        Text_L := COPYSTR(CodeINSEE_P, 1, 2);
        IF recCoutry_L.GET(Text_L) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := recCoutry_L.Code;
        END;
      END;

      IF Result_L = '' THEN BEGIN
        IF recCoutry_L.GET(recCRMSetup_G."Default Country") THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := recCoutry_L.Code;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchCountry@8053614(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCountry_P@8053600 : Record 9) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      StatusCode_L@8053603 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche le pays et le synchronise s'il le trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCountry_P : Le pays . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Enregistrement trouv' et synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
      ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
      SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_codeiso', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, 'cmc_codeiso', '=', recCountry_P.Code);
      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text8053611, recCountry_P.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF recCountry_P.Name <> GetAttribute(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name') THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name', recCountry_P.Name);
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_phoneformat', '175490000');
          UpdateRecord(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchCity@8053631(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCity_P@8053603 : Record 225;PostalCodeId_P@8053604 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      PostalCrmId_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      StatusCode_L@8053605 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche la ville et la synchronise s'il la trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCity_P : Le pays . synchroniser
      //    - PostalCodeId_P : ID CRM du code postal si trouv'
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Enregistrement trouv' et synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      PostalCrmId_L := PostalCodeId_P;

      //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
      IF PostalCrmId_L = '' THEN
        PostalCrmId_L := CleanGuidToCRM(GetGuid(Text8053613, recCity_P.Code));

      ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
      SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_inseecode', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      //recherche de la ville en tenant compte du code postal
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name', '=', recCity_P.City);
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, 'cmc_postalcode_id', '=', PostalCrmId_L);
      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text8053612, COPYSTR(recCity_P.Code + GetSeparator() + recCity_P.City, 1,
          MAXSTRLEN(recGuid_G."Code Nav")), TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF recCity_P."INSEE No." <> GetAttribute(EntityName_P, 'cmc_inseecode') THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_inseecode', recCity_P."INSEE No.");
          UpdateRecord(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchCampaign@8053643(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCampaign_P@8053600 : Record 8052991) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      StatusCode_L@8053604 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche la campagne la synchronise s'il la trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCampaign_P : Le pays . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Enregistrement trouv' et synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
      ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
      SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_startdate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_enddate', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id', '');
      SetField(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, 'cmc_code', '=', recCampaign_P."No.");
      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text8053622, recCampaign_P."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF (recCampaign_P."No." <> GetAttribute(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name')) OR
           (recCampaign_P."Starting Date" <> FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_P, 'cmc_startdate'))) OR
           (recCampaign_P."Ending Date" <> FormatDateFromCrm(GetAttribute(EntityName_P, 'cmc_enddate')))
        THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_name', recCampaign_P."No.");
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_code', recCampaign_P."No.");
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_startdate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_P."Starting Date"));
          SetField(EntityName_P, 'cmc_enddate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_P."Ending Date"));
          UpdateRecord(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchActivityResponsible@8053663(Activity_P@8053600 : Code[10];SalesNetwork_P@8053601 : Code[10];Insee_P@8053602 : Code[10]) Result_L : Code[20];
    VAR
      recRespons_L@8053610 : Record 8053161;
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053604 : Record 8053608;
      UserId_L@8053603 : Code[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche le magasin et le synchronise s'il le trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Activity_P : Activit' (PA ou PV)
      //    - SalesNetwork_P : R'seau commercial
      //    - Insee_P : code insee de le commune
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : USERID du responsable trouv'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';

      //recherche du code vendeur du responsable
      recRespons_L.RESET;
      recRespons_L.SETRANGE("Prod. Activity Code", Activity_P);
      recRespons_L.SETRANGE("Sales Network Code", SalesNetwork_P);
      recRespons_L.SETRANGE("INSEE No.", Insee_P);
      IF recRespons_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        // recherche du code utilisateur du vendeur trouv'
        IF recRespons_L."Salesperson Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recRespons_L."Salesperson Code");
          IF UserId_L <> '' THEN
            Result_L := UserId_L;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PV");
          IF UserId_L <> '' THEN
            Result_L := UserId_L;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchThirdPartyToCreate@8053754(Code_P@8053600 : Code[20];VAR recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P@8053601 : Record 8053600;CodeCompany_P@8053603 : Code[10];CompanyName_P@8053605 : Text[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recMasterRelation_L@8053602 : Record 8053076;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Pemet de n'utiliser qu'une seule initialisation de la connexion CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code du contact
      //    - recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P : Tiers MS . cr'er qui va pointer sur celui que l'on a trouv'
      //    - CodeCompany_P : Code soci't'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF CompanyName_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF CompanyName_P <> COMPANYNAME THEN BEGIN
          recMasterRelation_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
        END;
      END;
      recMasterRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Contact code", "MC Company Code", Type);
      recMasterRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact code", Code_P);
      recMasterRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CodeCompany_P);
      recMasterRelation_L.SETRANGE(Type, recMasterRelation_L.Type::"Third party");
      IF recMasterRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.RESET;
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.SETCURRENTKEY("Company No.","No.");
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.SETRANGE("Company No.", CodeCompany_P);
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.SETRANGE("No.", recMasterRelation_L."No.");
        IF recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END;

      Result_L := FALSE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchVariantSetup@8053004(VAR recVariantSetupVar_P@8053000 : Record 8053090;recItem_P@8053001 : Record 27) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche le param'trage de variante
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recVariantSetupVar_P : param'trage variant
      //    - recItem_P : article
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Param'trage trouv' ou non
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      recVariantSetupVar_P.RESET;
      recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE(Type, recVariantSetupVar_P.Type::Item);
      recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_P."No.");
      Result_L := recVariantSetupVar_P.FINDFIRST;

      IF (NOT Result_L) AND (recItem_P."Species Code"<>'') THEN BEGIN
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE(Type, recVariantSetupVar_P.Type::species);
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_P."Species Code");
        Result_L := recVariantSetupVar_P.FINDFIRST;
      END;

      IF (NOT Result_L) AND (recItem_P."Sub-Family Code"<>'') THEN BEGIN
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE(Type, recVariantSetupVar_P.Type::"Sub-family");
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_P."Sub-Family Code");
        Result_L := recVariantSetupVar_P.FINDFIRST;
      END;

      IF (NOT Result_L) AND (recItem_P."Family Code"<>'') THEN BEGIN
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE(Type, recVariantSetupVar_P.Type::Family);
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_P."Family Code");
        Result_L := recVariantSetupVar_P.FINDFIRST;
      END;

      IF (NOT Result_L) AND (recItem_P."Activity Code"<>'') THEN BEGIN
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE(Type, recVariantSetupVar_P.Type::Activity);
        recVariantSetupVar_P.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_P."Activity Code");
        Result_L := recVariantSetupVar_P.FINDFIRST;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ModifyProposition@8053602(RecRef_P@8053603 : RecordRef;CompanyCode_P@8053604 : Code[10];CompanyName_P@8053605 : Text[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      FieldRef_L@8053602 : FieldRef;
      recThirdPartyToCreate_L@8053601 : Record 8053600;
      cduSynchroCRM_L@8053600 : Codeunit 8053600;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Indique si l'on modifie les proposition par d'faut du systSme
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : RecordRef de la proposition
      //    - CompanyCode_P : Code de la soci't'
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't'
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Proposition . modifier
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      IF InterfaceIsActiveOnCompany(CompanyName_P) THEN BEGIN
        //Recherche du Tiers MS . cr'er
        recThirdPartyToCreate_L.RESET;
        recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Company No.", "No.");
        recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("Company No.", CompanyCode_P);
        FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
        recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L.VALUE));
        IF recThirdPartyToCreate_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          //Ajout des propositions
          Result_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE PropositionModified@8053603(RecRef_P@8053607 : RecordRef;RecCompanyMS_P@8053605 : Record 8053075);
    VAR
      FieldRef_L@8053603 : FieldRef;
      recThirdPartyToCreate_L@8053602 : Record 8053600;
      cduSynchroCRM_L@8053601 : Codeunit 8053600;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Indique si la proposition a 't' modifi'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - RecRef_P : RecordRef de la proposition
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF InterfaceIsActive THEN BEGIN
        //Recherche du Tiers MS . cr'er
        IF (RecCompanyMS_P."Allocate vendor" = FALSE) OR (RecCompanyMS_P."Allocate customer" = FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          recThirdPartyToCreate_L.RESET;
          recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Company No.", "No.");
          recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("Company No.", RecCompanyMS_P.Code);
          FieldRef_L := RecRef_P.FIELD(1);
          recThirdPartyToCreate_L.SETRANGE("No.", FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
          IF recThirdPartyToCreate_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(CONFIRM(Text8053609, FALSE, FieldRef_L.VALUE)) THEN
              ERROR(Text8053610, FORMAT(FieldRef_L));
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateLinkedGuid@8053610(Type_P@8053604 : Code[30];CodeNav_P@8053602 : Text[54];Guid_P@8053603 : GUID);
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053601;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053605 : TEMPORARY Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de peupler la table de correspondance entre les enregistrements NAV et les GUID MS CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - CodeNav_P : Code Navision de l'enregistrement . lier
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetType(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      IF recCrmGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", CodeNav_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF recCrmGuid_L.GUID <> FormatGuid(Guid_P) THEN BEGIN
          recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(Guid_P);
          recCrmGuid_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recCrmGuid_L.INIT;
        recCrmGuid_L."Type Data" := recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data";
        recCrmGuid_L."Code Nav" := CodeNav_P;
        recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(Guid_P);
        recCrmGuid_L.INSERT(TRUE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetGuid@8053615(Type_P@8053601 : Code[30];CodeNav_P@8053602 : Text[54]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053601;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053603 : TEMPORARY Record 8053601;
      FindGuid_L@8053605 : Boolean;
      Entity_L@8053604 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de r'cup'rer les GUID et de lancer la synchronisation si le lien n'existe pas
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - CodeNav_P : Code Navision de l'enregistrement . lier
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Le GUID sous forme texte. Vide si pas trouv' ou problSme de synchronisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      GetType(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      FindGuid_L := recCrmGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", CodeNav_P);
      IF FindGuid_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := recCrmGuid_L.GUID;
        IF ISNULLGUID(Result_L) THEN
          Result_L := '';
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCodeFromGuid@8053635(Type_P@8053602 : Code[30];TxtGuid_P@8053605 : Text[50];Delete_P@8053600 : Boolean) Result_L : Text[54];
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053604 : Record 8053601;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053603 : TEMPORARY Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de r'cup'rer les codes NAV en fonction des GUID
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //    - Delete_P : Supprime la donn'e aprSs l'avoir trouv'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Code NAV (vide si pas trouv')
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      GetType(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);

      //Recherche de l'enregistrement dans la table d'historique des GUID
      IF STRLEN(TxtGuid_P) > 10 THEN BEGIN
        recCrmGuid_L.RESET;
        recCrmGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
        recCrmGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data");
        recCrmGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, FormatGuid(TxtGuid_P));
        IF recCrmGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          IF Delete_P = TRUE THEN
            recCrmGuid_L.DELETE(TRUE)
          ELSE
            Result_L := recCrmGuid_L."Code Nav";
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatGuid@8053609(Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50]) Result_L : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de mettre les accolades au GUID texte retourn' par le connecteur pour l'utiliser dans NAV
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID format'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF STRLEN(Guid_P) = 36 THEN
        Result_L := '{' + Guid_P + '}'
      ELSE
        Result_L := Guid_P;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CleanGuidToCRM@8053611(Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      Guid_L@8053601 : GUID;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de supprimer les accolades au GUID texte . envoyer au CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID nettoy'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF STRLEN(Guid_P) = 38 THEN
        Result_L := DELCHR(Guid_P, '=', '{}')
      ELSE
        Result_L := Guid_P;
      IF Result_L = '' THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(Guid_L);
        Result_L := Guid_L;
        Result_L := DELCHR(Result_L, '=', '{}')
      END;
      IF Result_L = '' THEN
        Result_L := '#NULL#';
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CompareGuid@8053765(Guid1_P@8053600 : Text[50];Guid2_P@8053601 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      Guid1_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      Guid2_L@8053602 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de Comparer des GUID
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Guid1_P : GUID
      //    - Guid2_P : GUID
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : 'gaux ou non
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Guid1_P := FormatGuid(Guid1_P);
      Guid2_P := FormatGuid(Guid2_P);

      Result_L := FALSE;
      IF (Guid1_P = '') OR (Guid1_P = '#NULL#') THEN BEGIN
        Guid1_L := '';
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF ISNULLGUID(Guid1_P) THEN BEGIN
          Guid1_L := '';
        END ELSE BEGIN
          Guid1_L := FormatGuid(Guid1_P);
        END;
      END;

      IF (Guid2_P = '') OR (Guid2_P = '#NULL#') THEN BEGIN
        Guid2_L := '';
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF ISNULLGUID(Guid2_P) THEN BEGIN
          Guid2_L := '';
        END ELSE BEGIN
          Guid2_L := FormatGuid(Guid2_P);
        END;
      END;

      IF Guid1_L = Guid2_L THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteGuid@8053013(Type_P@8053001 : Code[30];Code_P@8053000 : Text[110];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053002 : Record 8053601;
      recGuid_L@8053003 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de Comparer des GUID
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : type de donn'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de la partie Nav
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'entit'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetType(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      IF recGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", Code_P) THEN BEGIN
        recGuid_L.DELETE;
      END;
      recGuid_L.RESET;
      recGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data");
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, FormatGuid(Guid_P));
      IF recGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recGuid_L.DELETE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetType@8053693(Type_P@8053600 : Code[30];VAR Var_recCrmGuid_P@8053601 : Record 8053601);
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de trouver le type de donn'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - Var_recCrmGuid_P : Enregistrement de stockage
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CASE UPPERCASE(Type_P) OF
        UPPERCASE(Text8053611): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::Country;
        UPPERCASE(Text8053612): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::City;
        UPPERCASE(Text8053613): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::"Post Code";
        UPPERCASE(Text8053620): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::"Group Measure";
        UPPERCASE(Text8053621): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::Measure;
        UPPERCASE(Text8053622): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::Campaign;
        UPPERCASE(Text8053623): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::Location;
        UPPERCASE(Text8053624): Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data" := Var_recCrmGuid_P."Type Data"::"Order Header";

    PROCEDURE GetResponsability@8053690(CodeCrm_P@8053600 : Code[20]) Result_L : Code[20];
    VAR
      recMappingCode_L@8053601 : Record 8053604;
      recJobResponsability_L@8053602 : Record 5066;
      CodeResponsability_L@8053603 : Code[10];
      DescriptionResponsability_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      Found_L@8053605 : Boolean;
      CreateMapping_L@8053606 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de trouver ou cr'' une responsabilit'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CodeCrm_P : Code CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : code Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      Found_L := FALSE;
      CreateMapping_L := TRUE;

      //Recherche du code Nav
      recMappingCode_L.RESET;
      recMappingCode_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", "Code CRM");
      recMappingCode_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recMappingCode_L."Type Data"::Responsability);
      recMappingCode_L.SETRANGE("Code CRM", CodeCrm_P);
      IF recMappingCode_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        //Code trouv'
        Result_L := recMappingCode_L."Code Nav";
        CreateMapping_L := FALSE;
        IF recJobResponsability_L.GET(recMappingCode_L."Code Nav") THEN
          Found_L := TRUE;
      END;

      IF Found_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        //Code . cr'er
        CASE CodeCrm_P OF
          '175 490 000': BEGIN
            CodeResponsability_L := TextCodeCoop; //Administrateur coop'rative
            DescriptionResponsability_L := TextDescCoop;
          END;

          '175 490 001': BEGIN
            CodeResponsability_L := TextCodeComReg; //Comit' de r'gion
            DescriptionResponsability_L := TextDescComReg;
          END;

          '175 490 002': BEGIN
            CodeResponsability_L := TextCodeComSec; //Comit' de secteur
            DescriptionResponsability_L := TextDescComSec;
          END;

          '175 490 003': BEGIN
            CodeResponsability_L := TextCodeDelegue; //D'l'gu'
            DescriptionResponsability_L := TextDescDelegue;
          END;
        END;
        IF CodeResponsability_L <> '' THEN
          //cr'ation de la responsabilit' si elle n'existe pas
          IF NOT(recJobResponsability_L.GET(CodeResponsability_L)) THEN BEGIN
            CLEAR(recJobResponsability_L);
            recJobResponsability_L.INIT;
            recJobResponsability_L.Code := CodeResponsability_L;
            recJobResponsability_L.Description := DescriptionResponsability_L;
            recJobResponsability_L.INSERT(TRUE);
          END;
      END;

      IF (CreateMapping_L = TRUE) AND (CodeResponsability_L <> '') THEN BEGIN
        //ajout du mapping puisque la responsabilit' . 't' soit cr''e soit trouv'e
        CLEAR(recMappingCode_L);
        recMappingCode_L.INIT;
        recMappingCode_L."Type Data" := recMappingCode_L."Type Data"::Responsability;
        recMappingCode_L."Code Nav" := CodeResponsability_L;
        recMappingCode_L."Code CRM" := CodeCrm_P;
        recMappingCode_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        Result_L := CodeResponsability_L;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateLinkedGuid2@8053731(Type_P@8053604 : Code[30];CodeNav_P@8053602 : Text[200];Guid_P@8053603 : GUID);
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053605 : TEMPORARY Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de peupler la table de correspondance entre les enregistrements NAV et les GUID MS CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - CodeNav_P : Code Navision de l'enregistrement . lier
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetType2(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      IF recCrmGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", CodeNav_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF recCrmGuid_L.GUID <> FormatGuid(Guid_P) THEN BEGIN
          recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(Guid_P);
          recCrmGuid_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recCrmGuid_L.INIT;
        recCrmGuid_L."Type Data" := recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data";
        recCrmGuid_L."Code Nav" := CodeNav_P;
        recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(Guid_P);
        recCrmGuid_L.INSERT(TRUE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetGuid2@8053730(Type_P@8053601 : Code[30];CodeNav_P@8053602 : Text[200]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053603 : TEMPORARY Record 8053609;
      FindGuid_L@8053605 : Boolean;
      Entity_L@8053604 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de r'cup'rer les GUID et de lancer la synchronisation si le lien n'existe pas
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - CodeNav_P : Code Navision de l'enregistrement . lier
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Le GUID sous forme texte. Vide si pas trouv' ou problSme de synchronisation
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      GetType2(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      FindGuid_L := recCrmGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", CodeNav_P);
      IF FindGuid_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := recCrmGuid_L.GUID;
        IF ISNULLGUID(Result_L) THEN
          Result_L := '';
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCodeFromGuid2@8053729(Type_P@8053602 : Code[30];TxtGuid_P@8053605 : Text[50];Delete_P@8053600 : Boolean) Result_L : Text[200];
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053604 : Record 8053609;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053603 : TEMPORARY Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de r'cup'rer les codes NAV en fonction des GUID
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //    - Delete_P : Supprime la donn'e aprSs l'avoir trouv'e
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Code NAV (vide si pas trouv')
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      GetType2(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);

      //Recherche de l'enregistrement dans la table d'historique des GUID
      IF STRLEN(TxtGuid_P) > 10 THEN BEGIN
        recCrmGuid_L.RESET;
        recCrmGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
        recCrmGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data");
        recCrmGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, FormatGuid(TxtGuid_P));
        IF recCrmGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          IF Delete_P = TRUE THEN
            recCrmGuid_L.DELETE(TRUE)
          ELSE
            Result_L := recCrmGuid_L."Code Nav";
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateGuid2@8053728(Type_P@8053601 : Code[30];CodeNav_P@8053600 : Text[200];TxtGuid_P@8053602 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053605 : Record 8053609;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053603 : TEMPORARY Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de mettre . jour en ajoutant ou supprimant les GUID
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - CodeNav_P : Code Navision de l'enregistrement . lier
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Op'ration r'ussie
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      GetType2(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);

      IF STRLEN(TxtGuid_P) > 10 THEN BEGIN
        IF recCrmGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", CodeNav_P) THEN BEGIN
          IF recCrmGuid_L.GUID <> FormatGuid(TxtGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
            recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(TxtGuid_P);
            Result_L := recCrmGuid_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recCrmGuid_L.INIT;
          recCrmGuid_L."Type Data" := recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data";
          recCrmGuid_L."Code Nav" := CodeNav_P;
          recCrmGuid_L.GUID := TxtGuid_P;
          Result_L := recCrmGuid_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateLinkedGuidWithCompany2@8053727(Type_P@8053604 : Code[30];CodeNav_P@8053602 : Text[110];Guid_P@8053603 : GUID;CompanyName_P@8053601 : Text[30]);
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053605 : TEMPORARY Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de peupler la table de correspondance entre les enregistrements NAV et les GUID MS CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Permet de connaOtre la table
      //    - CodeNav_P : Code Navision de l'enregistrement . lier
      //    - Guid_P : GUID MS CRM
      //    - CompanyName_P : Nom de la soci't' o- faire la mise . jour
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF CompanyName_P <> COMPANYNAME THEN
        recCrmGuid_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyName_P);
      GetType2(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      IF recCrmGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", CodeNav_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF recCrmGuid_L.GUID <> FormatGuid(Guid_P) THEN BEGIN
          recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(Guid_P);
          recCrmGuid_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recCrmGuid_L.INIT;
        recCrmGuid_L."Type Data" := recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data";
        recCrmGuid_L."Code Nav" := CodeNav_P;
        recCrmGuid_L.GUID := FormatGuid(Guid_P);
        recCrmGuid_L.INSERT(TRUE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteGuid2@8053016(Type_P@8053001 : Code[30];Code_P@8053000 : Text[200];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recCrmGuid_Tmp_L@8053002 : Record 8053609;
      recGuid_L@8053003 : Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de Comparer des GUID
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : type de donn'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de la partie Nav
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'entit'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      GetType2(Type_P, recCrmGuid_Tmp_L);
      IF recGuid_L.GET(recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data", Code_P) THEN BEGIN
        recGuid_L.DELETE;
      END;
      recGuid_L.RESET;
      recGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recCrmGuid_Tmp_L."Type Data");
      recGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, FormatGuid(Guid_P));
      IF recGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recGuid_L.DELETE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GuidFinded@8053745(Code_P@8053600 : Text[110];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50];Caption_P@8053602 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de v'rifier que le GUID a 't' trouv' et de lancer un message d'erreur dans le cas contraire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code trouv'
      //    - Guid_P : GUID initial
      //    - Caption_P : Ectiture . afficher en cas d'erreur
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF STRLEN(Guid_P) > 20 THEN BEGIN
        IF (Code_P = '') AND (NOT(ISNULLGUID(FormatGuid(Guid_P)))) THEN BEGIN
          ClearConnector();
          ERROR(Text8053677, Caption_P);
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF (Code_P <> '') THEN BEGIN
          ClearConnector();
          ERROR(Text8053687, Caption_P, Code_P);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GuidFindedWithoutError@8053755(Code_P@8053600 : Text[110];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de v'rifier que le GUID a 't' trouv'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code trouv'
      //    - Guid_P : GUID initial
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := TRUE;
      IF STRLEN(Guid_P) > 20 THEN BEGIN
        IF (Code_P <> '') AND (ISNULLGUID(FormatGuid(Guid_P))) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := FALSE;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF (Code_P <> '') THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := FALSE;
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GuidIsEmpty@8053750(Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de v'rifier si le GUID est vide
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Guid_P : GUID initial
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      IF STRLEN(Guid_P) < 20 THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;

      IF ISNULLGUID(FormatGuid(Guid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := TRUE;
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchGuid@8053756(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];Field1_P@8053602 : Text[50];Text1_P@8053600 : Text[110];Field2_P@8053606 : Text[50];Text2_P@8053605 : Text[110];Field3_P@8053604 : Text[50];Text3_P@8053603 : Text[110]) Result_L : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de rechercher si un enregistrement existe d'j. dans la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : nom de l'entit'
      //    - Field1_P : nom du 1er champ de recherche
      //    - Text1_P : Valeur du 1er champ de recherche
      //    - Field2_P : nom du 2Sme champ de recherche
      //    - Text2_P : Valeur du 2Sme champ de recherche
      //    - Field3_P : nom du 3Sme champ de recherche
      //    - Text3_P : Valeur du 3Sme champ de recherche
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : GUID trouv'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
      IF Field1_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, Field1_P, '=', Text1_P);
      END;
      IF Field2_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, Field2_P, '=', Text2_P);
      END;
      IF Field3_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        SetFilterEntity(EntityName_P, Field3_P, '=', Text3_P);
      END;
      IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_P) > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id');
        Result_L := FormatGuid(Result_L);
      END;

      ResetAuto(EntityName_P);
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitializeCrmConnexion@8061005() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      paramMessageGUID_L@1000000005 : GUID;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Initialisation des variables globales pour la connexion . Dynamics CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      GetSetup();

      //V'rification des paramStres de connexion . Dynamics CRM
      recCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("CRM URL Root");
      recCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("CRM URL");
      recCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("CRM Organization");
      recCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("User Login");

      IF ISCLEAR(NavBPMConnector_G) THEN
        CREATE(NavBPMConnector_G);

      NavBPMConnector_G.SetErrorLogging(NOT(recCRMSetup_G."No Log by Connector"));

      //Application des paramStres de connexion
      NavBPMConnector_G.CrmRootURL := recCRMSetup_G."CRM URL Root";
      NavBPMConnector_G.CrmURL := recCRMSetup_G."CRM URL";
      NavBPMConnector_G.CrmOrganization := recCRMSetup_G."CRM Organization";
      NavBPMConnector_G.CrmLogin := recCRMSetup_G."User Login";
      NavBPMConnector_G.CrmPassword := recCRMSetup_G."User Password";

      //Initialisation de la connexion
      Result_L := NavBPMConnector_G.InitializeConnexion;
      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
        // --> 20150601-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //ErrorConnection_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnector_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnection_G));
        //AFTER
        ErrorConnection_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnector_G.GetInitializationLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnection_G));
        // <-- 20150601-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
        CLEAR(NavBPMConnector_G);
      // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        ErrorConnection_G := '';
      // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InterfaceIsActive@8053601() Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de savoir si l'interface est active
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Interface active ou non
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      IF AlreadyGetDataSetup_G = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        AlreadyGetDataSetupResult_G := FALSE;
        AlreadyGetDataSetup_G := TRUE;
        IF recDataSetup_G.GET() THEN BEGIN
          IF recDataSetup_G."Active Synchro CRM" = TRUE THEN
            IF recCRMSetup_G.GET() THEN
              IF recCRMSetup_G."CRM URL Root" <> '' THEN
                AlreadyGetDataSetupResult_G := TRUE;
        END;
      END;

      Result_L := AlreadyGetDataSetupResult_G;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InterfaceIsActiveOnCompany@8053721(Company_P@8053600 : Text[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de savoir si l'interface est active sur la soci't' d'sign'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Company_P : Nom de la soci't'
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Interface active ou non
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      IF recDataSetup_G.GET() THEN BEGIN
        IF recDataSetup_G."Active Synchro CRM" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          recCRMSetup_G.CHANGECOMPANY(Company_P);
          IF recCRMSetup_G.GET() THEN BEGIN
            IF recCRMSetup_G."CRM URL Root" <> '' THEN BEGIN
              Result_L := TRUE;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      AlreadyGetDataSetupResult_G := FALSE;
      AlreadyGetDataSetup_G := FALSE;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCompanyMcCode@8053645() Result_L : Code[10];
    BEGIN

      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := recCompagny_G.Code;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddJournalExport@8053698(Type_P@8053600 : 'Account,Order,Item,Ship-to Address,Unloading Place,Unloading Method,Unloading Size,Unloading Time,Activity,Family,SubFamily,Specie,Quota,Transcript.,ArticleTranscript.,ElementCampaign,DetailsElementCampaign,TypeContratCadre,ModeleContratCadreAppro,Abaque,CoeffAbaque,TypeEngagement,Engagement,EntonnoirArticle,TypeContratCadreCollecte,EntonnoirContratCollecte,EngContratCadreCollecte,ContratCadreCollect,LigneContratCadreCollect,DetailLigneContratCadreCollect,Campaign,Import,Season,TPG,ContractTypeCamp,Parcel,TPGHeader,TPGLine,History,NbPf,Contact,City';Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053602 : Code[10];Company_P@8053604 : Text[30]);
    VAR
      recJournalExport_L@8053603 : Record 8053606;
      RecCRMSetup_L@1000000000 : Record 8053602;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de supprimer les accolades au GUID et de r'cup'rer l'enregistrement au CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Entit'
      //    - Code_P : Code ou GUID permettant au coe d'identifi' l'action qu'il devra faire
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action (modification, suppression, ...)
      //    - Company_P : Soci't' o- faire la mise . jour. Peut ^tre vide si on ne change pas de soci't'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27117-BSI01
      IF NOT RecCRMSetup_L.GET THEN
        CLEAR(RecCRMSetup_L);
      // <-- 20170509-WI00-TER01.01.00-27117-BSI01

      // <-- 20170407-WI00-TER01.01.00-3254-BSI01
      IF (Company_P <> '') AND (Company_P <> COMPANYNAME) THEN
        recJournalExport_L.CHANGECOMPANY(Company_P);
      IF recJournalExport_L.GET(Type_P, Code_P) THEN BEGIN
        //mise . jour de l'existant
        // --> 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
        IF (recJournalExport_L."Type Action" <> TypeAction_P) OR (recJournalExport_L.Closed <> FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
          recJournalExport_L."Type Action" := TypeAction_P;
          recJournalExport_L.Closed := FALSE;
          recJournalExport_L.MODIFY;
        // --> 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
        END;
        // <-- 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ligne
        recJournalExport_L.INIT;
        recJournalExport_L.Type := Type_P;
        recJournalExport_L."Document No." := Code_P;
        recJournalExport_L."Type Action" := TypeAction_P;
        recJournalExport_L.Closed := FALSE;
        // --> 20170407-WI00-TER01.01.00-3254-BSI01
        IF RecCRMSetup_L."Deployment Type" = RecCRMSetup_L."Deployment Type"::"On-line" THEN
          recJournalExport_L."Priority Order" := SetSyncPrioriyOrder(Type_P);
        // <-- 20170407-WI00-TER01.01.00-3254-BSI01
        recJournalExport_L.INSERT;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddJournalLineExport@8053699(Type_P@8053602 : 'Account,Order,Item,Ship-to Address,Unloading Place,Unloading Method,Unloading Size,Unloading Time,Activity,Family,SubFamily,Specie,Quota,Transcript.,ArticleTranscript.,ElementCampaign,DetailsElementCampaign,TypeContratCadre,ModeleContratCadreAppro,Abaque,CoeffAbaque,TypeEngagement,Engagement,EntonnoirArticle,TypeContratCadreCollecte,EntonnoirContratCollecte,EngContratCadreCollecte,ContratCadreCollect,LigneContratCadreCollect,DetailLigneContratCadreCollect,TPGHeader,TPGLine';Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];Entity_P@8053603 : Text[50];LineCode_P@8053607 : Text[50];Field_P@8053604 : Text[30];Data_P@8053605 : Text[250];Company_P@8053606 : Text[30]);
    VAR
      recJournalLineExport_L@8053600 : Record 8053607;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de supprimer les accolades au GUID et de r'cup'rer l'enregistrement au CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Type_P : Entit'
      //    - Code_P : Code ou GUID permettant au code d'identifi' l'action qu'il devra faire
      //    - Entity_P : Entit' . mettre . jour
      //    - LineCode_P : Code permettant d'identifi' la ligne
      //    - Field_P : Champ CRM . mettre . jour
      //    - Data-P : Donn'e . y placer
      //    - Company_P : Soci't' o- faire la mise . jour. Peut ^tre vide si on ne change pas de soci't'
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF (Company_P <> '') AND (Company_P <> COMPANYNAME) THEN
        recJournalLineExport_L.CHANGECOMPANY(Company_P);
      IF recJournalLineExport_L.GET(Type_P, Code_P, Entity_P, LineCode_P, Field_P) THEN BEGIN
        //mise . jour de l'existant
        // --> 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
        IF recJournalLineExport_L.Data <> Data_P THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
          recJournalLineExport_L.Data := Data_P;
          recJournalLineExport_L.MODIFY;
        // --> 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
        END;
        // <-- 20150330-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2864-FHS01
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ligne
        recJournalLineExport_L.INIT;
        recJournalLineExport_L.Type := Type_P;
        recJournalLineExport_L."Document No." := Code_P;
        recJournalLineExport_L.Entity := Entity_P;
        recJournalLineExport_L.Code := LineCode_P;
        recJournalLineExport_L.Field := Field_P;
        recJournalLineExport_L.Data := Data_P;
        recJournalLineExport_L.INSERT;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetFieldGuid@8053757(Entity_P@8053602 : Text[50];Field_P@8053601 : Text[50];Data_P@8053600 : Text[250]);

      //  Entr'es
      //    - Entity_P : Entit'
      //    - Field_P : champ . mettre . jour ou . consulter
      //    - Data_P : la donn'e concern'e
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF Data_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
        NavBPMConnector_G.SetField(Entity_P, LOWERCASE(Field_P), CleanGuidToCRM(Data_P));
      END ELSE BEGIN
        NavBPMConnector_G.SetField(Entity_P, LOWERCASE(Field_P), '#NULL#');
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetState@8053675(Entity_P@8053601 : Text[50];Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50];StatusCode_P@8053603 : Integer) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      Subject_L@8053602 : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet d'activer ou d'sactiver un enregistrement CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Entity_P : Entit'
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'entit' . d'sactiver
      //    - StatusCode_P : Code statut (entier)
      //  Sorties
      //    - Indique la r'ussite de l'op'ration
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
        NavBPMConnector2_G.ResetAllSelection();
        NavBPMConnector2_G.ResetAllQuery();
        NavBPMConnector2_G.Reset(Entity_P);
        NavBPMConnector2_G.ResetQuery(Entity_P);
        Subject_L := '';
        IF Entity_P = 'salesorder' THEN BEGIN
          CASE StatusCode_P OF
            4: BEGIN
              Subject_L := 'CANCELED';
            END;
            100001: BEGIN
              Subject_L := 'PROCESS';
            END;
            100002: BEGIN
              Subject_L := 'PROCESS';
            END;
          END;
        END;
        Result_L := NavBPMConnector2_G.SetState(Entity_P, CleanGuidToCRM(Guid_P), StatusCode_P, Subject_L);

        IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          ErrorConnector_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnector2_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnector_G));
          CLEAR(NavBPMConnector2_G);
          ERROR(ErrorConnector_G);
        END;

        // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //CLEAR(NavBPMConnector2_G);
        //AFTER
        // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SelectMultipleRecords@8053671(Entity_P@8053600 : Text[50]) Result_L : Integer;
