    PROCEDURE CheckItemBlockingSetupForSAS@8053042(RecSASLine_P@1000000004 : Record 8053611;LocationCode_P@1000000007 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      ItemBlockingSetup_L@8053001 : Record 8053230;
      BlockingType_L@8053002 : 'Location Type,Location,Salesperson,Responsibility Center,Customer,Vendor';
      SalespersonCode_L@8053010 : Code[10];
      ResponsibilityCenter_L@8053009 : Code[10];
      SalesHeader_L@8053008 : Record 36;
      Location_L@8053000 : Record 14;
      ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L@1000000000 : Integer;
      i_L@1000000001 : Integer;
      CUItemBlockingMgt_L@1000000002 : Codeunit 8053025;
      Text004_L@1000000003 : TextConst 'ENU=%1: %2  is blocked for %3: %4 !;FRA=SAS : %1: %2 est bloqu'(e) pour %3: %4.';
      FlowType@1000000005 : 'None,Sales Order,Sales Return,Shipment,Return Receipt,Sales Invoice,Sales Credit Memo,Transfer Order (Outbound),Transfer Order (Inbound),Transfer Shipment,Transfer Receipt,Purchase Order,Purchase Return,Receipt,Return Shipment,Purchase Invoice,Purch. Credit Memo,Requisition Line,Item Ledger Entry';
      RecSASHeader_L@1000000006 : Record 8053610;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20161026-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3169-BSI01
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cette fonction v'rifie le blocage des articles des lignes de commande de vente CRM
      //
      //
      // Entr'e:
      //   - RecSASLine_P : SAS ligne de commande de vente CRM
      //-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      RecSASHeader_L.GET(RecSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid");

      //IF RecSASLine_P."No." = '' THEN //. v'rifier
        //EXIT;

      FlowType := FlowType::"Sales Order";

      SalesHeader_L.GET(SalesHeader_L."Document Type"::Order,RecSASHeader_L."No.");
      SalespersonCode_L := RecSASLine_P."Salesperson Code";
      ResponsibilityCenter_L := '';

      IF SalespersonCode_L = '' THEN
        SalespersonCode_L := SalesHeader_L."Salesperson Code";

      IF ResponsibilityCenter_L = '' THEN
        ResponsibilityCenter_L := SalesHeader_L."Responsibility Center";

      ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L := 0;
      FOR i_L := 1 TO 4 DO BEGIN
        CASE i_L OF
          1 : BEGIN // Blocking Location and Location Type
                IF LocationCode_P <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L := CUItemBlockingMgt_L.IsItemBlocked(ItemBlockingSetup_L,
                    BlockingType_L::Location,SalesHeader_L."Order Date",
                    RecSASLine_P."No.",LocationCode_P,FlowType,RecSASLine_P."Variant Code");
                  IF ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
                    Location_L.GET(LocationCode_P);
                    IF Location_L."Location Type" <> '' THEN
                      ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L := CUItemBlockingMgt_L.IsItemBlocked(ItemBlockingSetup_L,
                        BlockingType_L::"Location Type",SalesHeader_L."Order Date",
                        RecSASLine_P."No.",Location_L."Location Type",FlowType,RecSASLine_P."Variant Code");
                  END;
                END;
              END;
          2 : BEGIN // Blocking Salesperson
                IF SalespersonCode_L  <> '' THEN
                  ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L := CUItemBlockingMgt_L.IsItemBlocked(ItemBlockingSetup_L,
                    BlockingType_L::Salesperson,SalesHeader_L."Order Date",
                    RecSASLine_P."No.",SalespersonCode_L,FlowType,RecSASLine_P."Variant Code");
              END;
          3 : BEGIN // Blocking Responsibility Center
                IF ResponsibilityCenter_L <> '' THEN
                  ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L := CUItemBlockingMgt_L.IsItemBlocked(ItemBlockingSetup_L,
                    BlockingType_L::"Responsibility Center",SalesHeader_L."Order Date",
                    RecSASLine_P."No.",ResponsibilityCenter_L,FlowType,RecSASLine_P."Variant Code");
              END;
          4 : BEGIN // Blocking Sell-to Customer
                IF SalesHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> '' THEN
                  ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L := CUItemBlockingMgt_L.IsItemBlocked(ItemBlockingSetup_L,
                     BlockingType_L::Customer,SalesHeader_L."Order Date",
                     RecSASLine_P."No.",SalesHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.",FlowType,RecSASLine_P."Variant Code");
              END;
        END;

        CASE ReturnValueIsItemBlocked_L OF
          1 : //Blocking -> send error
              ERROR(Text004_L,ItemBlockingSetup_L.Type,ItemBlockingSetup_L."No.",ItemBlockingSetup_L."Blocking Type",
                ItemBlockingSetup_L."Blocking No.");
          2 : EXIT; // Authorization -> stop searching
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20161026-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3169-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchProductNo@8053681(ProductId_P@8053600 : Text[54];VAR Variant_Var_P@8053601 : Boolean;VAR ItemNo_Var_P@8053602 : Code[20];VAR VariantNo_Var_P@8053603 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      Pos_L@8053604 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de reconstituer le No . mettre sur la ligne de commande en fonction de si un variant . 't' utilis' ou non
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - ProductId_P : Le n› du produit CRM . d'crypter
      //    - Variant_Var_P : Le n› du produit CRM . d'crypter
      //    - ItemNo_Var_P : Le n› du produit CRM . d'crypter
      //    - VariantNo_Var_P : Le n› du produit CRM . d'crypter
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Pos_L := STRPOS(ProductId_P, GetItemSeparator());
      IF (Pos_L > 1) AND (STRLEN(ProductId_P) > 5) THEN BEGIN
        ItemNo_Var_P := COPYSTR(ProductId_P, 1, Pos_L - 1);
        VariantNo_Var_P := COPYSTR(ProductId_P, Pos_L + 1, 20);
        Variant_Var_P := TRUE;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        ItemNo_Var_P := COPYSTR(ProductId_P, 1, 20);
        Variant_Var_P := FALSE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE RemoveSeparator@8053713(Code_P@8053600 : Text[54]) Result_L : Text[54];
    VAR
      Pos_L@8053604 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de reconstituer un code en enlevant le s'parateur s'il existe
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Le code . d'crypter
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : r'sultat
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Pos_L := STRPOS(Code_P, GetSeparator());
      IF (Pos_L > 1) THEN
        Result_L := COPYSTR(Code_P, Pos_L + 1, 54)
      ELSE
        Result_L := Code_P;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatDateFromCrm@8053655(TxtDate_P@8053600 : Text[22]) Result_L : Date;
    VAR
      Date_L@8053601 : Text[10];
      Position_L@8053602 : Integer;
      Day_L@8053603 : Integer;
      Month_L@8053604 : Integer;
      Year_L@8053605 : Integer;
      TxtMonth_L@8053606 : Text[2];
      TxtYear_L@8053607 : Text[4];
      TxtDay_L@8053608 : Text[2];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de reconstituer la date venant du CRM via le connecteur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - TxtDate_P : La date . d'crypter
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : la date au format Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Position_L := STRPOS(TxtDate_P, ' ');
      IF Position_L > 0 THEN
        Date_L := COPYSTR(TxtDate_P, 1, Position_L - 1)
      ELSE
        Date_L := TxtDate_P;

      IF Date_L = '' THEN
        Result_L := 0D
      ELSE BEGIN
        TxtMonth_L := COPYSTR(Date_L, 1, STRPOS(Date_L, '/') - 1);
        EVALUATE(Month_L, TxtMonth_L);
        Date_L := COPYSTR(Date_L, STRPOS(Date_L, '/') + 1, 7);
        TxtDay_L := COPYSTR(Date_L, 1, STRPOS(Date_L, '/') - 1);
        EVALUATE(Day_L, TxtDay_L);
        TxtYear_L := COPYSTR(Date_L, STRPOS(Date_L, '/') + 1, 4);
        EVALUATE(Year_L, TxtYear_L);
        Result_L := DMY2DATE(Day_L, Month_L, Year_L);
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatDateToCrm@8053656(Date_P@8053600 : Date) Result_L : Text[22];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de construire la date allant vers le CRM via le connecteur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Date_P : La date . transformer
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : la date au format CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF Date_P < DMY2DATE(1,1,1900) THEN
        Result_L := '#NULL#'
      ELSE
        Result_L := STRSUBSTNO('%1 00:00:01 AM', FORMAT(Date_P, 0, '<Month>/<Day>/<Year4>'));

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatBoolFromCrm@8053661(TexteBool_P@8053600 : Code[10]) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de convertir un r'sultat bool'en du CRM en bool'en Nav sans problSme de traduction
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - TexteBool_P : Texte retourn' par le CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : R'sultat en bool'en
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      IF TexteBool_P = 'TRUE' THEN
        Result_L := TRUE;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatBoolToCrm@8053733(Bool_P@8053600 : Boolean) Result_L : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de convertir un bool'en en donn'e interpr'table par la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Bool_P : Texte retourn' par le CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : R'sultat (0 ou 1 en texte)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '0';

      IF Bool_P = TRUE THEN
        Result_L := '1';

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatDecFromCrm@8053676(TextDec_P@8053600 : Text[30]) Result_L : Decimal;
    VAR
      TxtDec_L@8053601 : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de convertir un texte repr'sentant un d'cimal venant du CRM en d'cimal Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - TextDec_P : Texte retourn' par le CRM
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : R'sultat en bool'en
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      TxtDec_L := CONVERTSTR(TextDec_P, '.', ',');
      IF TxtDec_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        EVALUATE(Result_L, TxtDec_L);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Result_L := 0;
      END;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatDecToCrm@8053680(Dec_P@8053600 : Decimal) Result_L : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de convertir un d'cimal pour l'utiliser avec le connecteur CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Dec_P : d'cimal . convertir
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : R'sultat en bool'en
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FORMAT(Dec_P, 0, 1);
      Result_L := CONVERTSTR(Result_L, ',', '.');
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE FormatIntToCrm@8053682(Int_P@8053600 : Integer) Result_L : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de convertir un entier pour l'utiliser avec le connecteur CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Int_P : Entier . convertir
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : R'sultat en bool'en
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FORMAT(Int_P, 0, 1);
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE LogFile@8053689(recJournal_P@8053603 : Record 8053603);
    VAR
      cduToolsFile_L@8053601 : Codeunit 8061231;
      cduEngineManagement_L@8053600 : Codeunit 8061230;
      ConnectorName_L@8053609 : Code[20];
      ImpExpFolder_L@8053608 : Text[256];
      LogFolder_L@8053607 : Text[256];
      ExtensionFile_L@8053606 : Text[10];
      PrefixFile_L@8053605 : Text[10];
      FileLogName_L@8053604 : Text[256];
      FileName_L@8053611 : Text[256];
      FilePath_L@8053610 : Text[256];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de cr'er un fichier de log en cas de message d'erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recJournal_P : Message d'erreur
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : R'sultat en bool'en
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      cduEngineManagement_L.GetParameter(CODEUNIT::"CMC Synchro Dynamics CRM",
        ConnectorName_L, ImpExpFolder_L, LogFolder_L, ExtensionFile_L, PrefixFile_L);
      cduToolsFile_L.LogInit(LogFolder_L, 'SynchroCRM');
      IF (recJournal_P.Status = recJournal_P.Status::Error) AND (recJournal_P.Error_ <> '') THEN BEGIN
        //log d'une erreur
        cduToolsFile_L.SplitFilename(recJournal_P."File Name", FilePath_L, FileName_L);
        cduToolsFile_L.LogWriteErreur(FileName_L + ':' + recJournal_P.Error_);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF (recJournal_P.Status = recJournal_P.Status::Finished) AND (recJournal_P.Error_ <> '') THEN BEGIN
          //log d'une information
          cduToolsFile_L.SplitFilename(recJournal_P."File Name", FilePath_L, FileName_L);
          cduToolsFile_L.LogWriteInfo(FileName_L + ':' + recJournal_P.Error_);
        END;
      END;

      FileLogName_L := cduToolsFile_L.LogName;
      //Fermeture du fichier de log
      cduToolsFile_L.LogClose();
      //Enregistrement du log
      CASE cduToolsFile_L.LogErreurType OF
        'E': cduEngineManagement_L.InsertError(CODEUNIT::"CMC Synchro Dynamics CRM", FileLogName_L);
        'I': cduEngineManagement_L.InsertLog(CODEUNIT::"CMC Synchro Dynamics CRM", FileLogName_L);
        ELSE
          cduEngineManagement_L.InsertOk(CODEUNIT::"CMC Synchro Dynamics CRM");
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<--Fonction Dynamics365-->"@60000();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingEvent_D365@60208();
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053603;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053603;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053601;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053603;
      cduAddError_L@8053605 : Codeunit 8053603;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle

      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Status);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Status, recJournal_L.Status::" ", recJournal_L.Status::Error);
      IF NOT(recJournal_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
        IF InitializeCrmConnexion_D365 AND InitializeCrmConnexion2_D365 THEN BEGIN
          IF recJournal_L.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L."Entry No.");
              recJournalSearch_L.RESET;
              recJournalSearch_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Status, Origine, Events, Guid, "Entry No.");
              recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Status, recJournalSearch_L.Status::" ", recJournalSearch_L.Status::Error);
              recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Origine, recJournal_L.Origine);
              recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Events, recJournal_L.Events);
              recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Guid, recJournal_L.Guid);
              recJournalSearch_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '<%1', recJournal_L."Entry No.");
              //Recherche si l'ordre a d'j. 't' reÎu et est toujours en cours
              IF NOT(recJournalSearch_L.FINDFIRST) THEN BEGIN
                //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                cduSynchroError_L.SetParameter_D365(recCrmOrganisation_L,recJournal_L.Origine,recJournal_L.Events, recJournal_L.Guid,
                                                    NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
                IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN()) THEN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L.Error_))
                ELSE BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ := '';
                END;

                IF recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                  {
                  // ****D365**** A activer plutard
                  //on note toutes les commandes simplifi'es comme 'tant . lancer
                  IF recJournalUpdate_L.Origine = 'CRM_SALESORDER_EVENT' THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.Release := TRUE;
                  END;
                  }
                  //Mise . jour du journal
                  IF (recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ = '') OR (recJournal_L.Error_ = TextDeletevent) THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.Status := recJournalUpdate_L.Status::Finished;
                    //la commande est cr''e et lanc'e
                    recJournalUpdate_L.Release := FALSE;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L.Error_, Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Status := recJournalUpdate_L.Status::Finished;
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L.Error_, STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                        STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L.Error_));
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Status := recJournalUpdate_L.Status::Error;
                    END;
                  END;
                  {
                  // ****D365**** A activer plutard
                  IF recJournalUpdate_L.Origine = 'CRM_SALESORDER_EVENT' THEN BEGIN
                    CLEAR(cduAddError_L);
                    cduAddError_L.SetParameter(recJournalUpdate_L.Error_, recJournalUpdate_L.Guid,
                    NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
                    //on lance la mise . jour mais on ne fait rien si une erreur se produit
                    IF cduAddError_L.RUN() THEN ;
                  END;}
                END;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ := Text8053639;
                recJournalUpdate_L.Status := recJournal_L.Status::Finished;
                recJournalUpdate_L.Release := FALSE;
              END;

              IF (recJournalUpdate_L.Status = recJournal_L.Status::Finished) AND (recJournalUpdate_L.Release = FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                recJournalUpdate_L.DELETE(TRUE);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF recJournalUpdate_L.Error_ <> Text8053698 THEN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
              END;
              COMMIT;
            UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      ClearConnector_D365();

      recJournalUpdate_L.RESET;
      recJournalUpdate_L.SETRANGE(Status, recJournal_L.Status::Finished);
      recJournalUpdate_L.SETRANGE(Release, FALSE);
      IF NOT(recJournalUpdate_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        recJournalUpdate_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);
      END;
      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingRelease_D365@60206(recJournal_P@8053600 : Record 8053603);
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053601 : Record 8053603;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduAddError_L@8053602 : Codeunit 8053603;
      SalesHeaderCode_L@8053604 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Lancement des commandes indiqu'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recJournal_P : Journal portant le filtre
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recJournal_L.GET(recJournal_P."Entry No.");
      IF recJournal_L.Status = recJournal_L.Status::Finished THEN BEGIN
        IF InitializeCrmConnexion() AND InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
          SalesHeaderCode_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053629, recJournal_L.Guid, FALSE);
          IF SalesHeaderCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            //On essaie de lancer la commande
            CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
            cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(SalesHeaderCode_L);
            IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
              recJournal_L.Error_ := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournal_L.Error_));
            END ELSE BEGIN
              recJournal_L.Error_ := '';
              recJournal_L.Release := FALSE;
            END;
          END;
          CLEAR(cduAddError_L);
          cduAddError_L.SetParameter(recJournal_L.Error_, recJournal_L.Guid, NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
          //on lance la mise . jour mais on ne fait rien si une erreur se produit
          IF cduAddError_L.RUN() THEN ;

          recJournal_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
      ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunEvent_D365@60003(recCrmOrganisation_P@8053604 : Record 8053075;CodeOrigine_P@1000000002 : Text[50];CodeEvent_P@8053602 : Text[50];GuidTxt_P@1000000000 : Text[50]) Result_L : Text[250];
    VAR
      crmGuid_L@8053603 : GUID;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053602;
      Error_L@8053607 : Text[250];
      recElementCampagne_L@8053606 : Record 8053194;
      recDetailsElementCampagne_L@8053609 : Record 8053195;
      recContratCadreAppro_L@8053610 : Record 8053239;
      recLigneContratCadreAppro_L@8053611 : Record 8053240;
      recContratCadreCollecte_L@8053613 : Record 8053208;
      recLigneContratCadreCollecte_L@8053612 : Record 8053209;
      recDetailLigContrCadreColl_L@8053614 : Record 8053210;
      recSalesOrder_L@8053601 : Record 36;
      recParcellaire_L@8053605 : Record 8053123;
      recParcel_L@8053615 : Record 8053122;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gestion des demandes reÎues du CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Soci't' . l'origine de la demande
      //    - CodeOrigine_P : Code origine identifiant la demande
      //    - CodeEvent_P : Code de l''vSnement survenu
      //    - GuidTxt_P : GUID CRM de l'entit' . l'origine de l'action
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique la r'ussite de l'op'ration
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      CASE UPPERCASE(CodeOrigine_P) OF
        'ACCOUNT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION': BEGIN
              //Insertion du nouveau contact dans la table CMC MC Third party to create
              IF recCrmOrganisation_P."Active MC Third Party" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
                //here1
                Result_L := GetTierMSFromCrm_D365(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P);
              END;
            END;
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //Insertion du nouveau contact dans la table CMC MC Third party to create
              IF recCrmOrganisation_P."Active MC Third Party" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
                Result_L := GetTierMSFromCrm_D365(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P);
              END;
            END;
            TextDelete: BEGIN
            END;
          END;
        END;

        // ****D365
        'CONTACT' : BEGIN
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION','MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              Result_L := GetContactFromCrm_D365(crmGuid_L);
            END;
          END;
        END;

        'PLUS_ADRESSE' : BEGIN
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION','MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              Result_L := GetAdressFromCrm_D365(crmGuid_L);
            END;
          END;
        END;

        {. activer plutard

        'CRM_SALESORDER_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION': BEGIN
              //Insertion de la nouvelle commande comme 'tant une commande
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF GetSimpleOrder(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
              END ELSE
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recSalesOrder_L.TABLECAPTION);
            END;
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //Insertion de la nouvelle commande comme 'tant une commande
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF GetSimpleOrder(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
              END ELSE
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recSalesOrder_L.TABLECAPTION);
            END;
            TextDelete: BEGIN
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_ELEMENTCAMPAIGN_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF SyncElementCampagne(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
                COMMIT;
              END ELSE
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recElementCampagne_L.TABLECAPTION);
            END;
            TextDelete: BEGIN
              DeleteElementCampagne(crmGuid_L);
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_ELEMENTCAMPAIGNDETAIL_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_SUPPLYAGREEMENT_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF SyncContratCadreAppro(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
                COMMIT;
              END ELSE
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recContratCadreAppro_L.TABLECAPTION);
            END;
            TextDelete: BEGIN
              DeleteContratCadreAppro(crmGuid_L);
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_SUPPLYAGREEMENTLINE_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              Result_L := '';
              COMMIT;
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_COLLECTMASTERAGREEMENT_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF SyncContratCadreCollecte(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
                COMMIT;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recContratCadreCollecte_L.TABLECAPTION);
              END;
            END;
            TextDelete: BEGIN
              DeleteContratCadreCollecte(GuidTxt_P);
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_COLLECTAGREEMENTLINE_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
                Result_L := '';
                COMMIT;
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_PLOT_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF SyncParcel(crmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
                COMMIT;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recParcel_L.TABLECAPTION);
              END;
            END;
            // --> 20160830-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21027-BSI01
            {before
            TextDelete: BEGIN
            after}
            'SUPPRESSION' : BEGIN
            // <-- 20160830-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21027-BSI01
              DeleteParcel(GuidTxt_P);
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_PLOTPLAN_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION',
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //insertion/modification de l'entit'
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF SyncParcellaire(crmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
                COMMIT;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recParcellaire_L.TABLECAPTION);
              END;
            END;
            // --> 20160830-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21027-BSI01
            {before
            TextDelete: BEGIN
            after}
            'SUPPRESSION' : BEGIN
            // <-- 20160830-WI00-ADV01.01.00-21027-BSI01
              DeleteParcellaire(GuidTxt_P);
            END;
          END;
        END;
        'CRM_CMC_TPGAGREEMENT_EVENT' : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          CASE CodeEvent_P OF
            'CREATION': BEGIN
              //Insertion de la nouvelle commande comme 'tant une commande
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF GetTPGContract(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
              END ELSE
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, Text8053692);
            END;
            'MODIFICATION': BEGIN
              //Insertion de la nouvelle commande comme 'tant une commande
              crmGuid_L := GuidTxt_P;
              IF GetTPGContract(crmGuid_L, recCrmOrganisation_P) THEN BEGIN
                Result_L := '';
              END ELSE
                Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, Text8053692);
            END;
            TextDelete: BEGIN
            END;
          END;
        END
        }
        ELSE BEGIN
          ERROR(Text8053698);
        END;

      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ImportSimpleOrder_D365@60142(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      cduImportOrder_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recSASHeaderUpd_L@8053602 : Record 8053610;
      recSASHeaderLine_L@8053601 : Record 8053611;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // import d'une commande SAS
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeader_P : ent^te de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //tentative d'import de la commande
      CLEAR(cduImportOrder_L);
      cduImportOrder_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::Order, NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
      cduImportOrder_L.SetParameterOrder(recSASHeader_P);
      IF NOT(cduImportOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
        recSASHeaderUpd_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeader_P."SAS Status"::Error;
        recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
        recSASHeaderUpd_L.MODIFY;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recSASHeaderUpd_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        //suppression des lignes termin'es
        recSASHeaderLine_L.RESET;
        recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
        recSASHeaderLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASHeaderLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
        // --> 20171114-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
        recSASHeaderLine_L.SETRANGE(Deleted,FALSE);
        // <-- 20171114-WI00-ADV01.01.00-30947-BSI01
        IF recSASHeaderLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
          //la commande est int'gr'e et il n'y a plus de ligne
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status"::Created;
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          recSASHeaderUpd_L.MODIFY;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //il reste des lignes en erreur
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASHeaderUpd_L."SAS Status"::Error;
          recSASHeaderUpd_L."Synchro Error" := Text8053683;
          recSASHeaderUpd_L.MODIFY;
        END;
      END;

      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertSimpleOrder_D365@60138(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053601 : Record 8053610;
      recHeader_L@8053602 : Record 36;
      Modify_L@8053603 : Boolean;

      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053613 : Codeunit 414;

      recShipmentMethod_L@8053000 : Record 10;
      ApprovalManagement_L@8053001 : Codeunit 439;

      Module_L@8053004 : 'Purchase,Sales,Service,Reminder,Finance Charge';

      StatusReleased_L@1000000003 : Boolean;
      RecSalesLineCount_L@1000000004 : Record 37;
      LineCount_L@1000000005 : Integer;
      CUReleaseSalesDoc_L@1000000006 : Codeunit 414;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une commande du SAS
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeader_P : ent^te de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //v'rification ent^te complSte
      IF recSASHeader_P."Read Complete" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        GetCompanyMcCode();
        GetSimpleOrder(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid", recCompagny_G);
      END;

      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
      IF recSASHeader_L."Read Complete" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ERROR(Text8053678);
      END;

      IF recSASHeader_P.Type = recSASHeader_P.Type::"TPG Contract" THEN BEGIN
        InsertTPGContract(recSASHeader_P);
        EXIT;
      END;

      StatusReleased_L := FALSE;
      LineCount_L := 0;

      //Une commande en cours existe d'j.
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      IF recSASHeader_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, recSASHeader_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            StatusReleased_L := TRUE;
            CLEAR(RecSalesLineCount_L);
            RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",recHeader_L."Document Type");
            RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",recHeader_L."No.");
            LineCount_L := RecSalesLineCount_L.COUNT;
          END;

          IF recHeader_L."Freezed Document" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            IF recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
              //R'ouverture de la commande pour int'grer la suite
              CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
              cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(recHeader_L."No.");
              cduRunOrder_L.SetReopenOnly(TRUE);
              IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
                ERROR(Text8053680, GETLASTERRORTEXT());
              END;
              COMMIT;
            END;
            Modify_L := TRUE;
            recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order, recSASHeader_L."No.");
          END;
        END;
      END;

      GetSetup();

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ent^te
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(recHeader_L);
        recHeader_L.INIT;
        recHeader_L."Document Type" := recHeader_L."Document Type"::Order;
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", WORKDATE);
        recHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        recSASHeader_L."No." := recHeader_L."No.";
        recSASHeader_L.MODIFY;

        IF recHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." THEN BEGIN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.", recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
          recCustomer_L.GET(recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
          IF recCustomer_L."Group Account Code" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Group Account Code", recCustomer_L."Group Account Code");
        END;

        IF recSASHeader_L."Payment Terms Code" <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."Payment Terms Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Payment Terms Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Terms Code", recSASHeader_L."Payment Terms Code");

        IF recSASHeader_L."Payment Method Code" <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."Payment Method Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Payment Method Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Payment Method Code", recSASHeader_L."Payment Method Code");

        IF recSASHeader_L."Requested Delivery Date" <> 0D THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date", recSASHeader_L."Requested Delivery Date");
        IF recSASHeader_L."CMC Campaign No." <> '' THEN
          IF recHeader_L."CMC Campaign No." <> recSASHeader_L."CMC Campaign No." THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("CMC Campaign No.", recSASHeader_L."CMC Campaign No.");

        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("External Document No.", recSASHeader_L."External Document No.");

        IF (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> '') AND (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> recHeader_L."Salesperson Code") THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Salesperson Code", recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code");

        //utilisation d'une adresse de livraison uniquement si ce n'est pas une adresse principale
        IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recHeader_L."Ship-to Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code" THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code");
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Address", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address");
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address 2" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Address 2", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Address 2");
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Code" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Post Code", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to Post Code");
          IF recSASHeader_L."Ship-to City" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to City", recSASHeader_L."Ship-to City");
        END ELSE BEGIN
          recAddr_L.RESET;
          recAddr_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", recSASHeader_P."Sell-to Customer No.");
          IF recAddr_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Ship-to Code", recAddr_L.Code);
        END;

        IF recSASHeader_L."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Shipment Method Code", recSASHeader_L."Shipment Method Code");

        IF recHeader_L."Doc. Category Code" <> recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales" THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Doc. Category Code", recCRMSetup_G."Document Category for Sales");

        IF recHeader_L."Document Source" <> recCRMSetup_G."Document Source Simple Order" THEN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Source", recCRMSetup_G."Document Source Simple Order");

        RecSalesRecSetup_L.GET;
        IF recSASHeader_L."Location Code" <> RecSalesRecSetup_L."Drop Shipment Location Code" THEN
          IF recSASHeader_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN
            IF recHeader_L."Location Code" <> recSASHeader_L."Location Code" THEN
              recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Location Code", recSASHeader_L."Location Code");

        recHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

        //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
        CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053629, recHeader_L."No.", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");

        //gestion des commentaires logistics
        LineNo_L := 0;
        IF (recSASHeader_L.Comment <> '') AND (Modify_L = FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          LineNo_L += 10000;
          CLEAR(recComment_L);
          recComment_L.INIT;
          recComment_L."Document Type" := recHeader_L."Document Type";
          recComment_L."No." := recHeader_L."No.";
          recComment_L."Document Line No." := 0;
          recComment_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
          recComment_L.Date := TODAY;
          recComment_L.Comment := COPYSTR(recSASHeader_L.Comment, 1, 80);
          recComment_L.Type := recComment_L.Type::Logistic;
          recComment_L.INSERT(TRUE);

          IF STRLEN(recSASHeader_L.Comment) > 80 THEN
            recSASHeader_L.Comment := COPYSTR(recSASHeader_L.Comment, 81, STRLEN(recSASHeader_L.Comment) - 80)
          ELSE
            recSASHeader_L.Comment := '';
        UNTIL STRLEN(recSASHeader_L.Comment) = 0;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        ModifyHeader_L := FALSE;
        recSalesLine_L.RESET;
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recHeader_L."Document Type");
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recHeader_L."No.");
        IF recSalesLine_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Order Date", WORKDATE);
          recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", WORKDATE);
          ModifyHeader_L := TRUE;
        END;
        IF recSASHeader_L."Linked Order No." <> '' THEN
          IF recSASHeader_L."Linked Order No." <> recHeader_L."Linked Order No." THEN BEGIN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Linked Order No.", recSASHeader_L."Linked Order No.");
            ModifyHeader_L := TRUE;
          END;
        IF ModifyHeader_L = TRUE THEN
          recHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;

      //Si tout c'est bien pass', on sauvegarde l'ent^te de la commande avant d'ins'rer les lignes
      //Sauvegarde de l'int'gration de la commande
      COMMIT;

      //recherche des lignes de la commande
      recLine_L.RESET;
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF recLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //tentative d'import de la ligne
          CLEAR(cduImportLine_L);
          cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::OrderLine, NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterOrderLine(recLine_L);
          IF NOT(cduImportLine_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Error;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          END;
          recLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
          COMMIT;
        UNTIL recLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
      IF (recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Open) AND StatusReleased_L THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(RecSalesLineCount_L);
        RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",recHeader_L."Document Type");
        RecSalesLineCount_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",recHeader_L."No.");
        IF (RecSalesLineCount_L.COUNT = LineCount_L) AND (RecSalesLineCount_L.COUNT <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          recHeader_L.Status := recHeader_L.Status::Released;
          recHeader_L.MODIFY;
          CUReleaseSalesDoc_L.ReleaseSalesLines(recHeader_L);
        END;
      END;

      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
      IF (recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Open) THEN BEGIN
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recHeader_L."Document Type");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recHeader_L."No.");
      IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");
        recHeader_L."Is Manual Release" := TRUE;
        recHeader_L.MODIFY;
        COMMIT;
        CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
        cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(recHeader_L."No.");
        cduRunOrder_L.SetReopenOnly(FALSE);
        IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN    //lancement
          ERROR(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text8053679,GETLASTERRORTEXT), 1, 250));
        END;
      END;

      recLine_L.RESET;
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF NOT(recLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        COMMIT;
        ERROR(Text8053683);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertTPGContract_D365@60129(recSASHeader_P@8053600 : Record 8053610);
    VAR
      recSASHeader_L@8053601 : Record 8053610;
      recHeader_L@8053602 : Record 36;
      Modify_L@8053603 : Boolean;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053611;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053607 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduImportLine_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recLineUpd_L@8053609 : Record 8053611;
      StatutCode_L@8053610 : Code[10];
      Statut_L@8053612 : Integer;
      EntityName_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053613 : Codeunit 414;
      recSalesLine_L@8053614 : Record 37;
      ApprovalManagement_L@8053000 : Codeunit 439;
      MailMgt_L@8053003 : Codeunit 8060999;
      Module_L@8053002 : 'Purchase,Sales,Service,Reminder,Finance Charge';
      DocumentType_L@8053001 : ' ,Quote,Order,Return Order,Blanket Order,Invoice,Credit Memo,Shipment,Receipt';
      recCustomer_L@1000000000 : Record 18;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une commande du SAS (contrat TPG)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASHeader_P : ent^te de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //v'rification ent^te complSte
      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASHeader_P."CRM Guid");
      GetSetup();

      //Une commande en cours existe d'j.
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      IF recSASHeader_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recHeader_L.Status = recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            //R'ouverture de la commande pour int'grer la suite
            CLEAR(cduRunOrder_L);
            cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter(recHeader_L."No.");
            cduRunOrder_L.SetReopenOnly(TRUE);
            cduRunOrder_L.SetParameter2(1);
            IF NOT(cduRunOrder_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
              ERROR(Text8053680, GETLASTERRORTEXT());
            END;
            COMMIT;
          END;
          Modify_L := TRUE;
          recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.");
        END;
      END;

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ent^te
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        recHeader_L.INIT;
        recHeader_L."Document Type" := recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order";
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Document Date", WORKDATE);
        recHeader_L.INSERT(TRUE);
        recSASHeader_L."No." := recHeader_L."No.";
        recSASHeader_L.MODIFY;
      END;

      IF recHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." <> recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No." THEN BEGIN
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.", recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
          recCustomer_L.GET(recSASHeader_L."Sell-to Customer No.");
          IF recCustomer_L."Group Account Code" <> '' THEN
            recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Group Account Code", recCustomer_L."Group Account Code");
        END;

      recHeader_L.VALIDATE("External Document No.", recSASHeader_L."External Document No.");
      IF (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> '') AND (recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code" <> recHeader_L."Salesperson Code") THEN
        recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Salesperson Code", recSASHeader_L."Salesperson Code");
      recHeader_L.VALIDATE("Responsibility Center", recCRMSetup_G."TPG Responsibility Center");
      recHeader_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

      //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
      CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053694, recHeader_L."No.", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      COMMIT;

      //recherche des lignes de la commande
      CLEAR(recLine_L."CRM Guid");
      IF recLine_L.GET(recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid") THEN BEGIN
        //recherche des lignes de la commande
        IF recLine_L."SAS Status" <> recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created THEN BEGIN
          //tentative d'import de la ligne
          CLEAR(cduImportLine_L);
          cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::BlanketOrderLine, NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterOrderLine(recLine_L);
          IF NOT(cduImportLine_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Error;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          END;
          recLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
          COMMIT;
        END;
      END;

      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type", recHeader_L."No.");

      //on relance la commande
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.RESET;
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recHeader_L."Document Type");
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recHeader_L."No.");
        IF NOT(recSalesLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
        END;
      END ELSE
        MailMgt_L.InsertRec(recHeader_L."No.", Module_L::Sales, DocumentType_L::Order);
        ApprovalManagement_L.CancelSalesLineAppReqOnDocRel(recHeader_L);

      //COMMIT car on vient d'int'grer ce que l'on peut sans erreur et qu'il reste des lignes . int'grer
      recLine_L.RESET;
      recLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      recLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF NOT(recLine_L.ISEMPTY) THEN BEGIN
        COMMIT;
        ERROR(Text8053683);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertTPGContractLine_D365@60128(recSASLine_P@8053600 : Record 8053611);
    VAR
      recSASLine_L@8053602 : Record 8053611;
      recSASHeader_L@8053603 : Record 8053610;
      recSalesLine_L@8053604 : Record 37;
      LineNo_L@8053605 : Integer;
      cduRunOrder_L@8053609 : Codeunit 8053602;
      cduReleaseSalesDoc_L@8053601 : Codeunit 414;
      recHeader_L@8053612 : Record 36;
      EntityName_L@8053000 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      cduImportLine_L@8053606 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recLineUpd_L@8053607 : Record 8053611;
      SalesPriceDiscMgt_L@8053608 : Codeunit 8053017;
      AllOk_L@8053610 : Boolean;
      recLineDetails_L@8053611 : Record 8053170;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // insertion d'une ligne de commande de SAS (TPG avec GUID ligne . NULL)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSASLine_P : ligne de SAS de commande de vente . int'grer
      //  Sorties
      //    ERROR si erreur
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recSASHeader_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid");
      recSASLine_L.GET(recSASLine_P."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_P."CRM Guid");
      GetSetup();

      //Recherche du prochain num'ro de ligne
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      LineNo_L := 10000;
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recSalesLine_L.Type::Item);
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("No.", recSASLine_L."No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code", recSASLine_L."Variant Code");
      IF recSalesLine_L.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
        LineNo_L := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
        Modify_L := TRUE;
      END;

      //Cr'ation d'une nouvelle ligne
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.INIT;
        recSalesLine_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
        recSalesLine_L."Document Type" := recSalesLine_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order";
        recSalesLine_L."Document No." := recSASHeader_L."No.";
        recSalesLine_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
        recSalesLine_L.Type := recSalesLine_L.Type::Item;
        //Insertion de la ligne
        recSalesLine_L.INSERT(TRUE);
      END;

      recSalesLine_L."System Created" := TRUE;
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("No.", recSASLine_L."No.");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Variant Code", recSASLine_L."Variant Code");

      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity, recSASLine_L.Quantity);
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Starting Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Starting Date");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Contract Ending Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Ending Date");
      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date", recSASLine_L."Contract Starting Date");

      recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE("Responsibility Center", recCRMSetup_G."TPG Responsibility Center");

      recSalesLine_L."System Created" := FALSE;
      recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);

      //sauvegarde du GUID de la commande pour pouvoir int'grer les lignes
      CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053695, recSalesLine_L."Document No." + GetSeparator() + FORMAT(recSalesLine_L."Line No."),
        recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");

      //Si tout c'est bien pass', on essaie de lancer la commande et de la rouvrir avant d'ins'rer les lignes suivantes
      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
      END;
      COMMIT;
      //R'ouverture si la commande est lanc'e
      recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recSASHeader_L."No.");
      IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Open THEN BEGIN
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
        cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.Reopen(recHeader_L); //r'ouverture
      END;

      //Mise . jour de la ligne de SAS
      recSASLine_L."Document No." := recSalesLine_L."Document No.";
      recSASLine_L."Line No." := recSalesLine_L."Line No.";
      //recSASLine_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
      recSASLine_L."Synchro Error" := '';
      recSASLine_L.MODIFY;
      //Sauvegarde de l'int'gration de la ligne de commande
      COMMIT;

      //recherche des lignes de la commande
      AllOk_L := TRUE;
      CLEAR(recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
      recSASLine_L.RESET;
      recSASLine_L.SETRANGE("CRM Header Guid", recSASHeader_L."CRM Guid");
      recSASLine_L.SETFILTER(recSASLine_L."CRM Guid", '<>%1', recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
      recSASLine_L.SETFILTER("SAS Status", '<>%1', recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created);
      IF recSASLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //tentative d'import de la ligne
          CLEAR(cduImportLine_L);
          cduImportLine_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::BlanketOrderSubLine, NavBPMConnectorD365_G, NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterOrderLine(recSASLine_L);
          cduImportLine_L.SetParameterBlanketOrderLine(recSalesLine_L);
          IF NOT(cduImportLine_L.RUN) THEN BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recSASLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Error;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error"));
            AllOk_L := FALSE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            recLineUpd_L.GET(recSASLine_L."CRM Header Guid", recSASLine_L."CRM Guid");
            recLineUpd_L."SAS Status" := recSASLine_L."SAS Status"::Created;
            recLineUpd_L."Synchro Error" := '';
          END;
          recLineUpd_L.MODIFY;
          COMMIT;
        UNTIL recSASLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      IF AllOk_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        //Si tout c'est bien pass', on essaie de lancer la commande
        recHeader_L.GET(recHeader_L."Document Type"::"Blanket Order", recHeader_L."No.");
        IF recHeader_L.Status <> recHeader_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.DisableEmail(FALSE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.SetFromSync(TRUE);
          cduReleaseSalesDoc_L.RUN(recHeader_L); //lancement
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        ERROR(Text8053696);
      END;
    END;

    END;

    PROCEDURE InitializeCrmConnexion_D365@60060() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      paramMessageGUID_L@1000000005 : GUID;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Initialisation des variables globales pour la connexion . Dynamics CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      GetSetup();

      //V'rification des paramStres de connexion . Dynamics 365
      //RecCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("CRM URL Root"); // Ce n'est plus utilis'
      recCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("CRM URL");
      //RecCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("CRM Organization"); // Ce n'est plus utilis'
      recCRMSetup_G.TESTFIELD("User Login");

      IF ISCLEAR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G) THEN
        CREATE(NavBPMConnectorD365_G);

      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.SetErrorLogging(NOT(recCRMSetup_G."No Log by Connector"));

      //Application des paramStres de connexion
      //NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmRootURL := RecCRMSetup_G."CRM URL Root"; // Ce n'est plus utilis'
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmURL := recCRMSetup_G."CRM URL";
      //NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmOrganization := RecCRMSetup_G."CRM Organization"; // Ce n'est plus utilis'
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmLogin := recCRMSetup_G."User Login";
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmPassword := recCRMSetup_G."User Password";

      //Initialisation de la connexion
      Result_L := NavBPMConnectorD365_G.InitializeConnexion;
      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ErrorConnection_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G.GetInitializationLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnection_G));
        CLEAR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        ErrorConnection_G := '';
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitializeCrmConnexionOnComp_D@60059(Company_P@8053600 : Text[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      paramMessageGUID_L@1000000005 : GUID;
      recCRMSetup_L@8053601 : Record 8053602;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Initialisation des variables globales pour la connexion . Dynamics CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      recCRMSetup_L.CHANGECOMPANY(Company_P);
      recCRMSetup_L.GET();

      //V'rification des paramStres de connexion . Dynamics CRM
      recCRMSetup_L.TESTFIELD("CRM URL Root");
      recCRMSetup_L.TESTFIELD("CRM URL");
      recCRMSetup_L.TESTFIELD("CRM Organization");
      recCRMSetup_L.TESTFIELD("User Login");

      IF ISCLEAR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G) THEN
        CREATE(NavBPMConnectorD365_G);

      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.SetErrorLogging(NOT(recCRMSetup_G."No Log by Connector"));

      //Application des paramStres de connexion
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmRootURL := recCRMSetup_L."CRM URL Root";
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmURL := recCRMSetup_L."CRM URL";
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmOrganization := recCRMSetup_L."CRM Organization";
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmLogin := recCRMSetup_L."User Login";
      NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmPassword := recCRMSetup_L."User Password";

      //Initialisation de la connexion
      Result_L := NavBPMConnectorD365_G.InitializeConnexion;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetState_D365@60030(Entity_P@8053601 : Text[50];Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50];StatusCode_P@8053603 : Integer) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      Subject_L@8053602 : Text[30];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet d'activer ou d'sactiver un enregistrement CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Entity_P : Entit'
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'entit' . d'sactiver
      //    - StatusCode_P : Code statut (entier)
      //  Sorties
      //    - Indique la r'ussite de l'op'ration
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.ResetAllSelection();
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.ResetAllQuery();
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.Reset(Entity_P);
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.ResetQuery(Entity_P);
        Subject_L := '';
        IF Entity_P = 'salesorder' THEN BEGIN
          CASE StatusCode_P OF
            4: BEGIN
              Subject_L := 'CANCELED';
            END;
            100001: BEGIN
              Subject_L := 'PROCESS';
            END;
            100002: BEGIN
              Subject_L := 'PROCESS';
            END;
          END;
        END;
        Result_L := NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.SetState(Entity_P, CleanGuidToCRM(Guid_P), StatusCode_P, Subject_L);

        IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          ErrorConnector_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnector_G));
          CLEAR(NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
          ERROR(ErrorConnector_G);
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SelectMultipleRecords_D365@60029(Entity_P@8053600 : Text[50]) Result_L : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de filtrer sur un champ et de r'cup'rer tous les r'sultats et se positionne sur le premier
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Entity_P : Entit'
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Nombre d'enregistrements trouv's
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := NavBPMConnectorD365_G.SelectMultipleRecords(Entity_P);
      IF Result_L > 0 THEN
        NavBPMConnectorD365_G.FindFirst(Entity_P);
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;
