    PROCEDURE ReadMessageQueue@60002() Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@60000 : Text[50];
      Count_L@60001 : Integer;
      TxtGuid_L@60003 : Text[50];
      recJournal_L@60004 : Record 8053603;
      recJournalSearch_L@60005 : Record 8053603;
      EntryNo_L@60007 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion_D365 THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := FALSE;
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_messagequeue';
        ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
        Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            TxtGuid_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_messagequeueid');
            //Ajout de la demande dans le journal
            CLEAR(recJournalSearch_L);
            IF recJournalSearch_L.FINDLAST THEN
              EntryNo_L := recJournalSearch_L."Entry No." + 1
            ELSE EntryNo_L := 1;

            recJournal_L.INIT;
            recJournal_L."Entry No." := EntryNo_L;
            recJournal_L."File Date" := TODAY;
            recJournal_L."File Time" := TIME;
            recJournal_L.Status := recJournal_L.Status::Received;
            recJournal_L.Origine := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_origine');
            recJournal_L.Events := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_events');
            recJournal_L.Guid := FormatGuid(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_guid'));
            recJournal_L.Username := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_username');

            CLEAR(recJournalSearch_L);
            recJournalSearch_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Status, Origine, Events, Guid, "Entry No.");
            recJournalSearch_L.SETFILTER(Status, '<>%1', recJournalSearch_L.Status::Finished);
            recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Origine, recJournal_L.Origine);
            recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Events, recJournal_L.Events);
            recJournalSearch_L.SETRANGE(Guid, recJournal_L.Guid);
            IF NOT recJournalSearch_L.FINDFIRST THEN
              recJournal_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            COMMIT;
            DeleteMessageQueue(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L);
            Count_L -= 1;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1)) OR (Count_L = 0);
        END;
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;
      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ErrorConnector_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnector_G));
        ERROR(ErrorConnector_G);
      END ELSE
        CLEAR(ErrorConnection_G);
      ClearConnector_D365();
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteMessageQueue@60001(Entity_P@8053601 : Text[50];Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de supprimer un enregistrement de l'entit' MessageQueue
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Entity_P : Entit'
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'enregistrement . supprimer
      //  Sorties
      //    - Indique la r'ussite de l'op'ration
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion2_D365 THEN BEGIN
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.ResetAllSelection();
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.ResetAllQuery();
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.Reset(Entity_P);
        NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.ResetQuery(Entity_P);
        Result_L := NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.DeleteRecord(Entity_P, CleanGuidToCRM(Guid_P));
        IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          ErrorConnector_G := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnector_G));
          CLEAR(NavBPMConnectorD365_2_G);
          ERROR(ErrorConnector_G);
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE "-----BSI-----"@60008();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetTierMSFromCrm_D365@60009(CrmGuid_P@1000000000 : GUID;recCrmOrganisation_P@1000000001 : Record 8053075) Result_L : Text[250];
    VAR
      recThridPartyToCreate_L@1000000002 : Record 8053600;
      StatusCode_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      SynchroState_L@8053600 : Text[20];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'' le Tiers MS depuis les donn'es disponibles dans le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID du compte (contact) . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquel cr'er le contact
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := STRSUBSTNO(Text8053618, recThridPartyToCreate_L.TABLECAPTION);
      EntityName_L := 'account';


      IF NOT(recThridPartyToCreate_L.GET(recCrmOrganisation_P.Code, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'Name', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line1', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line2', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'Telephone1', '');
        //SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', '');
        //SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod', ''); to add ?
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'Fax', '');
        // --> 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_postalcode', '');
        // <-- 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line3', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_statutsynchronisation', '');
        //SetField_d365(EntityName_L, 'telephone2', '');
        //SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_responsibilitywithinthecooperative', '');
        //SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_shortname', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber', '');
        // --> 20170505-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_siret','');
        // <-- 20170505-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01



        //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
        IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
          IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber') <> '' THEN BEGIN
            // --> 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
            //GetOwnerIdGuid(CrmGuid_P);
            EXIT('');
            // <-- 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
          END;

          SynchroState_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_statutsynchronisation'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(SynchroState_L));
          //Synchronisation demand'e or En cours de synchronisation
          IF (SynchroState_L = '990260001') OR (SynchroState_L = '175490003') THEN BEGIN
            //Cr'ation du tiers MS dans la soci't' . l'origine de la demande
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.INIT;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id" := CrmGuid_P;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.Name := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'Name'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.Name));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.Address := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line1'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.Address));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Address 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line2'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Address 2"));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.City := COPYSTR(RemoveSeparator(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612,
              GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid'), FALSE)), 1, MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.City));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Phone No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'Telephone1'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Phone No."));
            //recThridPartyToCreate_L."Search Name" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_shortname'), 1,
             // MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Search Name"));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."E-Mail" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."E-Mail"));
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Home Page" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Home Page"));
            //recThridPartyToCreate_L."Country/Region Code" := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053611, GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,
              //'cmc_address1_country_id'), FALSE);
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Fax No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'Fax'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L."Fax No."));
            // --> 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Post Code" :=  GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'address1_postalcode');
            //N› SIRET
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Registration No." := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_siret');
            // <-- 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.County := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'Address1_line3'), 1,
              MAXSTRLEN(recThridPartyToCreate_L.County));
            //Gestion du StatusCode pour remplir l'Activity State
            StatusCode_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode);
            CASE StatusCode_L OF
              '2': // not active
                recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State"::"Not Active";
              '175490000': //actif sans activit'    //a rajouter
                recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State"::"Active Without Activity";
              ELSE
                recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Activity State"::Active;
            END;

            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Contact Type" := recThridPartyToCreate_L."Contact Type"::Farm;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L."Company No." := recCrmOrganisation_P.Code;
            recThridPartyToCreate_L.Status := recThridPartyToCreate_L.Status::" ";

            //synchronisation des responsabilit's
            {recThridPartyToCreate_L."Job Responsability" := COPYSTR(GetResponsability(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,
              'cmc_responsibilitywithinthecooperative')), 1, 20);
            }
            IF recThridPartyToCreate_L.INSERT(TRUE) THEN
              Result_L := '';

            //mise . jour du CRM pour indiquer que le compte est synchronis'
            ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_statutsynchronisation', '175490003'); //mettre en cours
            UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            Result_L := '';
          END;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //le compte . d'j. 't' trait'
        Result_L := '';
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GuidFinded_D365@60004(Code_P@8053600 : Text[110];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50];Caption_P@8053602 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de v'rifier que le GUID a 't' trouv' et de lancer un message d'erreur dans le cas contraire
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code trouv'
      //    - Guid_P : GUID initial
      //    - Caption_P : Ectiture . afficher en cas d'erreur
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF STRLEN(Guid_P) > 20 THEN BEGIN
        IF (Code_P = '') AND (NOT(ISNULLGUID(FormatGuid(Guid_P)))) THEN BEGIN
          ClearConnector_D365;
          ERROR(Text8053677, Caption_P);
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF (Code_P <> '') THEN BEGIN
          ClearConnector_D365;
          ERROR(Text8053687, Caption_P, Code_P);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteEntityFromNav_D365@60005(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];Guid_P@8053600 : Text[50];TypeEntity_P@8053602 : Integer;StatusCode_P@8053604 : Integer;TypeGuid_P@8053605 : Integer);
    VAR
      recGuid_L@8053603 : Record 8053601;
      recGuid2_L@8053606 : Record 8053609;
      Delete_L@8053000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gestion de la suppression d'une entit' dans CRM suite . une action dans Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : entit' de l'enregistrement
      //    - Guid_P : GUID de l'enregistrement
      //    - TypeEntity_P : Type de l'entit' . supprimer. Cette donn'e vient de la table de gestion des GUID
      //    - StatusCode_P : Le statut pour d'sactiver l'enregistrement
      //    - TypeGuid_P : Permet de choisir dans qui liste de GUID on supprime la donn'e
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_P, Guid_P) THEN BEGIN
        Delete_L := FALSE;
        CASE EntityName_P OF
          'cmc_collectmasteragreement','cmc_productquota':
            Delete_L := TRUE;
        END;
        IF Delete_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
          //si la donn'e existe dans le CRM, on le d'sactive et le d'synchronise
          IF SetState_D365(EntityName_P, Guid_P, StatusCode_P) THEN BEGIN //Inactive
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          DeleteRecord_D365(EntityName_P, Guid_P);
        END;
      END;
      //on va supprimer la valeur de la liste des GUID
      CASE TypeGuid_P OF
        1: BEGIN
          recGuid_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
          recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", TypeEntity_P);
          recGuid_L.SETRANGE(GUID, Guid_P);

          IF recGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recGuid_L.DELETE(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
        2: BEGIN
          recGuid2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
          recGuid2_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", TypeEntity_P);
          recGuid2_L.SETRANGE(GUID, Guid_P);

          IF recGuid2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            recGuid2_L.DELETE(TRUE);
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncEntity_D365@60006(EntityType_P@1000000000 : Code[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recordGUID_L@1000000001 : GUID;
      recordGUIDUpdate_L@1000000007 : GUID;
      recPostalCodeCity_L@1000000003 : Record 225;
      recCountry_L@1000000004 : Record 9;
      recPaymentTerm_L@1000000005 : Record 3;
      recUnitOfMeasure_L@8053607 : Record 204;
      LastData_L@8053600 : Code[20];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      ToProcess_L@8053602 : Boolean;
      DataRead_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      PostCodeId_L@8053605 : Code[20];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Guiduomschedule_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      recCampaign_L@8053609 : Record 8052991;
      recLocation_L@8053610 : Record 14;
      crmRecordCount_L@8053611 : Integer;
      i_L@8053612 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053613 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053614 : Integer;
      ValBufferLast_L@8053615 : Integer;
      PostalCodeIdFinded_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recRespons_L@8053617 : Record 8053161;
      PV_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      PAL_L@8053620 : Text[50];
      PAR_L@8053621 : Text[50];
      PAV_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      PAP_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      recUserSetup_L@8053624 : Record 91;
      UserId_L@8053625 : Text[1024];
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053627 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053626 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@8053618 : Record 8053167;
      Code_L@8053628 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053629 : Boolean;
      PosSlash_L@8053632 : Integer;
      LastCode_L@8053616 : Text[250];
      recFunnel_L@8053633 : Record 8053358;
      recFunnel_Line_L@8053631 : Record 8053359;
      Responsable_L@1000000002 : Text[30];
      recSalesPersonn_L@1000000008 : Record 8053608;
      TxtUserGuid_L@1000000009 : Text[50];
      recMappingSalesp_L@1000000010 : Record 8053608;
      recShipmentMethod_L@8053000 : Record 10;
      PaysIdFinded_L@1000000011 : Text[50];
      RecPaymentMethod_L@1000000013 : Record 289;
      RecPaymentTerms_L@1000000006 : Record 3;
      RecShipMethod_L@1000000012 : Record 10;
      RecModeCom_L@1000000014 : Record 60039;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise l'entit' (une table comme les codes postaux, les villes, ...) avec le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityType_P : Type d'entit' (Code)
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //RSgle de gestion mis en oeuvre :
      //Si existe dans CRM et pas dans NAV => suppression dans le CRM
      //Si existe dans NAV et pas dans CRM => cr'ation dans le CRM
      //Si existe dans NAV et CRM => met . jour la valeur dans le CRM
      //ToProcess_L : 0=Ne rien faire, 1=ajouter, 2=modifier
      //Si on a supprimer une donn'e . la fois dans NAV et dans le CRM, alors l'enregistrement dans la table de synchro des GUID restera
      //
      //Il faut synchroniser les codes postaux avant de synchroniser les villes
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      CASE UPPERCASE(EntityType_P) OF
        //Synchronisation de pays
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'COUNTRY': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'plus_pays';
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recCountry_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recCountry_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          IF recCountry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053611, recCountry_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeiso', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
                  SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeiso', recCountry_L.Code);
                  SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom', recCountry_L.Name);
                  UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCountry_D365(EntityName_L, recCountry_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
              IF NOT(SearchCountry_D365(EntityName_L, recCountry_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeiso', recCountry_L.Code);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom', recCountry_L.Name);
              IF CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053611, recCountry_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recCountry_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeiso', '');
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              ToInactive_L := FALSE;
              Code_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeiso');
              IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recCountry_L.Code) THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE
              ELSE
                IF NOT(recCountry_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                SetState_D365(EntityName_L, GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;

        {
          //Synchronisation des codes postaux
                            //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'POSTALCODE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_postalcode';
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          LastData_L := '';
          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recPostalCodeCity_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              IF (ValBuffer_L - ValBufferLast_L) >= 50 THEN BEGIN
                ValBufferLast_L := ValBuffer_L;
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBufferLast_L);
              END;
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //traitement de chaque code postal qu'une seule fois
            IF LastData_L <> recPostalCodeCity_L.Code THEN BEGIN
              LastData_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.Code;
              //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053613, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
              IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                IF ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
                  //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                  IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                    //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                    IF recPostalCodeCity_L.Code <> GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') THEN BEGIN
                      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
                      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
                      UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                    END;
                    IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                      SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                  END;
                END ELSE
                  IF NOT(SearchPostalCode_D365(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
                    ToProcess_L := TRUE;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchPostalCode_D365(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
              IF CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053613, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recPostalCodeCity_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          LastCode_L := '';
          REPEAT
            ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
            IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '>', LastCode_L);
            END;
            SetOrderBy_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
            Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
            i_L := 0;
            IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
              END;
              REPEAT
                IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                  i_L += 1;
                  ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                  ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
                END;

                ToInactive_L := FALSE;
                Code_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
                IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recPostalCodeCity_L.Code) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE BEGIN
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(Code, Code_L);
                  IF recPostalCodeCity_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;
                END;
                IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                  SetState_D365(EntityName_L, GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif
                LastCode_L := Code_L;
              UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1));
            END;
          UNTIL Count_L = 0;
        END;
       }

                SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeinsee', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
                  SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeinsee', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
                  SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
                  SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_codePostal',recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);

                  //SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_paysid',PaysIdFinded_L);
                  {
                  SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plantresponsibleid', PV_L);
                  SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rabbitresponsibleid', PAL_L);
                  SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ruminantresponsibleid', PAR_L);
                  SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_poultryresponsibleid', PAV_L);
                  SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pigresponsibleid', PAP_L);
                  }
                  UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCity_D365(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchCity_D365(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;
              //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
              IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
                SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeinsee', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
                SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
                SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_codePostal',recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
                //SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_paysid',PaysIdFinded_L);
                {
                SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plantresponsibleid', PV_L);
                SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rabbitresponsibleid', PAL_L);
                SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ruminantresponsibleid', PAR_L);
                SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_poultryresponsibleid', PAV_L);
                SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pigresponsibleid', PAP_L);
                }
                IF CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                  UpdateGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City, TxtCrmGuid_L)
                ELSE
                  EXIT(Result_L);
              END;
          UNTIL recPostalCodeCity_L.NEXT = 0;


          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          LastCode_L := '';
          REPEAT
            ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', '');
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
            IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', '>', LastCode_L);
            END;
            SetOrderBy_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle');
            Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
            i_L := 0;
            IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
              END;
              REPEAT
                IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                  i_L += 1;
                  ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                  ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
                END;

                ToInactive_L := FALSE;
                Code_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle');
                IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recPostalCodeCity_L.City) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE BEGIN
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(City, Code_L);
                  IF recPostalCodeCity_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;
                END;
                IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif
                LastCode_L := Code_L;
              UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1));
            END;
          UNTIL Count_L = 0;
        END;
       }
        //Synchronisation des utilisateurs
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'USER': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'systemuser';
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

          //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utile pour la mise . jour
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'systemuserid', '');
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_usercode', '');
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'domainname', '');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN
            REPEAT
              Responsable_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_usercode'), 1, 10);
              UserId_L := UPPERCASE(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'domainname'));
              IF UserId_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                PosSlash_L := STRPOS(UserId_L, '\');
                IF PosSlash_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
                  UserId_L := COPYSTR(UserId_L, PosSlash_L + 1, STRLEN(UserId_L) - PosSlash_L);
                END;
                UserId_L := COPYSTR(UserId_L, 1, 54);
                UpdateGuid(Text8053631, UserId_L, FormatGuid(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'systemuserid')));
                IF NOT(recSalesPersonn_L.GET(UserId_L, Responsable_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  recSalesPersonn_L.RESET;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.SETRANGE("User ID", UserId_L);
                  recSalesPersonn_L.DELETEALL;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.RESET;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.INIT;
                  recSalesPersonn_L."User ID" := UserId_L;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.Code := Responsable_L;
                  recSalesPersonn_L.INSERT(TRUE);
                END;

              END;
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;

        //Synchronisation des magasins
      //--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        'LOCATION': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_magasin';
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recLocation_L.RESET;
          recLocation_L.SETRANGE("Use As In-Transit", FALSE);
          Count_L := recLocation_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recLocation_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053623, recLocation_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                  IF recLocation_L.Code <> GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') THEN BEGIN
                    ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
                    SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recLocation_L.Code);
                    SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_nom', recLocation_L.Name);
                    UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  END;
                  IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchLocation_D365(EntityName_L, recLocation_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchLocation_D365(EntityName_L, recLocation_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recLocation_L.Code);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_nom', recLocation_L.Name);
              IF CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053623, recLocation_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recLocation_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
             ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
             IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              ToInactive_L := FALSE;
              Code_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
              IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recLocation_L.Code) THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE
              ELSE
                IF NOT(recLocation_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE
                  //Si c'est un magasin de transit, on n'en a pas besoin dans le CRM
                  IF recLocation_L."Use As In-Transit" = TRUE THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                SetState_D365(EntityName_L, GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;

      {
      // ****D365**** A activer plutard
      //Cr'ation du groupe de mesure Nav si non synchronis'
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'GROUPMEASURE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'uomschedule';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'name', '=', TextConstanteNav);
            IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid'));
                UpdateGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            END ELSE
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT(GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', TextConstanteNav);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'baseuomname', TextConstanteNav);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;

        //synchronisation des unit's de mesure
                              //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'MEASURE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'uom';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recUnitOfMeasure_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnitOfMeasure_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recUnitOfMeasure_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            Guiduomschedule_L := GetGuid(Text8053620, TextConstanteNav);
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053621, recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait mais que la donn'e n'est pas bonne, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantity', '1');
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', CleanGuidToCRM(Guiduomschedule_L));
                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchMeasure(EntityName_L, recUnitOfMeasure_L, Guiduomschedule_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
              IF NOT(SearchMeasure(EntityName_L, recUnitOfMeasure_L, Guiduomschedule_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'quantity', '1');
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'uomscheduleid', CleanGuidToCRM(Guiduomschedule_L));
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053621, recUnitOfMeasure_L.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recUnitOfMeasure_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        //Synchronisation des campagnes
                              //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'CAMPAIGN': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_productioncampaign';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recCampaign_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recCampaign_L.COUNT;

          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          IF recCampaign_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053622, recCampaign_L."No.");
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_Code', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCampaign_L."No.");
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recCampaign_L."No.");
                  IF recCampaign_L."Starting Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Starting Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Ending Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Ending Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. Start. Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedbegindate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. Start. Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedbegindate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. End Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedenddate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Creat. End Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplancreationallowedenddate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod Start. Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedbegindate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod Start. Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedbegindate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  IF recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod End Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedenddate',
                      FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Crop Rot. Mod End Date"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_croppingplanupdateallowedenddate', '#NULL#');
                  END;

                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCampaign(EntityName_L, recCampaign_L)) THEN
                 ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchCampaign(EntityName_L, recCampaign_L)) THEN
               ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCampaign_L."No.");
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recCampaign_L."No.");
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Starting Date"));
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', FormatDateToCrm(recCampaign_L."Ending Date"));
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053622, recCampaign_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recCampaign_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              ToInactive_L := FALSE;
              Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code');
              IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recCampaign_L."No.") THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE
              ELSE
                IF NOT(recCampaign_L.GET(Code_L)) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE;

              IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif

            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;

        //Synchronisation du prix
                              //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'PRICE': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'pricelevel';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          ToProcess_L := FALSE;
          TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'name', '=', TextConstanteNav);
            IF SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'pricelevelid'));
                UpdateGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            END ELSE
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF NOT(GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          END;

          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', TextConstanteNav);
            IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053632, TextConstanteNav, TxtCrmGuid_L)
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
        END;


        //Synchronisation des modalit's des lieux de d'chargement
                              //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'UNLOADINGMODALITY': BEGIN
          //synchronisation de tous les modes de d'chargement pr'sents dans Nav
          i_L := 0;
          recUnloadingMethod_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnloadingMethod_L.COUNT;
          IF recUnloadingMethod_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              SyncUnloadingMethod(recUnloadingMethod_L, COMPANYNAME, TextModify, '', recUnloadingMethod_L);
            UNTIL recUnloadingMethod_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          //d'sactivation des modes de d'chargement non pr'sents dans Nav
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '=', '175490002');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid');
              IF NOT(recUnloadingMethod_L.GET(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))) THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
              ELSE
                IF GetCodeFromGuidWithCompany(Text8053641, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE, COMPANYNAME) = '' THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;

          //synchronisation de toutes les tailles de v'hicule pr'sentes dans Nav
          i_L := 0;
          recUnloadingVehSize_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnloadingVehSize_L.COUNT;
          IF recUnloadingVehSize_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              SyncUnloadingVehSize(recUnloadingVehSize_L, COMPANYNAME, TextModify, '', recUnloadingVehSize_L);
            UNTIL recUnloadingVehSize_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          //d'sactivation de tailles de v'hicules non pr'sentes dans Nav
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');

          //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '=', '175490000');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid');
              IF NOT(recUnloadingVehSize_L.GET(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))) THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
              ELSE
                IF GetCodeFromGuidWithCompany(Text8053642, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE, COMPANYNAME) = '' THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;

          //synchronisation de tous les horaires de d'chargement pr'sents dans Nav
          i_L := 0;
          recUnloadingTime_L.RESET;
          Count_L := recUnloadingTime_L.COUNT;
          IF recUnloadingTime_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              SyncUnloadingTime(recUnloadingTime_L, COMPANYNAME, TextModify, '', recUnloadingTime_L);
            UNTIL recUnloadingTime_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;

          //d'sactivation des horaires de d'chargement non pr'sents dans Nav
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid', '');
          SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');

          //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '=', '175490001');
          SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');

          Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          i_L := 0;
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
            END;
            REPEAT
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                i_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
              END;

              TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalityid');
              IF NOT(recUnloadingTime_L.GET(GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code'))) THEN
                SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
              ELSE
                IF GetCodeFromGuidWithCompany(Text8053643, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE, COMPANYNAME) = '' THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2)
            UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        END;
        //Synchronisation des activit's, familles, sous-familles et espSces
                              //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'SPECIES': BEGIN
          Result_L := SyncActivity();
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN
            Result_L := SyncFamily();
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN
            Result_L := SyncSubFamily();
          IF Result_L = TRUE THEN
            Result_L := SyncSpecies();
        END;
        //Synchronisation des entonnoir articles
                              //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
      --
        'FUNNELFILTER': BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.RESET;
          IF recFunnel_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::EntonnoirArticle, recFunnel_L.Code, TextModify, '');
            UNTIL recFunnel_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;
      }
      END;
      Result_L := TRUE;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchCountry_D365@60016(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCountry_P@8053600 : Record 9) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      StatusCode_L@8053603 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche le pays et le synchronise s'il le trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCountry_P : Le pays . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Enregistrement trouv' et synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_codeiso', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_nom', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_codeiso', '=', recCountry_P.Code);
      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text8053611, recCountry_P.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF recCountry_P.Name <> GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_nom') THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_nom', recCountry_P.Name);
          UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState_D365(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchCity_D365@60015(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recCity_P@8053603 : Record 225) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      StatusCode_L@8053605 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche la ville et la synchronise s'il la trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recCity_P : Le pays . synchroniser
      //    - PostalCodeId_P : ID CRM du code postal si trouv'
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Enregistrement trouv' et synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_codeinsee', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      //recherche de la ville en tenant compte du code postal
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_libelle', '=', recCity_P.City);
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_P,'plus_codePostal','=',recCity_P.Code);

      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text8053612, COPYSTR(recCity_P.Code + GetSeparator() + recCity_P.City, 1,
          MAXSTRLEN(recGuid_G."Code Nav")), TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF recCity_P."INSEE No." <> GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_codeinsee') THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'plus_codeinsee', recCity_P."INSEE No.");
          UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState_D365(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchPostalCode_D365@60014(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recPostalCode_P@8053603 : Record 225) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
