    END;

    PROCEDURE GetContactFromCrm_D365@1000000014(CrmGuid_P@1000000000 : GUID) Result_L : Text[250];
    VAR
      StatusCode_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      SynchroState_L@8053600 : Text[20];
      RecContact_L@1000000003 : Record 5050;
      AccountNo_L@1000000004 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@1000000005 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cr'er un contact depuis les donn'es disponibles dans le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - CrmGuid_P : GUID du compte (contact) . synchroniser
      //    - recCrmOrganisation_P : Organisation sur laquel cr'er le contact
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'contact';
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := '';
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_contactnumber', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'parentcustomerid', ''); // nom de la soci't' parente
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'fullname', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_city', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_country', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line1', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line2', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_postalcode', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'telephone1', '');

      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'parentcustomerid'); //contact de type soci't'
        AccountNo_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
        IF AccountNo_L <> '' THEN
          GuidFinded_D365(AccountNo_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, RecContact_L."Company No.")
        ELSE EXIT('');

        IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_contactnumber') <> '' THEN
          EXIT('');

        {SynchroState_L := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_statutsynchronisation'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(SynchroState_L));
        //Synchronisation demand'e or En cours de synchronisation
        IF (SynchroState_L = '990260001') OR (SynchroState_L = '175490003') THEN BEGIN}

        RecContact_L.INIT;
        RecContact_L.Type := RecContact_L.Type::Person;
        RecContact_L.Name := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'fullname'), 1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L.Name));
        RecContact_L.VALIDATE("Company No.",AccountNo_L);
        RecContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := CrmGuid_P;
        RecContact_L.Address := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line1'),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L.Address));
        RecContact_L."Address 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line2'),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L."Address 2"));
        // post code

        RecContact_L.VALIDATE(City,COPYSTR(RemoveSeparator(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612,
                             GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_villeid'),FALSE)),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L.City)));

        RecContact_L."Phone No." := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'telephone1'),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L."Phone No."));
        RecContact_L."E-Mail" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1'),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L."E-Mail"));
        RecContact_L."Home Page" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl'),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecContact_L."Home Page"));
        RecContact_L."Contact CRM" := TRUE;

        IF RecContact_L.INSERT(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          //mise . jour du CRM pour indiquer que le compte est synchronis'
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_statutdesynchronisation', '990260002'); //Synchronis'
          UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P);
          CreateLinkedGuid(Text60000, RecContact_L."No.", CrmGuid_P);

          AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Contact, RecContact_L."No.", TextModify, '');
        END;

         //2706
      END
      ELSE Result_L := '';
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncVille@1000000016(DocumentNo_P@1000000046 : Text[250]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recordGUID_L@1000000045 : GUID;
      recordGUIDUpdate_L@1000000044 : GUID;
      recPostalCodeCity_L@1000000043 : Record 225;
      recCountry_L@1000000042 : Record 9;
      recPaymentTerm_L@1000000041 : Record 3;
      recPaymentMethod_L@1000000040 : Record 289;
      recUnitOfMeasure_L@1000000039 : Record 204;
      LastData_L@1000000038 : Code[20];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@1000000037 : Text[50];
      ToProcess_L@1000000036 : Boolean;
      DataRead_L@1000000035 : Text[50];
      PostCodeId_L@1000000034 : Code[20];
      EntityName_L@1000000033 : Text[50];
      Guiduomschedule_L@1000000032 : Text[50];
      recCampaign_L@1000000031 : Record 8052991;
      recLocation_L@1000000030 : Record 14;
      crmRecordCount_L@1000000029 : Integer;
      i_L@1000000028 : Integer;
      Count_L@1000000027 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@1000000026 : Integer;
      ValBufferLast_L@1000000025 : Integer;
      PostalCodeIdFinded_L@1000000024 : Text[50];
      recRespons_L@1000000023 : Record 8053161;
      PV_L@1000000022 : Text[50];
      PAL_L@1000000021 : Text[50];
      PAR_L@1000000020 : Text[50];
      PAV_L@1000000019 : Text[50];
      PAP_L@1000000018 : Text[50];
      recUserSetup_L@1000000017 : Record 91;
      UserId_L@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      recUnloadingMethod_L@1000000015 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@1000000014 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@1000000013 : Record 8053167;
      Code_L@1000000012 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@1000000011 : Boolean;
      PosSlash_L@1000000010 : Integer;
      LastCode_L@1000000009 : Text[250];
      recFunnel_L@1000000008 : Record 8053358;
      recFunnel_Line_L@1000000007 : Record 8053359;
      Responsable_L@1000000006 : Text[30];
      recSalesPersonn_L@1000000005 : Record 8053608;
      TxtUserGuid_L@1000000004 : Text[50];
      recMappingSalesp_L@1000000003 : Record 8053608;
      recShipmentMethod_L@1000000002 : Record 10;
      PaysIdFinded_L@1000000001 : Text[50];
      GUIDBIS@1000000000 : Text[50];
      PostCode_L@1000000047 : Code[50];
      City_L@1000000048 : Text[50];
      RecExportJournal_L@1000000049 : Record 8053606;
    BEGIN
      EntityName_L := 'plus_ville';
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

      PostCode_L := COPYSTR(DocumentNo_P, 1, STRPOS(DocumentNo_P, ':') - 1);
      City_L := COPYSTR(DocumentNo_P, STRPOS(DocumentNo_P, ':') + 1, MAXSTRLEN(DocumentNo_P));

      //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
      recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;

      recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(Code,PostCode_L);
      recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(City,City_L);

      Count_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      ValBufferLast_L := 0;
      GetSetup();

      IF recPostalCodeCity_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN

        {IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          i_L += 1;

          ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
          IF (ValBuffer_L - ValBufferLast_L) >= 50 THEN BEGIN
            ValBufferLast_L := ValBuffer_L;
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBufferLast_L);
          END;
        END; }

        ToProcess_L := FALSE;
        //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
        //PaysIdFinded_L := '{544C8A3E-B813-E711-80FE-5065F38BD4E1}';
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeinsee', '');
            //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
            IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Si le lien est bien fait, on la met . jour
              ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeinsee', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
              SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_codePostal',recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
              //SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_paysid',PaysIdFinded_L);
              UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            END;
          END ELSE
            IF NOT(SearchCity_D365(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
              ToProcess_L := TRUE;
        END ELSE
          IF NOT(SearchCity_D365(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L)) THEN
            ToProcess_L := TRUE;
          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_codeinsee', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
            SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_codePostal',recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
            //SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_paysid',PaysIdFinded_L);
            IF CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              UpdateGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City, TxtCrmGuid_L);

              IF RecExportJournal_L.GET(RecExportJournal_L.Type::City,DocumentNo_P) THEN BEGIN
                RecExportJournal_L.Closed := TRUE;
                 RecExportJournal_L.MODIFY;
              END;

            END
            ELSE
              EXIT(Result_L);
          END;
      END;
      //UNTIL recPostalCodeCity_L.NEXT = 0;

      {
      //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
      LastCode_L := '';
      REPEAT
        ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', '');
        SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
        SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
        IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle', '>', LastCode_L);
        END;
        SetOrderBy_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle');
        Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
        i_L := 0;
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
          END;
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;
              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
            END;

            ToInactive_L := FALSE;
            Code_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_libelle');
            IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recPostalCodeCity_L.City) THEN
              ToInactive_L := TRUE
            ELSE BEGIN
              recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
              recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(City, Code_L);
              IF recPostalCodeCity_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                ToInactive_L := TRUE;
            END;
            IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
              SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif
            LastCode_L := Code_L;
          UNTIL NOT(NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;
      UNTIL Count_L = 0;}
    END;

    PROCEDURE AssignOwner_D365@1000000019(OwnerGuid_P@1000000000 : Text[50];Entity_P@1000000001 : Text[50];Guid_P@1000000002 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      Result_L := NavBPMConnectorD365_G.AssignOwner(CleanGuidToCRM(OwnerGuid_P),Entity_P, CleanGuidToCRM(Guid_P));

      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN
        GetLastConnectorError_D365;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchPaymentMethode_D365@1000000023(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];RecPaymentMethod_P@8053603 : Record 289) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      StatusCode_L@8053605 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      Result_L := FALSE;

      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);

      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_code', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_code', '=', RecPaymentMethod_P.Code);

      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text60001, COPYSTR(RecPaymentMethod_P.Code, 1,
          MAXSTRLEN(recGuid_G."Code Nav")), TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF RecPaymentMethod_P.Description <> GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_nom') THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_nom', RecPaymentMethod_P.Description);
          UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState_D365(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchLocation_D365@1000000020(EntityName_P@8053601 : Text[50];recLocation_P@8053600 : Record 14) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      crmRecordCount_L@8053602 : Integer;
      StatusCode_L@8053604 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche le magasin et le synchronise s'il le trouve
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityName_P : L'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - recLocation_P : Le magasin . synchroniser
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Enregistrement trouv' et synchronis'
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      //On v'rifie si l'enregistrement existe d'j. dans le CRM
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_code', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_nom', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode, '');
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_code', '=', recLocation_P.Code);
      crmRecordCount_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_P);
      IF crmRecordCount_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //Il existe alors on va le mettre . jour et l'ajouter . la liste des GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := FormatGuid(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, EntityName_P + 'id'));
        StatusCode_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, TextStatusCode);
        UpdateGuid(Text8053623, recLocation_P.Code, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        IF recLocation_P.Name <> GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_nom') THEN BEGIN
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_P);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_P, 'cmc_nom', recLocation_P.Name);
          UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L = '2'THEN
          SetState_D365(EntityName_P, CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L), 1); //Active - Actif
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetSyncPrioriyOrder@1000000027(Type_P@1000000001 : 'Account,Order,Item,Ship-to Address,Unloading Place,Unloading Method,Unloading Size,Unloading Time,Activity,Family,SubFamily,Specie,Quota,Transcript.,ArticleTranscript.,ElementCampaign,DetailsElementCampaign,TypeContratCadre,ModeleContratCadreAppro,Abaque,CoeffAbaque,TypeEngagement,Engagement,EntonnoirArticle,TypeContratCadreCollecte,EntonnoirContratCollecte,EngContratCadreCollecte,ContratCadreCollect,LigneContratCadreCollect,DetailLigneContratCadreCollect,Campaign,Import,Season,TPG,ContractTypeCamp,Parcel,TPGHeader,TPGLine,History,NbPf,Contact,City') Result_L : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //Definir l'ordre de priorit' des entit's . synchroniser
      Result_L := 0;
      CASE Type_P OF
        Type_P::City    : Result_L := 1;
        Type_P::Account : Result_L := 2;
        Type_P::"Ship-to Address"  : Result_L := 3;
        Type_P::Contact  : Result_L := 4;
      END;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertMQContact_D365@1000000024(ParentGuid_P@1000000003 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
      Count_L@1000000001 : Integer;
      ErrorConnector_L@1000000002 : Text[250];
      MessageQueueEntity_L@1000000004 : Text[50];
      MessageQueueGUID_L@1000000005 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'contact';
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'contactid','');
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'parentcustomerid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(ParentGuid_P));
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L,'cmc_statutdesynchronisation','!=','990260002');
      Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          MessageQueueEntity_L := 'cmc_messagequeue';
          NavBPMConnectorD365_G.Reset(MessageQueueEntity_L);
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_origine','contact');
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_events','MODIFICATION');
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_guid',GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'contactid'));
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_username','Bilel');
          Result_L := CreateRecord_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L, MessageQueueGUID_L);
          Count_L -= 1;
        UNTIL NOT (NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1)) OR (Count_L = 0);
      END
      ELSE Result_L := TRUE;

      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ErrorConnector_L := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnector_L));
        ERROR(ErrorConnector_L);
      END ELSE
        CLEAR(ErrorConnector_L);
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertMQAdresses_D365@1000000026(ParentGuid_P@1000000003 : Text[50]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
      Count_L@1000000001 : Integer;
      ErrorConnector_L@1000000002 : Text[250];
      MessageQueueEntity_L@1000000004 : Text[50];
      MessageQueueGUID_L@1000000005 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      Result_L := FALSE;
      EntityName_L := 'plus_adresse';
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_adresseid','');

      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_compteid', '=', CleanGuidToCRM(ParentGuid_P));
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L,'cmc_statutdesynchronisation','!=','990260002');
      SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_isadresseprincipale','=',FormatBoolToCrm(FALSE));

      Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L);
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          MESSAGE(FORMAT(Count_L));
          MessageQueueEntity_L := 'cmc_messagequeue';
          NavBPMConnectorD365_G.Reset(MessageQueueEntity_L);
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_origine','plus_adresse');
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_events','MODIFICATION');
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_guid',GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_adresseid'));
          SetField_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L,'cmc_username','Bilel');
          Result_L := CreateRecord_D365(MessageQueueEntity_L, MessageQueueGUID_L);
          Count_L -= 1;
        UNTIL NOT (NextEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 1)) OR (Count_L = 0);
      END
      ELSE Result_L := TRUE;

      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        ErrorConnector_L := COPYSTR(NavBPMConnectorD365_G.CrmLastError(), 1, MAXSTRLEN(ErrorConnector_L));
        ERROR(ErrorConnector_L);
      END ELSE
        CLEAR(ErrorConnector_L);
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAdressFromCrm_D365@1000000031(CrmGuid_P@1000000000 : GUID) Result_L : Text[250];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      AccountNo_L@1000000004 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@1000000005 : Text[50];
      RecShipAddress_L@1000000001 : Record 222;
      RecShipAddress2_L@1000000002 : Record 222;
      AdrCode_L@1000000003 : Code[10];
      PostCodeCity_L@1000000006 : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      Result_L := '';
      EntityName_L := 'plus_adresse';
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        Result_L := '';
        EXIT(Result_L);
      END;
      ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_compteid', ''); // nom de la soci't' parente
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue1', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue2', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue3', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_code', '');
      SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_villeid', '');
      SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_pays', '');
      //On se positionne sur l'enregistrement CRM que l'on veut lire
      IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
        TxtCrmGuid_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_compteid'); //contact de type soci't'
        AccountNo_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);

        IF AccountNo_L <> '' THEN
          GuidFinded_D365(AccountNo_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, RecShipAddress_L."Customer No.")
        ELSE EXIT('');

        // --> ADD 20170419
        IF GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_code') <> '' THEN
          EXIT('');

        CLEAR(RecShipAddress2_L);
        RecShipAddress2_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.",AccountNo_L);
        IF RecShipAddress2_L.FINDLAST THEN
          AdrCode_L := INCSTR(RecShipAddress2_L.Code)
        ELSE AdrCode_L := '001';

        RecShipAddress_L.INIT;
        RecShipAddress_L."Customer No." := AccountNo_L;
        RecShipAddress_L.Code := UPPERCASE(AdrCode_L);
        RecShipAddress_L."Name 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom'), 1,MAXSTRLEN(RecShipAddress_L.Name));
        RecShipAddress_L.Address := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue1'), 1,MAXSTRLEN(RecShipAddress_L.Address));
        RecShipAddress_L."Address 2" := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue2'), 1,MAXSTRLEN(RecShipAddress_L."Address 2"))
      ;
        RecShipAddress_L.County := COPYSTR(GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue3'), 1,MAXSTRLEN(RecShipAddress_L.County));
        //RecShipAddress_L.City := COPYSTR(RemoveSeparator(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612,
                           //GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_villeid'),FALSE)),1,MAXSTRLEN(RecShipAddress_L.City));
        PostCodeCity_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053612,GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_villeid'),FALSE);
        RecShipAddress_L."Post Code" := COPYSTR(PostCodeCity_L, 1, STRPOS(PostCodeCity_L, GetSeparator()) - 1);
        RecShipAddress_L.City
         := COPYSTR(PostCodeCity_L, STRPOS(PostCodeCity_L, GetSeparator()) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(PostCodeCity_L));
        RecShipAddress_L."Country/Region Code" := COPYSTR(GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053611,GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_pays'),FALSE)
                                                  ,1,MAXSTRLEN(RecShipAddress_L."Country/Region Code"));
        IF RecShipAddress_L.INSERT(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          //mise . jour du CRM pour indiquer que le compte est synchronis'
          ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_statutdesynchronisation', '990260002'); //Synchronis'
          SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_code', RecShipAddress_L.Code);
          UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, CrmGuid_P);
          CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053636, AccountNo_L + GetSeparator + RecShipAddress_L.Code, CrmGuid_P);
        END;
      END
      ELSE Result_L := '';
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitCity_D365@1000000028();
    VAR
      RecPostCode_L@1000000000 : Record 225;
      recRecordRef_L@1000000001 : RecordRef;
      i_L@1000000006 : Integer;
      Count_L@1000000005 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@1000000004 : Integer;
      ValBufferLast_L@1000000003 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(RecPostCode_L);
      Count_L := RecPostCode_L.COUNT;
      i_L := 0;
      IF RecPostCode_L.FINDSET THEN
      REPEAT
        IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          i_L += 1;
          ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
          ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
        END;
        recRecordRef_L.GETTABLE(RecPostCode_L);
        SyncModify(recRecordRef_L, recRecordRef_L);
      UNTIL RecPostCode_L.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetOwnerIdGuid@1000000029(TxtCrmGuid_P@1000000001 : Text[50]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
      RecCRMSalesperson_L@1000000003 : Record 8053608;
      RecContactSalesperson_L@1000000007 : Record 8053160;
      RecContactSalesperson2_L@1000000006 : Record 8053160;
      ContactNo_L@1000000005 : Code[20];
      OwnerGuid_L@1000000004 : Text[50];
      OwnerCode_L@1000000002 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      // --> 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
      OwnerCode_L := '';
      IF InitializeCrmConnexion_D365 THEN BEGIN
        EntityName_L := 'account';
        ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
        IF GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_P) THEN BEGIN
          ContactNo_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber');
          OwnerGuid_L := GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'ownerid');
          OwnerCode_L := GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053631, OwnerGuid_L, FALSE);
          GuidFinded(OwnerCode_L, OwnerGuid_L, RecCRMSalesperson_L.FIELDCAPTION("User ID"));
          CLEAR(RecCRMSalesperson_L);
          RecCRMSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("User ID",OwnerCode_L);
          IF RecCRMSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            RecCRMSalesperson_L.TESTFIELD(Code);
            CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson_L);
            RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code",ContactNo_L);
            RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("Salesperson Code",RecCRMSalesperson_L.Code);
            IF RecContactSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson2_L);
              RecContactSalesperson2_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code",ContactNo_L);
              RecContactSalesperson2_L.MODIFYALL(Owner,FALSE);
              RecContactSalesperson_L.Owner := TRUE;
              RecContactSalesperson_L.MODIFY;
            END
            ELSE BEGIN
              CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson2_L);
              RecContactSalesperson2_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code",ContactNo_L);
              RecContactSalesperson2_L.MODIFYALL(Owner,FALSE);
              RecContactSalesperson_L.INIT;
              RecContactSalesperson_L."Contact Code" := ContactNo_L;
              RecContactSalesperson_L."Prod. Activity Code" := 'PV';
              RecContactSalesperson_L."Salesperson Code" := RecCRMSalesperson_L.Code;
              RecContactSalesperson_L.Owner := TRUE;
              RecContactSalesperson_L.INSERT;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE ReturnAvailableSeason@1000000030(Campagne_P@8053600 : Code[10];Rec@8053601 : Record 8053611) : Integer;
    VAR
      Season_L@8053001 : Record 8053154;
      i_L@1000000001 : Integer;
      LevelFound_L@1000000002 : Boolean;
      recSalesHeader_L@8053602 : Record 8053610;
      recItem_L@8053603 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053604 : Record 5401;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20170907-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-BSI01
      recSalesHeader_L.GET(Rec."CRM Header Guid");

      Season_L.RESET;
      Season_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Type,"No.","Variant Code","Order Starting Date");
      Season_L.SETFILTER("Order Starting Date", '<=%1', WORKDATE);
      Season_L.SETFILTER("Order Ending Date", '>=%1', WORKDATE);
      IF Campagne_P <> '' THEN
        Season_L.SETRANGE(Season_L."Campaign No.", Campagne_P);
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Unactive,FALSE);

      recItem_L.GET(Rec."No.");
      //Search by Item and Variant Code
      IF Rec."Variant Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recVariant_L.GET(Rec."No.", Rec."Variant Code");
        Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Item);
        Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", Rec."No.");
        Season_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code", Rec."Variant Code");
        IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");
      END;

      //Search by Item
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Item);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", Rec."No.");
      Season_L.SETRANGE("Variant Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");

      // --> 20230315-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3614-CJY01
      IF recItem_L."Code tarif" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::"Rate Code");
        Season_L.SETRANGE("No.",recItem_L."Code tarif");
        IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");
      END;
      // <-- 20230315-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3614-CJY01


      //Search by Species
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Species);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Species Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");

      //Search by Subfamily
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Subfamily);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Sub-Family Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");

      //Search by Family
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Family);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Family Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");

      //Search by Activity
      Season_L.SETRANGE(Type, Season_L.Type::Activity);
      Season_L.SETRANGE("No.", recItem_L."Activity Code");
      IF Season_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(Season_L."ID No.");


      EXIT(0);
      // <-- 20170907-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26281-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncEntitySelectCity@1000000033(EntityType_P@1000000000 : Code[30];Code_P@1000000011 : Code[20];City_P@1000000012 : Text[30]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      recordGUID_L@1000000001 : GUID;
      recordGUIDUpdate_L@1000000007 : GUID;
      recPostalCodeCity_L@1000000003 : Record 225;
      recCountry_L@1000000004 : Record 9;
      recPaymentTerm_L@1000000005 : Record 3;
      recPaymentMethod_L@1000000006 : Record 289;
      recUnitOfMeasure_L@8053607 : Record 204;
      LastData_L@8053600 : Code[20];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      ToProcess_L@8053602 : Boolean;
      DataRead_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      PostCodeId_L@8053605 : Code[20];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Guiduomschedule_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      recCampaign_L@8053609 : Record 8052991;
      recLocation_L@8053610 : Record 14;
      crmRecordCount_L@8053611 : Integer;
      i_L@8053612 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053613 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053614 : Integer;
      ValBufferLast_L@8053615 : Integer;
      PostalCodeIdFinded_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recRespons_L@8053617 : Record 8053161;
      PV_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      PAL_L@8053620 : Text[50];
      PAR_L@8053621 : Text[50];
      PAV_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      PAP_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      recUserSetup_L@8053624 : Record 91;
      UserId_L@8053625 : Text[1024];
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053627 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053626 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@8053618 : Record 8053167;
      Code_L@8053628 : Text[250];
      ToInactive_L@8053629 : Boolean;
      PosSlash_L@8053632 : Integer;
      LastCode_L@8053616 : Text[250];
      recFunnel_L@8053633 : Record 8053358;
      recFunnel_Line_L@8053631 : Record 8053359;
      Responsable_L@1000000002 : Text[30];
      recSalesPersonn_L@1000000008 : Record 8053608;
      TxtUserGuid_L@1000000009 : Text[50];
      recMappingSalesp_L@1000000010 : Record 8053608;
      recShipmentMethod_L@8053000 : Record 10;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronise une ville venant de Nav
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - EntityType_P : Type d'entit' (Code)
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      // --> 20190605-WI00-ADV01.01.00-42631-CJY

      Result_L := FALSE;

      CASE UPPERCASE(EntityType_P) OF
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        //Synchronisation des villes
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        'CITY': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_city';
          ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //1Sre partie : cr'ation modification depuis NAV
          recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
          recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(Code,Code_P);
          recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(City,City_P);
          Count_L := recPostalCodeCity_L.COUNT;
          i_L := 0;
          ValBufferLast_L := 0;
          GetSetup();
          IF recPostalCodeCity_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              i_L += 1;

              ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
              IF (ValBuffer_L - ValBufferLast_L) >= 50 THEN BEGIN
                ValBufferLast_L := ValBuffer_L;
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBufferLast_L);
              END;
            END;

            //Recherche des responsables : on r'cupSre les UserId
            //V'g'tal
            PV_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PV",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PV", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PV_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PV_L) THEN BEGIN
              PV_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PV");
              PV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PV_L);
            END;
            //Lapin
            PAL_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAL", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAL_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAL_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAL_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAL_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAL");
              PAL_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAL_L);
            END;
            //Ruminant
            PAR_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAR", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAR_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAR_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAR_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAR_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAR");
              PAR_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAR_L);
            END;
            //Volaille
            PAV_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAV", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAV_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAV_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAV_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAV");
              PAV_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAV_L);
            END;
            //Porc
            PAP_L := SearchActivityResponsible(recCRMSetup_G."Activity PA",
              recCRMSetup_G."Sales Network PAP", recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
            PAP_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAP_L);
            IF GuidIsEmpty(PAP_L) THEN BEGIN
              PAP_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recCRMSetup_G."Default Vendor PAP");
              PAP_L := GetGuid(Text8053631, PAP_L);
            END;

            ToProcess_L := FALSE;
            //recherche dans la table des GUID si la donn'e est d'j. synchronis'e
            TxtCrmGuid_L := GetGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
            PostalCodeIdFinded_L := GetGuid(Text8053613, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code);
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_inseecode', '');
                //v'rification de l'existance de cet enregistrement dans le CRM
                IF GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  //Si le lien est bien fait, on la met . jour
                  ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_inseecode', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
                  SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_postalcode_id', PostalCodeIdFinded_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plantresponsibleid', PV_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rabbitresponsibleid', PAL_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ruminantresponsibleid', PAR_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_poultryresponsibleid', PAV_L);
                  SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pigresponsibleid', PAP_L);

                  UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  IF GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1' THEN
                    SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                END;
              END ELSE
                IF NOT(SearchCity(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L, PostalCodeIdFinded_L)) THEN
                  ToProcess_L := TRUE;
            END ELSE
              IF NOT(SearchCity(EntityName_L, recPostalCodeCity_L, PostalCodeIdFinded_L)) THEN
                ToProcess_L := TRUE;

            //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
            IF ToProcess_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_inseecode', recPostalCodeCity_L."INSEE No.");
              SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recPostalCodeCity_L.City);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_postalcode_id', PostalCodeIdFinded_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plantresponsibleid', PV_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rabbitresponsibleid', PAL_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ruminantresponsibleid', PAR_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_poultryresponsibleid', PAV_L);
              SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pigresponsibleid', PAP_L);
              IF CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN
                UpdateGuid(Text8053612, recPostalCodeCity_L.Code + GetSeparator() + recPostalCodeCity_L.City, TxtCrmGuid_L)
              ELSE
                EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recPostalCodeCity_L.NEXT = 0;

          //2Sme partie : d'sactivation des valeurs pr'sentes uniquement dans le CRM
          LastCode_L := '';
          REPEAT
            ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
            SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
            IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '>', LastCode_L);
            END;
            SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name');
            Count_L := SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
            i_L := 0;
            IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, 0);
              END;
              REPEAT
                IF WindowOpened_G = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                  i_L += 1;
                  ValBuffer_L := ROUND(i_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                  ProgressDialog_G.UPDATE(2, ValBuffer_L);
                END;

                ToInactive_L := FALSE;
                Code_L := GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name');
                IF STRLEN(Code_L) > MAXSTRLEN(recPostalCodeCity_L.City) THEN
                  ToInactive_L := TRUE
                ELSE BEGIN
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.RESET;
                  recPostalCodeCity_L.SETRANGE(City, Code_L);
                  IF recPostalCodeCity_L.ISEMPTY THEN
                    ToInactive_L := TRUE;
                END;
                IF ToInactive_L = TRUE THEN
                  SetState(EntityName_L, GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'), 2); //Inactive - Inactif
                LastCode_L := Code_L;
              UNTIL NOT(NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
            END;
          UNTIL Count_L = 0;
        END;
      END;
      Result_L := TRUE;

      EXIT(Result_L);
      // <-- 20190605-WI00-ADV01.01.00-42631-CJY
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsCrmContact@1000000039(ContactNo_P@1000000001 : Code[20];CompanyNo_P@1000000002 : Code[10]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      GuidTable_L@1000000000 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20201130-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3501-CJY01
      GuidTable_L.RESET;
      GuidTable_L.CHANGECOMPANY(CompanyNo_P);
      GuidTable_L.SETRANGE("Type Data",GuidTable_L."Type Data"::Account);

      GuidTable_L.SETRANGE("Code Nav",ContactNo_P);
      GuidTable_L.SETFILTER(GUID,'<>%1','{00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000}');

      IF GuidTable_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(TRUE)
      ELSE
        EXIT(FALSE);

      // <-- 20201130-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3501-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE AssociateContactByRec@1000000032(VAR recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P@1000000000 : Record 8053600;recThirdPartyNo_P@1000000002 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      recThirdParty_L@8053600 : Record 8053185;
      pagThirdParty_L@8053601 : Page 8053349;
      cduCMCMcDataManag_L@8053602 : Codeunit 8053306;
      RecRef_L@8053603 : RecordRef;
      recMasterdataRelation_L@8053604 : Record 8053076;
      recContact_L@8053605 : Record 5050;
      recCompany_L@8053606 : Record 8053075;
      recContactToModify_L@1000000001 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Association du contact et synchronisation avec le CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - ThirdPartyToCreate_P : Tiers MS . associer
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Erreur si une action a d'j. 't' r'alis'e
      //il s'agit de la copie de la fonction AssociateContact, on remplace l'utilisation d'une selection dans une page par le passage
      //direct du num'ro de tiers ms

      // --> 20211025-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3579-CJY01
      IsMasterCompany();

      IF recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.Status <> recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.Status::" " THEN
        ERROR(Text8053607);

      //CLEAR(pagThirdParty_L);
      //recThirdParty_L.RESET;
      //pagThirdParty_L.SETTABLEVIEW(recThirdParty_L);
      //pagThirdParty_L.LOOKUPMODE := TRUE;
      IF recThirdParty_L.GET(recThirdPartyNo_P) THEN BEGIN
      //IF pagThirdParty_L.RUNMODAL = ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
        //pagThirdParty_L.GETRECORD(recThirdParty_L);

        //Recherche du contact associ' s'il existe
        IF recMasterdataRelation_L.GET(recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Company No.", recMasterdataRelation_L.Type::"Third party",
           recThirdParty_L."No.")
        THEN BEGIN

         //on 'tend les conditions : refus si contact code <> '' ET que le contact Code a . lien GUID
         IF ((recMasterdataRelation_L."Contact code" <> '') AND
          (IsCrmContact(recMasterdataRelation_L."Contact code",recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Company No."))) THEN // = diffusion faite
           //controle insufisant : ajouter aussi la pr'sence du lien dans la table GUID pour d'lencher l'erreur
            ERROR(Text60022,recMasterdataRelation_L."Contact code",recThirdPartyNo_P);
           //si pas d'association GUID, la cr'er (donc il faut bien ^tre parti d'un enreg "liste tier ms . cr'er" qui avait une GUID
           //penser . mettre . jour la fiche du contact qui devient donc un "contact CRM . synchroniser"

         recCompany_L.GET(recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Company No.");
         UpdateGuidWithCompany(Text8053626, recMasterdataRelation_L."Contact code",
           recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Dynamics CRM id", recCompany_L."Company Name");
         //mise . jour contact
         recContactToModify_L.CHANGECOMPANY(recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Company No.");
         IF recContactToModify_L.GET(recMasterdataRelation_L."Contact code") THEN BEGIN
          recContactToModify_L."Contact CRM" := TRUE;
          recContactToModify_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Dynamics CRM id";
          recContactToModify_L.MODIFY;
         END;
         AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recMasterdataRelation_L."Contact code", TextModify, recCompany_L."Company Name");

        END;

      //La cr'ation de la relation du Tiers MS se fait de la diffusion du Tiers MS. Elle n'est pas g'rer . ce moment dans le processus.
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."Third Party No." := recThirdParty_L."No.";
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P."No." := recThirdParty_L."No.";
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.Status := recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.Status::"Contact Associated";
        recThirdPartyToCreate_Var_P.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END ELSE
        ERROR(Text60021,recThirdPartyNo_P);

      // <-- 20211025-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3579-CJY01
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      // --> 3LI BEGIN

      20130617-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-FHU01 : NEWOBJ New CU to manage sync with CRM.
      20140312-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-1808-FHS01 : MODCODE (Correction suite . la pr'sentation)
